>    atomicity (overspend safety AND `/decide` idempotency) is enforced in
>    Postgres — `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` + a partial `UNIQUE` index — proven
>    ACROSS replicas by `TestReserveIfFitsRace` /
>    `TestReserveIsIdempotentAcrossReplicas`
>    (`internal/meter/store/postgres_race_test.go`), with no service-side
>    mutex in the way. The former **G6** guard, `meter.unsafeAllowMultipleReplicas`,
>    and the `maximum: 3` schema cap on `meter.replicaCount` are **DELETED**.
>    `meter.replicaCount` still **defaults** to `1` (a resource-footprint

> **verifies** they shipped; it does not build them.

**Goal:** Ship `chart/gonk` — **one umbrella Helm chart that deploys the whole
factory**: the Gas City controller, a Dolt SQL server, `gonk-intake`,
`gonk-meter`, and the gonk pack — mounts their externally-provisioned secrets as
files, renders the operator config they consume, wires Prometheus/Grafana, locks
the network down, switches the meter's ledger between the bundled Dolt, an
external Dolt, and CNPG Postgres, and makes **every bundled component
individually toggleable** (`<component>.enabled`) and every bring-your-own seam
(GitLab, LiteLLM, CNPG Postgres) explicit — with a test suite that runs
**without a cluster** wherever it can, and a single early **smoke deploy** for the
three controller facts that only a cluster can settle.

**Architecture:** gonk is a Gas City *pack plus glue* (spec §1), and this chart is
the standard umbrella / conditional-component pattern: `helm install` (in practice
a Flux `HelmRelease`) yields a working factory. It bundles **five components** —
Gas City controller, Dolt, intake, meter, and the pack installed into the
controller — each gated by `<component>.enabled`, wiring out to **three
pre-existing** things it never deploys: GitLab, LiteLLM, and the CNPG Postgres
operator. Disabling a component demands its external replacement (a render `fail`
if absent): `dolt.enabled: false` points Gas City (the beads store — RECONCILED,
Task 0: this toggle has **no effect on the ledger**, which is always
Postgres/CNPG; see ADR-004 Decision 10) at an external Dolt; `gascity.enabled:
false` (BYO controller) demands a supervisor URL. **Beads is Dolt-only** — CNPG
can only ever be the *ledger* backend, never the beads store. Fail-closed is the
design rule throughout: a misconfiguration that
could let work run unmetered, unbudgeted, or unverified must be **impossible**
(schema), **loud at render time** (`fail`), or **loud at startup** (the binary
refuses to boot) — never silently permissive.

**Tech Stack:** Helm 3 (>= 3.14; `helm v3.19.0` is what the owner has),
`values.schema.json` (JSON Schema draft 2020-12, enforced by Helm itself),
`kubeconform` v0.6.7, and **Go tests that shell out to `helm template`** (see
"Why not helm unittest", below). No cluster, anywhere in this plan.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-12-gonk-stack-design.md` — §4.1
(components), §5.1 (credential modes), §7.4 (**BYO seams — the heart of this
plan**), §8 (observability, three dashboards), §9 (security posture), §10.3
(component tests), §11 (validation milestones), §12 (risks).

**Depends on:** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-13-plan-02-gitlab-intake.md`,
`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-13-plan-03-gonk-meter.md`, ADR-002,
**`docs/environment.md`**.

## The deployment target is real, and it has conventions. Conform; do not invent.

**`docs/environment.md` was read off the live cluster and the live GitLab. It is
authoritative, and it answers four of this plan's eight owner decisions
outright.** The gitops repo (`/mnt/c/Users/steve/Code/gitops`) has its own
`CLAUDE.md`, a conventions spec, and a **pre-commit hook (`scripts/verify-conventions.sh`)
that will fail your commit** if you ignore it.

| Fact | Consequence for this chart |
|---|---|
| **Deployment is Flux**, from `gitops:clusters/orac/apps/gonk/` | The chart is consumed as a **`HelmRelease`**, never `helm install` by hand. |
| **Charts are NEVER vendored.** `charts/` at the gitops repo root is **gitignored**. | Gonk's chart is **pushed as OCI** to `registry.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project/charts` and referenced by an **`OCIRepository`** in `clusters/orac/sources/`. **This answers OD-8.** There is no `charts/gonk/` directory in the gitops repo and there must never be one. |
| **One Kubernetes object per file**, named `<kind>-<name>.yaml`, `metadata.name` matching; the directory's `kustomization.yaml` **must list every tracked yaml** or the commit fails | The chart already honours one-object-per-file. The Flux side (`namespace-gonk.yaml`, `ocirepository-gonk.yaml`, `helmrelease-gonk.yaml`, the ExternalSecrets, `kustomization.yaml`) is a **gitops-repo MR**, not a change in this repo — but Task 9's README must spell it out, file by file, or the operator will get it wrong. |
| **`clusters/orac/apps/renovate/` is gonk's near-twin** (GitLab bot PAT + webhook + CA trust); **`apps/nagus/`** is the model for a first-party image + shared CNPG | Copy them. Do not invent a fifth way to do the same three things. |
| **Images:** `registry.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project/<image>:<tag>`; per-namespace `externalsecret-gitlab-registry-pull-creds.yaml` → `imagePullSecrets: [{name: gitlab-registry-pull-creds}]`; **pin exact tags, never `latest`** (Renovate autodiscovers and bumps them) | **This answers OD-1.** `image.registry` defaults to `registry.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project`; `image.pullSecrets` defaults to `[{name: gitlab-registry-pull-creds}]`. G11 already rejects `latest` and empty — keep it. |
| **Ingress is Traefik** (the only IngressClass), host **`gonk.orac.local`**, ClusterIssuer **`orac-services-ca-issuer`**, and **the hostname must ALSO be added to the CoreDNS hosts block** (`clusters/orac/foundation/coredns/configmap-coredns.yaml`) or in-cluster resolution fails **silently** | **This answers OD-2.** And: **do NOT combine a `cert-manager.io/cluster-issuer` annotation with an explicit `Certificate` object** — that flip-flop once reissued 863 certs. Annotation only. |
| **GitLab runs IN-CLUSTER**, namespace `gitlab` | **This answers OD-6.** Use `networkPolicy.gitlabInCluster` (a `namespaceSelector`), **not** `networkPolicy.gitlab.cidrs`. A CIDR for an in-cluster Service is a pod IP, and pod IPs move. |
| **Private CA everywhere.** trust-manager publishes ConfigMap **`trust-bundle`** (key `tls-ca-bundle.pem`) into **every** namespace | `gitlab.caCert.existingConfigMap` defaults to **`trust-bundle`**, key `tls-ca-bundle.pem`, mounted at `/etc/ssl/orac/ca.crt`, with **`SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/orac/ca.crt`** on both containers. **There is no value that disables TLS verification, and there will not be one.** |
| **Secrets:** ClusterSecretStore **`vault-backend`**, Vault KV path **`eso/gonk/<concern>`** | The chart still renders **no `Secret`**. The ExternalSecrets are gitops-repo objects. **GOTCHA, FROM A REAL INCIDENT: write the Vault value BEFORE merging the ExternalSecret.** A missing key leaves the ExternalSecret NotReady and can **wedge the entire Flux reconcile**. Task 9's README leads with this. |
| **House style consumes secrets as env (`secretKeyRef`). Gonk uses FILE MOUNTS.** | **This is a deliberate, owner-approved deviation** (2026-07-13), because env leaks into `/proc/<pid>/environ`, crash dumps, and every child process. ESO **delivery** is identical; only **consumption** differs. **Note the deviation in `chart/gonk/README.md` and in ADR-006**, so the next person to run `verify-conventions.sh` knows it is on purpose. |
| **LiteLLM is BYO and already deployed**: ns `litellm`, `http://litellm.litellm.svc.cluster.local:4000` | **This confirms OD-3.** `litellm.externalURL` gets that value in `ci/values-default.yaml`. |
| **LiteLLM's model list AND its prices live in ONE gitops file:** `clusters/orac/apps/litellm/litellm.yaml`, `spec.values.proxy_config.model_list` + `spec.values.litellm_settings.model_cost_map`. **Local models are priced at ZERO there.** | The advisory price ConfigMap (Task 2) must say **exactly that path and that key**, and must say that the operator's job is a **gitops MR**. See the flagged ambiguity in Task 2. |
| **CNPG tenancy needs FOUR objects**, not one | Task 6. See below — this is the most commonly-got-wrong item in the whole gitops repo. |
| **NetworkPolicies are NOT ENFORCED on this cluster** | See "KNOWN LIMITATION" below. This is not a detail. |
| **Every GitLab runner is offline. CI has never run.** | **OD-7 is moot in practice:** the chart gate is **local**, forever, until a runner exists. Keep the CI jobs; keep them honest. First images are built and pushed **by hand**. |

## KNOWN LIMITATION: the chart's central security claim is NOT ENFORCED, and the chart must say so out loud

Spec §9 — *"agent pods have egress only to GitLab and LiteLLM, **so budgets cannot
be bypassed**"* — is **false on this cluster today, at the network layer.**

- **NetworkPolicies are not enforced.** Flannel does not implement them, and the
  **Cilium HelmRelease that would is suspended**
  (`gitops:clusters/orac/foundation/kustomization.yaml`). Every existing policy in
  the gitops repo carries a comment saying exactly this. There is no cluster-wide
  default-deny and no enforced egress anywhere.
- **The concrete bypass:** LiteLLM routes local models to **Ollama at
  `http://192.168.1.142:11434`, which requires no credential.** An agent pod can
  reach it directly, skip LiteLLM entirely, and burn local GPU with **zero
  metering and no ceiling**.

**Owner decision (2026-07-13): ship it, document the gap, do not gate on it.**
This chart implements that decision as follows, and **an executor must not
"tidy" any of it away**:

1. **Task 7 ships the correct egress NetworkPolicy anyway.** It is
   documentation-as-code: it becomes real the day Cilium is unsuspended, with no
   further work. Guard **G10** still refuses to render an empty
   `agentPodSelector`.
2. **Every NetworkPolicy template carries a comment** saying it is **not enforced
   today**, naming Flannel and Cilium — matching the convention the gitops repo
   already uses on its own policies.
3. **`chart/gonk/README.md` (Task 9) states it in the operator's face**, not in a
   footnote, in the honest form: **cloud-rung budgets are hard** (a cloud call
   needs a key the pod only holds via LiteLLM's virtual key, and LiteLLM refuses
   at the ceiling); **local-model budgets are advisory** until Cilium lands.
4. **ADR-006 records it** as a known, accepted, un-closed gap — not as a risk that
   was mitigated.
5. **Plan 06 writes the egress-denial test and SKIPS it**, with a skip message
   naming Cilium. **Un-skipping that test is the gate.** Note that a *wrong* pod
   selector renders fine and enforces nothing (OD-5), so **the test, not the
   template, is what will prove it.**

Closing the gap is infra work **outside this repo**: unsuspend Cilium, or put a
credential in front of Ollama so a direct call fails on auth.

## What this chart deploys, and what it deliberately does not

**The umbrella deploys five components; each is `<component>.enabled`.** It wires
to three pre-existing things it never deploys. This table is the authoritative
scope; the older "BYO Gas City / BYO Dolt" framing anywhere below is superseded.

| Thing | Who deploys it | Toggle | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Gas City controller** (workload + Service + namespaced RBAC + pack/city delivery) | **this chart** | `gascity.enabled` (default `true`) | The factory's brain. No upstream chart — this chart templates it (`docs/environment.md`). `gascity.enabled: false` ⇒ BYO controller, `gascity.supervisorURL` **required**. Three deploy facts are smoke-gated (Task 0.5). |
| **The gonk pack** (installed into the controller) | **this chart** | rides `gascity.enabled` | Pack + city config delivered at `/city`, prebaked into the controller image or mounted (Task 6.5). Plan 04 builds the pack; the `gc init`→`.gc-start` sentinel handshake is smoke-gated (Task 0.5). |
| **Dolt SQL server** (StatefulSet + headless Service + PVC) | **this chart** | `dolt.enabled` (default `true`) | **RECONCILED, Task 0: backs the Gas City beads store ONLY** — Task 0b's spike ran and Dolt failed the reservation-race isolation test (12.8× overspend, `docs/spikes/dolt-reservation-isolation.md`); the ledger is Postgres/CNPG unconditionally (ADR-004 Decision 10). `dolt.enabled: false` ⇒ external Dolt, `dolt.external.host` **required**. Auth is `root`/empty/`--no-tls` — no credential to configure. |
| `gonk-intake` Deployment + 2 Services + Ingress | **this chart** | `intake.enabled` (default `true`) | It is gonk's. |
| `gonk-meter` Deployment + Service + RBAC | **this chart** | `meter.enabled` (default `true`) | It is gonk's. |
| Operator-config ConfigMap (instance/group policy, rung catalog, synthetic prices) | **this chart** | rides `meter.enabled` | What `pkg/opercfg` eats [P03 §Task 2]. |
| The meter **ledger** backend (CNPG `Cluster` / shared CNPG tenancy / external Postgres) | **this chart**, behind a mode switch | `ledger.postgres.mode` | **RECONCILED, Task 0: SETTLED, not pending** — Task 0b's spike ran (Dolt failed decisively), the owner chose Postgres (ADR-004 Decision 10), and `GONK_METER_STORE_BACKEND` has exactly one valid value, `postgres`. The only remaining switch is *which* Postgres (`shared`/`cnpg`/`external`). **Beads is Dolt-only; CNPG is the ledger only.** |
| ServiceMonitors, PrometheusRule, 3 Grafana dashboards, NetworkPolicies | **this chart** | `monitoring.*`, `networkPolicy.enabled` | Spec §8, §9. |
| **GitLab** (18.10.1 CE, in-cluster) | **NOT this chart** (pre-existing) | — | Bring-your-own. Seam: `gitlab.url` + CA trust. |
| **LiteLLM (+ its Postgres/Valkey)** | **NOT this chart** (pre-existing) | — | Already deployed (ns `litellm`). Spec §7.4 / **OD-3**: external-only in v1, `litellm.externalURL` required. |
| **CNPG Postgres operator** | **NOT this chart** (pre-existing) | — | The chart may emit a `Cluster` CR for the ledger, but never installs the operator. |
| **The Secrets themselves** | **NOT this chart** (Vault/1Password → ExternalSecret → Secret) | — | House rule. The chart only *references* them. |
| **Agent session pods** | **NOT this chart** (the bundled Gas City controller's k8s session provider creates them at runtime) | — | The chart ships a NetworkPolicy that *selects* them (`app: gc-agent`) — see **OD-5**, the single most dangerous seam. |

## The secret provisioning contract (hard requirement)

**No secret value appears in `values.yaml`. No secret is committed. No secret is
an env var.** The path is: **Vault/1Password → ExternalSecret (or vault-injector)
→ k8s Secret → projected file mount → the binary reads the file** [P02 §Task 10,
P03 §Decision 12]. Every credential has **two rotation slots** so a rotation is
not an outage.

The chart consumes Secrets it did not create. This is the exact contract the
operator must satisfy — the chart mounts these paths and passes them as
`*_FILE` env vars. **A Secret or key that is missing makes the pod fail to
start** (projected volumes are `optional: false`), which is the loud failure we
want, not a silent one.

Every one of these is delivered by an **ExternalSecret** in the gitops repo, from
ClusterSecretStore **`vault-backend`**, Vault KV path **`eso/gonk/<concern>`**.
**Write the Vault value BEFORE merging the ExternalSecret** — a missing key leaves
it NotReady and can wedge the whole Flux reconcile (a real past incident,
`docs/environment.md`).

| Secret (default name) | Key | Vault path | Mounted at | Env var | Consumer |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `gonk-gitlab` | `token` | `eso/gonk/gitlab` | `/etc/gonk/secrets/gitlab/token` | `GONK_GITLAB_TOKEN_FILE` | intake |
| `gonk-gitlab` | `token-previous` *(rotation slot 2, opt-in)* | `eso/gonk/gitlab` | `/etc/gonk/secrets/gitlab/token-previous` | `GONK_GITLAB_TOKEN_PREVIOUS_FILE` | intake |
| `gonk-gitlab` | `admin-token` *(only when `gitlab.mode=split-credential`)* | `eso/gonk/gitlab` | `/etc/gonk/secrets/gitlab/admin-token` | `GONK_GITLAB_ADMIN_TOKEN_FILE` | intake |
| `gonk-webhook` | `token` | `eso/gonk/webhook` | `/etc/gonk/secrets/webhook/token` | `GONK_WEBHOOK_SECRET_FILE` | intake |
| `gonk-webhook` | `token-previous` *(rotation slot 2, opt-in)* | `eso/gonk/webhook` | `/etc/gonk/secrets/webhook/token-previous` | `GONK_WEBHOOK_SECRET_PREVIOUS_FILE` | intake |
| `gonk-meter-api` | `token` | `eso/gonk/meter-api` | `/etc/gonk/secrets/meter-api/token` | `GONK_METER_TOKEN_FILE` | **intake and meter** |
| `gonk-meter-api` | `token-previous` *(slot 2, opt-in)* | `eso/gonk/meter-api` | `/etc/gonk/secrets/meter-api/token-previous` | `GONK_METER_TOKEN_PREVIOUS_FILE` | **meter only** (it is the verifier; intake presents slot 1) |
| `gonk-litellm` | `admin-key` | `eso/gonk/litellm` | `/etc/gonk/secrets/litellm/admin-key` | `LITELLM_ADMIN_KEY_FILE` | meter |
| `gonk-litellm` | `admin-key-previous` *(slot 2, opt-in)* | `eso/gonk/litellm` | `/etc/gonk/secrets/litellm/admin-key-previous` | `LITELLM_ADMIN_KEY_PREVIOUS_FILE` | meter |
| `gonk-ledger` | `dsn` | `eso/gonk/ledger` *(or CNPG's generated Secret — Task 6)* | `/etc/gonk/secrets/ledger/dsn` | `GONK_METER_STORE_DSN_FILE` | meter |

Plus one **non-credential** mount, which is just as load-bearing:

| ConfigMap | Key | Mounted at | Env var | Consumer |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **`trust-bundle`** (published into every namespace by trust-manager) | `tls-ca-bundle.pem` | `/etc/ssl/orac/ca.crt` | **`SSL_CERT_FILE`** | **intake** (and meter, for symmetry) |

That is how a Go service trusts `https://gitlab.orac.local`'s **private CA**. Go's
`crypto/x509` reads `SSL_CERT_FILE` with no application code at all. **There is no
value in this chart that disables TLS verification, and there must never be one.**

Mount rules, applied identically everywhere (Task 3 and Task 5 both use the
`gonk.secretVolumes` / `gonk.secretMounts` helpers from Task 1):

- `projected` volumes, `defaultMode: 0400`, container mount `readOnly: true`.
- **Never** `envFrom`, **never** `valueFrom.secretKeyRef`. If you find yourself
  typing either, stop: that puts the material in `/proc/<pid>/environ` and every
  child process.
- Rotation slot 2 is **opt-in per credential** (`secrets.rotation.*`), because a
  projected volume with a `key` that does not exist in the Secret makes the pod
  fail to start — and slot 2 usually does not exist.
- The **ExternalSecret** carries `homelab.orac.local/rotation: enabled` plus the
  rotation annotations, **exactly as `clusters/orac/apps/renovate/`'s GitLab bot
  PAT does**. Gonk's deviation from house style is **consumption** (files, not
  env), not delivery.

> **Previously-reported incoherence — NOW RESOLVED UPSTREAM, in the owning plan.**
> This plan used to report that [P02 §Task 10]'s prose promised "two rotation
> slots for every credential" while its `Config` struct shipped **one** for the
> GitLab PAT, and concluded that rotating the PAT was therefore a brief outage.
> **Plan 02 has since fixed the struct** (P02 §AD-4b): `GONK_GITLAB_TOKEN_PREVIOUS_FILE`
> exists, and the semantics are direction-dependent —
>
> - a credential intake **verifies** (the webhook token) → **accept either slot**;
> - a credential intake **presents** (the GitLab PAT) → **present slot 1, and on a
>   `401`/`403` retry once with slot 2**, incrementing
>   `gonk_intake_gitlab_auth_fallback_total`.
>
> So the chart now mounts **both** slots for `gonk-gitlab`, and the README's
> rotation procedure is uniform across all three credentials — **no exception, no
> outage.** `GONK_METER_TOKEN_PREVIOUS_FILE` remains **meter-only**: intake
> presents slot 1, and meter is the verifier that accepts both.

## The component toggles and BYO seams, in one table (spec §7.4)

**Two kinds of knob.** A **component toggle** (`<component>.enabled`) turns a
bundled workload on or off; turning one off makes its external replacement
**required** (a `fail` if absent). A **BYO seam** points at something the chart
never bundles. Every one is a first-class key in `values.yaml`, and every one is
exercised by a golden-render profile in `chart/gonk/ci/` (Task 9) and by the
toggle matrix (Task 8.5).

| Knob | Value | Options | Default | When disabled/external |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Gas City controller** (bundled) | `gascity.enabled`, `gascity.supervisorURL`, `gascity.dispatch`, `gascity.supervisorPort`, `gascity.delivery`, `gascity.workload` | `enabled` on/off; `dispatch: http`\|`log` | `enabled: true` | `enabled: false` ⇒ `supervisorURL` **required** (BYO controller). Port/delivery/workload are **smoke-gated** (Task 0.5). |
| **Dolt** (bundled) | `dolt.enabled`, `dolt.external.host`, `dolt.external.port`, `dolt.persistence.*`, `dolt.image` | `enabled` on/off | `enabled: true` | `enabled: false` ⇒ `dolt.external.host` **required**. Serves **beads ONLY** (RECONCILED, Task 0: never the ledger — ADR-004 Decision 10). Beads is Dolt-only. |
| `gonk-intake` (bundled) | `intake.enabled`, `intake.*` | on/off | `enabled: true` | `enabled: false` ⇒ nothing dispatches; a valid dry-run only with `gascity.dispatch: log`. |
| `gonk-meter` (bundled) | `meter.enabled`, `meter.*` | on/off | `enabled: true` | `enabled: false` **while `intake.enabled`** ⇒ `fail` (G8): intake would dispatch unmetered work. |
| **Meter ledger backend** | `ledger.backend`, `ledger.postgres.mode` | `postgres` ONLY (RECONCILED, Task 0: `dolt` is not a valid value — ADR-004 Decision 10), mode `shared`\|`cnpg`\|`external` | `postgres`, mode `shared` | Always Postgres/CNPG. **CNPG is ledger-only, never beads** — `dolt.enabled` above is a completely separate toggle for beads. |
| **Your GitLab** (BYO) | `gitlab.url`, `gitlab.botUsername`, `gitlab.mode`, `gitlab.caCert.existingConfigMap` | `bot-does-everything` \| `split-credential` | `https://gitlab.orac.local`, `bot-does-everything` | always external. |
| **Your LiteLLM** (BYO) | `litellm.mode`, `litellm.externalURL` | `external` (v1) | `external`, URL **required** | always external (OD-3). |
| **Your Prometheus/Grafana** (BYO) | `monitoring.*` | all off by default; chart **never installs** them | `serviceMonitor.enabled: false` | always external. |
| **Your ingress** | `ingress.className`, `ingress.host`, `ingress.tls` | standard | `enabled: false` | — |
| **Your secrets store** | `secrets.*.existingSecret` | any name | `gonk-*` | always external. |
| **Your storage** | `dolt.persistence.storageClass`, `ledger.postgres.cnpg.storage.storageClass` | any | `""` (cluster default) | — |

## Fail-closed guards (the complete enumeration)

Three layers. **A. Schema** (`values.schema.json`, enforced by Helm before any
template runs — a violation is impossible, not just loud). **B. Render-time
`fail`** (for cross-field invariants JSON Schema cannot express). **C. Startup**
(the binary refuses to boot; the chart's only job is to be unable to prevent it).

| # | Unsafe state | Guard | Layer |
|---|---|---|---|
| G1 | **Empty instance ladder** → any project may name any rung, bounded only by budget (PLAN.md carry-forward, ADR-002) | `operatorConfig.instance.ladder` `minItems: 1` | **A** + C (`opercfg.Load` refuses to start) |
| G2 | A rung named in a ladder is **not in the catalog** → meter cannot price it | `fail` listing the unknown rungs | **B** |
| G3 | **Local rung with no synthetic price** → LiteLLM's USD door never closes on tokens (P03 Decision 9) | `fail` | **B** + C |
| G4 | **Cloud rung priced at $0** → free money past the cost gate | `fail` (schema: `est_cost_usd` `exclusiveMinimum: 0` when `kind: cloud`) | **A/B** + C |
| G5 | **Onboarding default rung not in the instance ladder** → *every newly-onboarded project resolves to `disabled`* (OD-B in P02/P03) | `fail` | **B** |
| ~~G6~~ | **VOID (Task 0 reconciliation).** Written on the theory that meter's in-process per-project lock was the only thing serializing concurrent budget decisions (P03 AD-10). **ADR-004 supersedes this**: Task 0b's spike measured Dolt failing the reservation race (12.8× overspend, `docs/spikes/dolt-reservation-isolation.md`), the owner moved the ledger to Postgres, and Postgres's `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` + a partial `UNIQUE` index serialize BOTH overspend and `/decide` idempotency **in the database, across replicas** — proven by `TestReserveIfFitsRace` / `TestReserveIsIdempotentAcrossReplicas` with no service mutex in the way. **AD-10 is lifted; meter is multi-replica-safe.** Do not build this guard, the `meter.unsafeAllowMultipleReplicas` value, or its schema field/tests. | — (deleted) |
| G7 | **No webhook secret** → unverified webhooks | There is **no value that disables verification.** `secrets.webhook.existingSecret` is `required` + `minLength: 1`; intake refuses to start on an empty secret file | **A** + C |
| G8 | **Meter unreachable / not deployed, intake dispatching anyway** | `fail` if `intake.enabled` and neither `meter.enabled` nor `intake.meterURL` is set. (The deeper guard is in code: intake dispatches **nothing** for a project it has not got a `200 / state: active` for [P02 §"Failure semantics"], and its `/readyz` is false while meter's `/healthz` is down.) | **B** + C |
| G9 | **Orders silently going nowhere** (`LogDispatcher`) | `fail` if `gascity.dispatch: http`, `gascity.enabled: false` (BYO controller), and `gascity.supervisorURL` is empty. When `gascity.enabled: true` the URL is **derived** from the in-chart controller Service, so it need not be set. `dispatch: log` must be typed **deliberately** | **B** |
| G10 | **NetworkPolicy that selects no pods** — an agent-pod egress policy with a wrong selector is a policy that *renders, validates, and blocks nothing* | `fail` if `networkPolicy.enabled` and `networkPolicy.agentPodSelector` is `{}`. **This one cannot be closed by the chart at all.** The chart catches an *empty* selector; it cannot catch a *wrong* one (OD-5). **And on this cluster the policy is not enforced regardless** (Flannel; Cilium suspended) — so **G10 is the weakest guard in this table, and it is the one under spec §9's biggest claim.** Plan 06 carries the egress-denial test, **written and skipped**; un-skipping it is the gate. | **B** + **Plan 06 (skipped)** |
| G11 | **`latest` / empty image tag** → an unreproducible deploy, and a silent rollback of the budget enforcer | schema `pattern` on `*.image.tag` rejecting empty and `latest` — **including `gascity.image.tag`** (now deployed by this chart) and `componentImages.agent.tag` (the pin the chart carries for Renovate/e2e even though Gas City's session provider, not the chart, pulls it) | **A** |
| G12 | **Ingress enabled with no host or class** | `fail` | **B** |
| G13 | **Ingress exposing the private listener** (metrics/health/**`/admin/reconcile`**) | The Ingress backend is hard-wired to the public Service and its single port; a test asserts port `9090` and the private Service name appear in **no** Ingress | **B** + test |
| G14 | **A secret in `values.yaml`** | A test greps the rendered output and `values.yaml` for `glpat-`, `sk-`, `-----BEGIN`, and asserts no `Secret` object is rendered by the chart **at all** | test |
| G15 | **`ledger.backend: memory`** (meter's test-only store — loses reservations on restart, fail-open on headroom) | Not in the enum (RECONCILED, Task 0: neither is `dolt` — the enum is `["postgres"]`, a single value; see ADR-004 Decision 10). It cannot be set. | **A** |
| **G16** | **`dolt.enabled: false` with no external Dolt** → Gas City's **beads store** (and ONLY the beads store — RECONCILED, Task 0: the ledger is never Dolt, so this guard no longer has a ledger clause) has nowhere to connect | `fail` if `dolt.enabled: false` and `dolt.external.host` is empty. **Beads is Dolt-only; there is no Postgres fallback for beads** (`docs/environment.md`), so the message names the external DoltLab as the only option. | **B** |
| **G17** | **`gascity.enabled: false` with no supervisor URL** → intake has nowhere to send orders and the pack lives nowhere | `fail` if `gascity.enabled: false` and `gascity.supervisorURL` is empty (BYO controller must be addressable). Composes with G9. | **B** |
| **G18** | **`meter.enabled: false` while `intake.enabled: true`** → intake dispatches unmetered work | already **G8**; restated here as a component-toggle invariant so the toggle matrix (Task 8.5) covers it. | **B** |
| **G19** | **RECONCILED (Task 0): no ledger DSN, for ANY of the three Postgres modes** → the ledger (always Postgres/CNPG now — ADR-004 Decision 10; there is no `ledger.backend: dolt` position) has no connection string. Unlike the old bundled-Dolt case, **none** of `shared`/`cnpg`/`external` derive a DSN automatically — even `mode: cnpg`'s generated CNPG Secret must be pointed at by hand. | `fail` if `secrets.ledger.existingSecret` is empty, full stop — not conditioned on mode. | **B** + C |
| **G20** | **`gascity.enabled: true` with no controller image tag** → the umbrella's brain is an unpullable pin | already covered by **G11** (`gascity.image.tag` rejects empty/`latest`); listed so the controller task (Task 6.5) traces to a guard. | **A** |

---

## Why not `helm unittest`

Spec §10.3 says "helm lint + helm unittest + kubeconform". We keep `helm lint`
and `kubeconform` and **replace `helm unittest` with Go tests that shell out to
`helm template`** (`internal/charttest`, build tag `chart`). Reasons, in order:

1. **The fail-closed guards are the most important thing in this chart, and they
   are assertions that `helm template` *fails* with a specific message.**
   `helm unittest`'s `failedTemplate` can do this, but a Go test does it with the
   full `errors.Is`/substring machinery and no plugin.
2. **No plugin to install.** The owner's runners were offline for all of Plan 01
   (PLAN.md). `helm plugin install` needs the network; `go test` does not.
3. It joins the **existing repo gate** (`go test ./... -race -count=1`).

The chart tests are behind `//go:build chart` and need `helm` and `kubeconform`
on `PATH`. Both are already installed on the owner's box (`helm v3.19.0`,
`kubeconform v0.6.7`). **The gate is: `go test -tags chart ./internal/charttest/... -count=1`.**

---

## Owner decisions needed

> **FOUR OF THESE EIGHT ARE NOW ANSWERED BY `docs/environment.md`** (OD-1, OD-2,
> OD-6, OD-8), and they are marked **[ANSWERED]** below with the fact and its
> source. **Re-verify against the live cluster before relying on any of them** —
> `environment.md` is a snapshot, not a contract. The remaining four (OD-3, OD-4,
> OD-5, OD-7) are still open, and **OD-5 is still the most dangerous unknown in
> this chart.**

**OD-1 — Image registry and tags. [ANSWERED]** `registry.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project/<image>:<tag>`
— the in-cluster GitLab container registry (owner decision 2026-07-13; Harbor is
not deployed and zot is a pull-through cache only). Pulls need a per-namespace
`gitlab-registry-pull-creds` Secret (itself an ExternalSecret), referenced as
`imagePullSecrets`. **Tags are still pinned exactly and `latest` is still
rejected** (G11) — Renovate autodiscovers and bumps them, and a floating tag would
let it silently roll back the budget enforcer. `image.tag` therefore keeps its
**no default**: a chart that renders and cannot pull is better than one that
pulls something nobody chose. **And note: no runner has ever executed on this
repo, so the first images are built and pushed BY HAND.**

**OD-2 — Ingress + TLS. [ANSWERED]** **Traefik** (the only IngressClass on the
cluster), host **`gonk.orac.local`**, ClusterIssuer
**`orac-services-ca-issuer`**, `external-dns.alpha.kubernetes.io/hostname`
annotation. Three things that are easy to get wrong and expensive to get wrong:

1. **Annotate; do not also ship a `Certificate`.** Combining a
   `cert-manager.io/cluster-issuer` annotation with an explicit `Certificate`
   object once **reissued 863 certs**. Annotation only.
2. **Add `gonk.orac.local` to the CoreDNS hosts block**
   (`gitops:clusters/orac/foundation/coredns/configmap-coredns.yaml`) **or
   in-cluster resolution fails silently.** This is a gitops-repo change, it is not
   optional, and the failure mode is a webhook that never arrives with no error
   anywhere.
3. `GONK_WEBHOOK_SSL_VERIFY` stays **true**. GitLab must *trust* the ingress cert,
   not skip verifying it. The cert is issued by the same private CA GitLab's own
   pod trusts. **Plan 06 (P2-5) proves this for real** — it is the one direction
   nothing in this repo can check.

`ingress.enabled` still defaults to **false** (the chart exposes nothing until you
say so), and `intake.webhookPublicURL` is **required regardless**, because intake
writes it into every hook it provisions.
*Blast radius if wrong:* GitLab cannot reach the webhook → reconciliation (every
10 min) still works, so gonk degrades to slow, not broken (spec §5.2).

**OD-3 — Does the chart bundle LiteLLM?** Spec §4.1 lists "LiteLLM proxy
Deployment (+ Postgres)" among the chart's components; spec §7.4 says
`litellm.enabled: false` + `externalUrl` is supported. These pull opposite ways.
*Assumed default: **external / BYO only** in v1* (`litellm.mode: external`,
`externalURL` required). Bundling LiteLLM means a subchart dependency,
`helm dependency build` in CI (network), and owning its Postgres — a large lift
for a component the owner already plans to run. The seam is left open
(`litellm.mode` is an enum with one member; adding `subchart` later is additive).
*Blast radius if wrong:* the operator deploys LiteLLM themselves. The chart still
validates the URL, mounts the admin key, and renders the **synthetic price list**
for them to paste into LiteLLM's model config (Task 2).

**OD-4 — Does the chart deploy Gas City, and how is the pack registered?
[ANSWERED — owner decision 2026-07-13: the chart DEPLOYS it.]** The earlier
"Gas City is external, take a `supervisorURL`" assumption is **reversed**.
`docs/environment.md` verified there is **no Gas City on the cluster and no
`gonk-city` repo** — so the umbrella chart bundles the controller (`gascity.enabled`,
default `true`) and installs the gonk pack into it. There is **no upstream Helm
chart**: `contrib/k8s/` ships raw namespace/RBAC/Dolt/mail manifests but the
controller itself is deployed upstream by an imperative script that `kubectl cp`s a
city dir into a bare `restartPolicy: Never` Pod. **This chart templates the
controller as a proper workload** (Task 6.5). The pack + `city.toml` are delivered
at `/city` declaratively — baked into the `gonk-controller` image (Plan 04
`prebaked=true`) or mounted as a ConfigMap — and the `gc init`→`.gc-start` sentinel
handshake must still fire; **that, the supervisor port, and Deployment-vs-Pod
tolerance are the three smoke-gated unknowns (Task 0.5).** `gascity.enabled: false`
keeps the BYO path: `gascity.supervisorURL` becomes **required** and the chart
deploys no controller (G17). The pin that imports the pack into the city's root
`pack.toml` is the city config's concern, delivered with the pack — not a separate
private repo in v1 (`docs/environment.md`: `gonk-city` is a *future option*).

**OD-5 — What labels does Gas City's k8s session provider put on agent pods?
[ANSWERED — but it changes nothing about the enforcement gap.]**
Spec §9's central security claim is enforced by a NetworkPolicy whose
`podSelector` must match those pods. **Plan 04 read the labels out of Gas City's
source:** the k8s session provider labels agent pods **hardcoded** in
`internal/runtime/k8s/pod.go` — `app: gc-agent`, `gc-session: <label>`,
`gc-agent: <agentLabel>`, annotation `gc-session-name`; the provider itself lists
by `app=gc-agent`. So **`networkPolicy.agentPodSelector` defaults to
`matchLabels: {app: gc-agent}`** — shipped as the default, not left empty.

> **And read this next sentence twice:** knowing the selector does **not** close
> the gap. **The cluster does not enforce NetworkPolicies at all** (Flannel; Cilium
> suspended — see "KNOWN LIMITATION" above), so a *right* selector on an
> *unenforced* policy still blocks nothing. Guard G10 still refuses an **empty**
> selector; it still cannot catch a **wrong** one. "OD-5 answered" is **not** the
> same sentence as "spec §9 satisfied" — do not let it be quoted as such. Plan 06's
> egress-denial test — not this chart's render test — is the gate, and it stays
> **written and skipped** until Cilium lands.

**OD-6 — GitLab egress. [ANSWERED]** **GitLab runs in-cluster, namespace
`gitlab`.** Use **`networkPolicy.gitlabInCluster`** (a `namespaceSelector`), not
`networkPolicy.gitlab.cidrs`. A CIDR pointing at an in-cluster Service is a pod
IP, and pod IPs move on every restart — the policy would work until the first
reschedule and then silently stop allowing GitLab. The `gitlab.cidrs` path stays
in the chart for operators whose GitLab is genuinely external, and it still has no
default.

**OD-7 — The CI tools image.** `chart-lint` can run on `alpine/helm:3.19.0`. The
Go chart tests need an image with **go + helm + kubeconform**. The owner names it
(or we build one in Plan 04's image pipeline).
**Moot in practice, and say so honestly: every GitLab runner is offline and this
repo's CI has never executed** (`docs/environment.md`). The chart gate is
**local** — `go test -tags chart ./internal/charttest/...` on the owner's box —
and it will stay local until a runner exists. Ship the CI jobs; do not pretend
they have run.

**OD-8 — Chart publishing. [ANSWERED]** **Charts are NEVER vendored into the
gitops repo** — `charts/` at its root is gitignored, and a vendored chart would be
rejected by `scripts/verify-conventions.sh`. Gonk's chart is **packaged and pushed
as OCI** to **`registry.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project/charts`**, and consumed by
Flux through an **`OCIRepository`** in `gitops:clusters/orac/sources/` plus a
`HelmRelease` in `gitops:clusters/orac/apps/gonk/`. Credentials are the same
`gitlab-registry-pull-creds` the images use. The `chart-publish` job stays
`when: manual` — **not because the registry is unknown, but because no runner
exists to run it**; the first push is by hand:

```bash
helm package chart/gonk
helm push gonk-0.1.0.tgz oci://registry.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project/charts
```

---

## Assumed defaults (revisit if wrong)

**AD-1 — Namespace: `gonk`, and the chart does not create it.** Helm/Flux owns
namespace creation. *Blast radius:* `helm install -n gonk --create-namespace`, or
a Flux `HelmRelease` with `targetNamespace`. Nothing in the chart hard-codes it.

**AD-2 — StorageClass: `""` (the cluster default).** Spec §3 says Longhorn, but
naming it hard-codes the homelab into an open-source chart. *Blast radius:* if the
cluster has no default StorageClass, the ledger PVC pends forever and meter never
becomes ready — loud, not silent.

**AD-3 — `dolt.enabled: true`, `ledger.backend: postgres`, `ledger.postgres.mode:
shared` (RECONCILED, Task 0 — SUPERSEDES the original AD-3 below, kept struck
through for the record).** Task 0b's spike **ran and Dolt failed**: 32
concurrent writers raced a ceiling with headroom for 2, at three escalating
isolation strategies including `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE`, and every single one of
90 independent iterations overspent 12.8× with zero errors
(`docs/spikes/dolt-reservation-isolation.md`). The owner settled the ledger on
**Postgres, on the existing CNPG cluster** (ADR-004 Decision 10) — **there is no
`ledger.backend: dolt` any more; `GONK_METER_STORE_BACKEND` only ever accepts
`postgres`** (`cmd/gonk-meter/main.go`'s `openStore` — there is no
`store.OpenDolt`, and the switch this AD originally described no longer exists).
Dolt is **not** dropped: the bundled server (`dolt.enabled`, default `true`)
still backs Gas City's **beads store only**. `dolt.enabled: false` points beads
at an external Dolt (`dolt.external.host`); it has **no effect on the ledger**,
which is always Postgres, in one of three modes (`shared` — the existing
`databases-app/postgres` CNPG cluster, **the real target-cluster default**;
`cnpg` — a chart-managed `Cluster` CR; `external` — a BYO DSN).
> ~~Original AD-3 (struck through, kept for history): "`dolt.enabled: true`,
> `ledger.backend: dolt`. This is Plan 03's Decision 10, pending Task 0b. Blast
> radius: if Task 0b fails, flip `ledger.backend: postgres`... for the ledger
> only; beads stays on the bundled Dolt regardless. That is why the switch
> exists." Task 0b did not fail quietly — it failed decisively, and the switch
> it names no longer has a "dolt" position.~~

**AD-4 — Instance budget defaults to `monthly_cost_usd: 0`.** The chart, out of
the box, **cannot spend a single dollar**: the only rung in the default catalog is
local, and the instance cost ceiling is zero (ceilings only tighten downward, so
no `.gonk.yml` can raise it). Enabling cloud rungs is a **deliberate three-part
edit**: add the rung to `operatorConfig.rungs`, add it to
`operatorConfig.instance.ladder`, and raise `instance.budget.monthly_cost_usd`.
*Blast radius:* someone is surprised that cloud rungs defer. Good.

**AD-5 — `intake.replicaCount: 2`, `meter.replicaCount: 1` (RECONCILED, Task 0:
this is now a resource-footprint DEFAULT, not a correctness requirement — see
the Task 0 reconciliation note at the top of this plan and ADR-004's "The
ledger is multi-replica-safe; AD-10 is lifted").** Intake is stateless and
idempotent (its cache is derived). Meter's `ReserveIfFits` atomicity now lives
in Postgres (`SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` + a partial `UNIQUE` index), proven across
replicas by `TestReserveIfFitsRace` / `TestReserveIsIdempotentAcrossReplicas`
(`internal/meter/store/postgres_race_test.go`) — **meter may run more than one
replica.** The chart still **defaults** `meter.replicaCount` to `1` (the
modest footprint of the initial deployment), but it must NOT fail-closed-guard
a higher value: **G6 and `meter.unsafeAllowMultipleReplicas` are DELETED, not
implemented** — the money-safety property does not depend on replica count, so
there is nothing left for that guard to protect against. Former G6 is void;
`internal/charttest`'s `TestPostgresDoesNotUnlockMeterReplicas` and the
`unsafeAllowMultipleReplicas` schema field/values key must NOT be built.

**AD-6 — Monitoring CRs are off by default** (`serviceMonitor.enabled: false`,
etc.) and are gated by **values, not `.Capabilities.APIVersions`**. Capability
gating makes `helm template` render differently from `helm install`, which would
make the golden tests a lie. Turn them on explicitly.

**AD-7 — Grafana dashboard discovery label: `grafana_dashboard: "1"`**
(kube-prometheus-stack's sidecar default), overridable.

---

## What `helm template` CANNOT prove → hand to Plan 06

A chart that renders is not a chart that works. **These are Plan 06's, and they
are not smuggled in here:**

1. **The Secret actually exists and actually mounts.** `helm template` is happy to
   reference `gonk-webhook`; a missing Secret is a `CreateContainerConfigError` at
   runtime. Same for a **wrong key name** — the whole contract table above is
   unverified until a pod starts.
2. **The NetworkPolicy actually blocks — AND ON THIS CLUSTER IT DOES NOT.**
   Rendering a policy proves nothing: the CNI must enforce it, and **the agent-pod
   selector must actually match the pods the bundled controller creates (OD-5)**.
   **Neither holds today.** Flannel does not implement NetworkPolicy, Cilium is
   suspended, and even once the umbrella deploys Gas City the CNI still will not
   enforce. Plan 06 therefore **writes the egress-denial test
   (exec into a session pod, `curl` a non-allow-listed host — including Ollama at
   `192.168.1.142:11434` — require a timeout) and SKIPS it**, with a skip message
   naming Cilium. **The test is not deleted and it must never pass vacuously.**
   Un-skipping it is the gate. Until then, spec §9's "budgets cannot be bypassed"
   is not an unverified claim — **it is a known-false one**, at the network layer,
   for local models.
3. **The probes actually pass.** Intake's `/readyz` requires the bot identity to
   resolve, the first reconcile to complete, and **meter's `/healthz` to be OK**
   [P02 §Task 10]; meter's `/readyz` is false until its **first spend sync**
   completes [P03]. Neither can be exercised without GitLab and LiteLLM.
4. **The readiness coupling's blast radius.** Meter down → intake NotReady →
   removed from the Service → the Ingress 502s → **GitLab may auto-disable the
   webhook** after repeated failures [P02 §Plan-06 item 6]. Fail-closed, but the
   recovery path needs a real test.
5. **ServiceMonitor actually scrapes** (label selector must match the Prometheus
   Operator's `serviceMonitorSelector`) and **the Grafana sidecar actually
   discovers** the dashboard ConfigMaps.
6. **The RBAC actually permits** meter's KeySink to write per-project Secrets
   (Task 4 tests against a fake clientset; a fake clientset does not enforce RBAC).
7. **CNPG's `Cluster` actually reconciles** / the Dolt PVC actually binds / **Dolt
   as deployed serializes the concurrent reservation race** (Plan 03 Task 0b spike).
8. **The Gas City controller actually runs as templated.** Task 0.5's smoke deploy
   settles the three unknowns (supervisor **port**; declarative pack/city delivery +
   the `gc init`→`.gc-start` **sentinel handshake**; **Deployment vs bare-Pod vs
   StatefulSet** tolerance). Beyond that, only a real run proves the controller's
   **namespaced RBAC is sufficient** to create/exec/log agent pods and that its
   probes (`/health`, `/v0/readiness`, `/v0/provider-readiness`) pass. A fake
   clientset does not enforce RBAC; `helm template` cannot exec a pod. **(Task 0.5
   settles the three deploy facts; Plan 06 proves the full run.)**
9. **The synthetic price agreement.** The chart renders the same prices into the
   operator config *and* into an advisory LiteLLM model-price ConfigMap — but
   nothing verifies the operator actually pasted them into LiteLLM. **A local rung
   priced at $0 in LiteLLM means the USD virtual-key door never closes on tokens**
   [P03 Decision 9]. Plan 06 must diff LiteLLM's `/model/info` against the catalog.
10. **`helm install` on kind and on the real cluster**, idle = platform services
   only, zero agent pods (spec §11.1).

## File structure

```
chart/gonk/
  Chart.yaml
  values.yaml                                  the BYO seams; NO SECRET MATERIAL
  values.schema.json                           layer-A guards (Helm enforces this itself)
  README.md                                    the secret provisioning contract, operator-facing
  templates/
    _helpers.tpl                               names, labels, secret volumes/mounts, env
    _guards.tpl                                ALL layer-B `fail`s, in one place
    NOTES.txt
    configmap-operator-config.yaml             what pkg/opercfg eats
    configmap-litellm-model-prices.yaml        advisory: the same synthetic prices, for LiteLLM
    # --- Gas City controller (gascity.enabled) --- Task 6.5 ---
    workload-gonk-controller.yaml              Deployment | Pod | StatefulSet per gascity.workload
                                               (SMOKE-GATED, Task 0.5). Runs `gc start --foreground /city`.
    service-gonk-controller.yaml               supervisor REST/SSE. Port is gascity.supervisorPort
                                               (SMOKE-GATED: 9443 vs 8372).
    serviceaccount-gc-controller.yaml          the controller's SA (spawns agent pods)
    role-gc-controller.yaml                    NAMESPACED: pods{crud}, pods/exec{create}, pods/log{get},
                                               configmaps{crud}. Never Cluster*.
    rolebinding-gc-controller.yaml
    serviceaccount-gc-agent.yaml               agent pods' SA: pods[get] only
    role-gc-agent.yaml
    rolebinding-gc-agent.yaml
    configmap-gonk-city.yaml                   city.toml + pack, when gascity.delivery=configmap
                                               (else prebaked into the controller image; SMOKE-GATED)
    deployment-gonk-intake.yaml
    service-gonk-intake-public.yaml            :8080 — THE WEBHOOK, AND NOTHING ELSE
    service-gonk-intake-internal.yaml          :9090 — health/metrics/admin. Never ingressed.
    ingress-gonk-intake.yaml
    deployment-gonk-meter.yaml
    service-gonk-meter.yaml
    serviceaccount-gonk-meter.yaml             the only SA in the chart: meter talks to the API server
    role-gonk-meter.yaml                       KeySink: per-project Secrets. NAMESPACED, never Cluster*
    rolebinding-gonk-meter.yaml
    # --- Dolt (dolt.enabled) --- backs beads ONLY (RECONCILED, Task 0: never the
    # ledger -- ADR-004 Decision 10) --- Task 6 ---
    statefulset-gonk-dolt.yaml                 the bundled Dolt SQL server (beads). dolt.enabled=false ->
                                               renders nothing; the controller uses dolt.external.host.
    service-gonk-dolt.yaml                     headless Service for the StatefulSet.
    cluster-gonk-ledger-postgres.yaml          CNPG Cluster CR -- only for ledger.postgres.mode=cnpg
                                               (ledger.backend is ALWAYS postgres now -- RECONCILED,
                                               Task 0). postgres.mode=shared (the DEFAULT) renders
                                               NOTHING: it consumes databases-app/postgres, four
                                               objects in the GITOPS repo (Task 6 Step 5b). CNPG is
                                               LEDGER-ONLY, and Dolt above is BEADS-ONLY -- two
                                               entirely separate stores, never the same server.
    networkpolicy-default-deny.yaml
    networkpolicy-gonk-intake.yaml
    networkpolicy-gonk-meter.yaml
    networkpolicy-gonk-controller.yaml         controller ingress (intake, traefik) + egress
                                               (GitLab, LiteLLM, Dolt, k8s API, DNS).
    networkpolicy-gonk-dolt.yaml               ingress from the controller ONLY (RECONCILED, Task 0:
                                               meter is NOT admitted -- its ledger is Postgres/CNPG,
                                               never Dolt, so it has no reason to reach this Service).
    networkpolicy-gonk-agent.yaml              the spec-§9 policy. OD-5 lives here.
    servicemonitor-gonk-intake.yaml
    servicemonitor-gonk-meter.yaml
    prometheusrule-gonk.yaml
    configmap-dashboard-factory.yaml
    configmap-dashboard-cost.yaml
    configmap-dashboard-health.yaml
  dashboards/
    factory.json  cost.json  health.json       loaded with .Files.Get
  ci/                                          golden-render profiles (RENDERED, never installed)
    values-default.yaml
    values-byo-everything.yaml
    values-cnpg.yaml
    values-full-monitoring.yaml
    values-all-bundled.yaml                    every component enabled (the whole factory)
    values-external-dolt.yaml                  dolt.enabled=false + external host (BEADS only --
                                               RECONCILED, Task 0: has no effect on the ledger)
    values-byo-gascity.yaml                    gascity.enabled=false + supervisorURL
    values-ledger-cnpg.yaml                    dolt on (beads, unaffected) + ledger.postgres.mode=cnpg
                                               (RECONCILED, Task 0: was "ledger.backend=postgres";
                                               backend is always postgres now, this profile just picks
                                               the cnpg Postgres mode instead of the default shared one)
  smoke/
    gc-controller-smoke.md                     Task 0.5's recorded findings: port, delivery, workload
chart/values-e2e.yaml                          *** INSTALLED by Plan 06's L3 (HB-5). ***
                                               NOT in ci/: ci/ is render-only. This is the
                                               deployment the e2e suite actually tests, and it
                                               comes from the chart so the harness cannot invent
                                               its own.
internal/charttest/
  charttest.go                                 helm template runner + YAML object index
  render_test.go                               golden manifests, one per ci/ profile
  guards_test.go                               every guard in the table above, asserted to FAIL
  secrets_test.go                              G14 + the secret-contract assertions + the KeySink RBAC
  controller_test.go                           the Gas City controller + pack delivery + its RBAC (Task 6.5)
  toggle_test.go                               the component-toggle matrix: all-bundled, external Dolt,
                                               BYO Gas City, ledger-on-CNPG each render or fail closed (Task 8.5)
  netpol_test.go                               the policies render -- AND that they declare
                                               themselves unenforced (Flannel/Cilium)
  testdata/golden/*.yaml
docs/adr/ADR-006-chart-seams-and-fail-closed-guards.md
```

**`internal/meter/keysink/k8s.go` is NOT in this list.** It is **Plan 03's**
(Task 7 Step 4b). This chart owns the `ServiceAccount`/`Role`/`RoleBinding` that
make it legal, and nothing else. See Task 4.

**Two RBAC sets, do not confuse them.** `gonk-meter`'s SA/Role/RoleBinding (Task 4)
let *meter* write per-project virtual-key Secrets. The `gc-controller` and
`gc-agent` SA/Role/RoleBinding (Task 6.5) let the *bundled Gas City controller*
spawn/exec/log agent pods and let agent pods read their own. The controller RBAC is
transcribed from `docs/environment.md` (which read it off `gastownhall/gascity`):
`pods` [get,list,watch,create,update,patch,delete], `pods/exec` [create],
`pods/log` [get], `configmaps` [get,list,watch,create,update,patch,delete] for
`gc-controller`; `pods` [get] for `gc-agent`. **All namespaced — never `Cluster*`.**

House rule honored: **one Kubernetes object per file**, named
`<kind>-<name>.yaml`.

| meter listener | one port, `:8080`; `/healthz`, `/readyz`, `/metrics` unauthenticated, everything else bearer | `internal/meter/service/http.go` | **MATCH** (not re-read line-by-line; no code changed the port/route contract). |

**Additional drift found beyond the table above (not itemized by the original
Task 0, found by reading the merged code end to end):**

1. **`componentImages.*.repository` / `intake.image.repository` / etc. carried
   an extra `gonk/` path segment** (`gonk/gonk-agent`, `gonk/gonk-intake`,
   `gonk/gonk-meter`, `gonk/gonk-controller`) that does not match what
   `images/Dockerfile.*` + the Makefile actually build and tag
   (`registry.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project/gonk-agent:<tag>`, no extra
   `gonk/` segment). **Fixed**: `repository` is now the bare image name for
   all four.
2. **The `gonk.image` helper had no per-image registry override**, so
   `dolt.image` (a third-party `docker.io/dolthub/dolt-sql-server` image)
   would have inherited the global `registry.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project`
   default and rendered an unpullable path. **Fixed**: the helper now checks
   `.image.registry` before falling back to the global default, and
   `dolt.image.registry: docker.io` is set explicitly.
3. **The meter egress `NetworkPolicy` (Task 7) had no rule for
   `ledger.postgres.mode: shared`or `external`** — only `mode: cnpg` (a
   chart-managed `Cluster`, matched by podSelector) had an egress target. Since
   `shared` is the real target-cluster default, the chart's own default
   profile would have shipped a policy with no path to meter's own ledger.
   **Fixed**: added `networkPolicy.ledgerPostgres.namespaceSelector`
   (default: `databases-app`) and a namespaceSelector egress rule for the
   non-`cnpg` modes.
4. **The Dolt ingress `NetworkPolicy` admitted `gonk-meter`** ("the ledger,
   when `ledger.backend: dolt`") — that state never shipped. **Fixed**: only
   the Gas City controller is admitted.

- [x] **Step 2: VERIFY the two contract additions Plan 03 now owns**

Both shipped, verified against the merged code (not re-implemented here, per
this step's own rule):

**(a) The meter store backend.** `cmd/gonk-meter/main.go`'s `openStore`:

```
GONK_METER_STORE_BACKEND   postgres   ONLY (REQUIRED; no default). NOT
                                       "dolt | postgres" as originally assumed
                                       -- see the reconciliation note at the
                                       top of this plan and ADR-004 Decision
                                       10. `memory` and `dolt` are both
                                       deliberately NOT selectable: `memory`
                                       loses reservations on restart
                                       (fail-open on headroom); `dolt` does
                                       not serialize the reservation race at
                                       all (measured -- 12.8x overspend, see
                                       docs/spikes/dolt-reservation-isolation.md).
GONK_METER_STORE_DSN_FILE  path to a file holding the Postgres DSN (REQUIRED)
```

Confirmed: `internal/meter/store/` ships `postgres.go` (+ `memory.go`,
test-only) — **no `store/dolt.go` exists.** `grep -rn "func OpenDolt" internal/meter/store/`
returns nothing; `grep -rn "func NewPostgres" internal/meter/store/` finds it
in `postgres.go`. The DSN is a Postgres connection string
(`github.com/jackc/pgx/v5`), carries a password, and is a **file**, from a
Secret. An unknown backend, an empty DSN file, or a store that will not
connect is a **fatal startup error**. The chart sets both from `ledger.backend`
(always `"postgres"`) and `secrets.ledger` (Tasks 3 and 6).

**(b) `keysink.K8s`.** Confirmed shipped: `internal/meter/keysink/k8s.go`
exists, `func NewK8s(cs kubernetes.Interface, namespace, prefix string) *K8s`
is real, tested against a fake clientset, needs no cluster. `cmd/gonk-meter/main.go`'s
`openKeysink` reads `GONK_KEYSINK_NAMESPACE` / `GONK_KEYSINK_PREFIX` (default
prefix `"gonk-key-"`) and falls back to an in-process memory sink with a LOUD
error log if the namespace is unset. **This chart still owns the RBAC** (the
`Role`, `RoleBinding`, `ServiceAccount`) — Task 4, unchanged by this
reconciliation.

**Neither half works alone.** Without Plan 03's `k8s.go` meter has no sink;
without this chart's `Role`, every `Put` is a `403`, every project sits in
`key-missing`, and `/decide` defers forever.

git commit -m "docs(chart): reconcile plan 05 against the shipped intake/meter contracts"

### Task 0.5: Smoke-deploy the Gas City controller — settle the THREE unknowns (BLOCKING)

> ### ⚠ THIS TASK GATES THE CONTROLLER WIRING. DO NOT ASSUME AWAY WHAT IT MEASURES.
>
> The Gas City controller has **no upstream Helm chart** and **no controller
> manifest** — upstream deploys it with an imperative script that `kubectl cp`s a
> city dir into a bare `restartPolicy: Never` Pod (`docs/environment.md`, read off
> `gastownhall/gascity`). This chart must template it as a proper workload, and
> **three facts nobody has measured decide how.** Until they are measured, Task 6.5
> (the controller templates) and every port/probe/NetworkPolicy that references the
> controller are **provisional**. This task settles them against a **real cluster**
> in a **throwaway namespace** and records the answers.
>
> **CROSS-PLAN DEPENDENCY, stated plainly:** this task needs the **`gonk-controller`
> image from Plan 04** (with the gonk pack + a `city.toml`). **Plan 04 (images) must
> therefore partially precede Plan 05 hardening.** If Plan 04 has not produced that
> image, this task blocks — do not fabricate a controller manifest against a guessed
> port and a guessed delivery mechanism and call the chart done.
>
> `kind` is NOT installed and the WSL box's CNI is broken (`docs/environment.md`),
> so target the **real orac cluster** in namespace `gonk-smoke-<runid>`, and delete
> it at the end. This is a spike, not a deployment: the artifacts are *findings*,
> not shipped templates.

**The three unknowns, and what "settled" means for each:**

| # | Unknown | Candidate answers | How to settle | Consumed by |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **U1** | Supervisor **port** | `9443` (config docs) vs `8372` (earlier research) | Deploy the controller with `bind=0.0.0.0`, `allow_mutations=true`; `curl` `/health` on each candidate from a debug pod; see which the process actually listens on (`ss -ltnp` in the pod). | `gascity.supervisorPort` default; Service, probes, NetworkPolicy (Task 6.5, Task 7) |
| **U2** | Declarative **pack/city delivery** + the `gc init`→`.gc-start` **sentinel handshake** | prebaked into the image (`prebaked=true`) vs mounted ConfigMap at `/city` | Run each; confirm `gc start --foreground /city` reads the pack, and that the sentinel handshake fires (grep the controller log for the `.gc-start` sentinel; confirm a session can be requested). | `gascity.delivery` default; `configmap-gonk-city.yaml` vs image bake (Task 6.5) |
| **U3** | **Workload type** the controller tolerates | `Deployment` (`restartPolicy: Always`) vs bare `Pod` (`Never`) vs `StatefulSet` | Run it as a single-replica Deployment; watch for duplicate-reconcile / Dolt-lock / crash-loop pathologies across a pod restart. If it misbehaves, fall back to Pod/StatefulSet. | `gascity.workload` default; `workload-gonk-controller.yaml` (Task 6.5) |

- [ ] **Step 1: Provision the throwaway namespace and a bundled Dolt**

The controller needs a beads store to start. Stand up a minimal Dolt in the smoke
namespace (the Task 6 StatefulSet is not written yet, so use the raw upstream
`contrib/k8s/` Dolt manifest, or a one-off `dolt sql-server` Pod), auth `root` /
empty / `--no-tls`, and note its `host:port`.

```bash
NS=gonk-smoke-$(date +%s)
kubectl create namespace "$NS"
# apply a minimal dolt sql-server (headless svc + pod) into $NS; record host:port
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Deploy the controller image from Plan 04 and probe the port (U1)**

```bash
# Pull creds first (registry.orac.local needs gitlab-registry-pull-creds).
# Run the controller with GC_* overrides: bind=0.0.0.0, allow_mutations=true,
# [session] provider=k8s, [beads]/[dolt] pointed at Step 1's Dolt.
# Then, from a debug pod in $NS:
kubectl -n "$NS" exec deploy/gc-controller-smoke -- ss -ltnp
for p in 9443 8372; do
  kubectl -n "$NS" exec deploy/gc-controller-smoke -- \
    sh -c "wget -qO- --timeout=3 http://127.0.0.1:$p/health && echo \"  <- $p ANSWERS\"" || true
done
```

Record the port that answers `/health`, `/v0/readiness`, `/v0/provider-readiness`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Settle pack/city delivery + the sentinel handshake (U2)**

Run the controller twice: once with the pack **prebaked** into the image
(`prebaked=true`), once with the pack+`city.toml` mounted from a **ConfigMap** at
`/city`. For each, confirm the controller reads `/city/city.toml` and that the
`gc init`→`.gc-start` sentinel handshake fires (grep the log; request a session and
confirm an agent pod is created). **If the ConfigMap path does not fire the
handshake, prebaked is the only declarative option and the chart must bake — record
that.**

- [ ] **Step 4: Settle Deployment tolerance (U3)**

Run the controller as a **single-replica Deployment**. Delete its pod; watch the
replacement. Look for: duplicate reconcile, a Dolt advisory-lock refusal, a
crash-loop, or a wedged beads state. If any appear, retry as a bare `Pod`
(`restartPolicy: Never`) and then as a `StatefulSet`, and record which is clean.
**No leader election exists** (it is a single reconcile loop) — so two controllers
must never run at once; whatever workload is chosen must guarantee exactly one.

- [ ] **Step 5: Record the findings and tear down**

Write `chart/gonk/smoke/gc-controller-smoke.md` with: the resolved port; the chosen
delivery mechanism and whether the sentinel fired for each; the chosen workload and
the pathology (if any) that ruled the others out; the exact `GC_*` overrides used;
and the controller's observed RBAC needs vs the `docs/environment.md` Role. **These
answers become the defaults in Task 1's `values.yaml` and the template choices in
Task 6.5.** Then:

```bash
kubectl delete namespace "$NS"
git add chart/gonk/smoke/gc-controller-smoke.md
git commit -m "spike(chart): smoke-deploy the Gas City controller — settle port, delivery, workload"
```

- [ ] **Step 6: If Plan 04's image is not ready — record the block, do not guess**

If Task 0.5 cannot run because Plan 04 has not shipped the `gonk-controller` image,
**stop and record it in `PLAN.md`**: Plan 05 hardening of the controller is blocked
on Plan 04. Tasks 1–5 (scaffold, guards, meter, RBAC, intake) and Task 6 (Dolt +
ledger) **do not depend on this** and may proceed; Task 6.5 (controller templates)
carries provisional defaults (`supervisorPort: 8372`, `delivery: prebaked`,
`workload: Deployment`) flagged as **UNVERIFIED** until this task runs. The golden
manifests for any controller-bearing profile are **not authoritative** until then.

---

### Task 1: Chart scaffold, `values.yaml`, `values.schema.json`, and the no-cluster test harness — DONE

The harness comes **first**, so that every later task is TDD'd against a real
`helm template` run.

> **Execution note (Task 1, done at execution):** two deviations found by
> actually running the harness against real `helm` (v3.19), both left in
> place because the alternative was a chart or test that lies:
>
> 1. **`Minimum()`'s `synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens=0.20` needed `--set-json`, not
>    `--set`.** Helm's plain `--set` only ever infers `int64`/`bool`/`string` —
>    never `float64` — so `--set operatorConfig.rungs[0].synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens=0.20`
>    hands `values.schema.json` the STRING `"0.20"`, and the `"type": "number"`
>    check on that field rejects it (verified empirically: `helm template
>    --set x=0.20` renders `x` as `xtype: string`, `--set-json x=0.20` renders
>    `xtype: float64`). Fixed in `internal/charttest/charttest.go`'s `Minimum()`
>    by using `--set-json` for that one flag only; every other flag is
>    unchanged. This is a CLI-invocation fix, not a schema or chart change.
> 2. **A real object template was needed for `TestChartLintsAndRendersWithDefaults`
>    to have anything to assert `len(objs) != 0` against.** Task 1's own file
>    list (Chart.yaml, values.yaml, values.schema.json, `_helpers.tpl`,
>    NOTES.txt) renders zero Kubernetes objects on its own — `_helpers.tpl` is
>    a partial and `helm template` does not emit `NOTES.txt` — and the
>    component workloads are Tasks 3/5/6.5. Added
>    `chart/gonk/templates/serviceaccount.yaml`, gated on the
>    `serviceAccount.create`/`serviceAccount.annotations` values that were
>    already specified verbatim in Step 5's `values.yaml` (and otherwise
>    unused by any template until later tasks). No new values or schema keys
>    were introduced.

**Files:** Create `chart/gonk/Chart.yaml`, `chart/gonk/values.yaml`,
`chart/gonk/values.schema.json`, `chart/gonk/templates/_helpers.tpl`,
`chart/gonk/templates/NOTES.txt`, `internal/charttest/charttest.go`,
`internal/charttest/render_test.go`.

- [x] **Step 1: Write the failing harness test** — `internal/charttest/render_test.go`

```go
//go:build chart

package charttest

import "testing"

// The chart must lint and render with the default values plus the minimum set of
// required values (image tags, URLs). If this fails, nothing else in this plan
// can be tested.
func TestChartLintsAndRendersWithDefaults(t *testing.T) {
	Lint(t, Minimum()...)
	out := Render(t, Minimum()...)
	objs := Objects(t, out)
	if len(objs) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("chart rendered zero objects")
	}
}

// Helm enforces values.schema.json itself, before any template runs. These are
// the layer-A guards: they are not "loud", they are IMPOSSIBLE.
func TestSchemaRejectsMissingImageTag(t *testing.T) {
	_, err := RenderErr(t, "--set", "intake.image.tag=")
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("an empty image tag was accepted")
	}
}

func TestSchemaRejectsLatestTag(t *testing.T) {
	_, err := RenderErr(t, append(Minimum(), "--set", "intake.image.tag=latest")...)
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("image tag 'latest' was accepted; a deploy must be reproducible")
	}
}

// G1: the load-bearing one (PLAN.md carry-forward, ADR-002).
func TestSchemaRejectsEmptyInstanceLadder(t *testing.T) {
	_, err := RenderErr(t, append(Minimum(), "--set", "operatorConfig.instance.ladder=null")...)
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("an empty instance ladder was accepted; every project could then name any rung")
	}
}

// G15: the memory store loses reservations on restart -- fail-open on headroom.
func TestSchemaRejectsMemoryLedger(t *testing.T) {
	_, err := RenderErr(t, append(Minimum(), "--set", "ledger.backend=memory")...)
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("ledger.backend=memory was accepted")
	}
}

// G15, RECONCILED (Task 0): "dolt" is ALSO not a valid ledger.backend any more
// -- it was the chart's own default before Task 0b's spike ran. There is no
// store.OpenDolt for the ledger (cmd/gonk-meter/main.go's openStore has
// exactly one case, "postgres"), so a chart that let this render would produce
// a meter that fails at startup instead of failing at `helm template`.
func TestSchemaRejectsDoltLedger(t *testing.T) {
	_, err := RenderErr(t, append(Minimum(), "--set", "ledger.backend=dolt")...)
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("ledger.backend=dolt was accepted; there is no dolt-backed ledger any more (ADR-004 Decision 10)")
	}
}

// The chart ships NO rung catalog and NO instance ladder, and it must FAIL to
// render without an operator-supplied one -- a site-local default naming a model
// the operator's LiteLLM has never heard of is worse than no default (Plan 04
// AD-1; there is no model called `qwen-local`). This is the fail-closed inverse
// of the old "defaults ship a ladder" expectation: pure defaults (no operator
// override) must NOT render.
func TestChartFailsToRenderWithoutOperatorRungCatalogAndLadder(t *testing.T) {
	// Everything Minimum() supplies EXCEPT the operator rung catalog / ladder /
	// defaultRung: ALL FOUR component image tags and the URLs, so the failure is
	// unambiguously the empty operator config (G1/G2/G5), not a missing tag or a
	// component guard.
	base := []string{
		"--set", "intake.image.tag=v0.1.0",
		"--set", "meter.image.tag=v0.1.0",
		"--set", "gascity.image.tag=v0.1.0",
		"--set", "dolt.image.tag=v1.43.0",
		"--set", "intake.webhookPublicURL=https://gonk.example.test/hook/gitlab",
		"--set", "litellm.externalURL=http://litellm.litellm.svc:4000",
	}
	if _, err := RenderErr(t, base...); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("chart rendered with an EMPTY rung catalog and instance ladder; it must fail closed (a phantom-model default 404s at first token)")
	}
	// And with a full operator config (Minimum), it renders and the ladder is present.
	cm := MustObject(t, Render(t, Minimum()...), "ConfigMap", "gonk-operator-config")
	oc := cm.Data(t)["operator-config.yaml"]
	if !contains(oc, "ladder:") || !contains(oc, "qwen-local") {
		t.Fatalf("operator-supplied ladder did not reach the rendered config:\n%s", oc)
	}
}

func contains(hay, needle string) bool { return len(hay) > 0 && len(needle) > 0 && (stringIndex(hay, needle) >= 0) }
```

(Use `strings.Contains` / `strings.Index` — the two helpers above are shorthand so
the test reads cleanly; import `strings` and delete them.)
