- [x] **Step 3: Implement the harness** — `internal/charttest/charttest.go`

```go
//go:build chart

// Package charttest renders chart/gonk with the real `helm` binary and lets Go
// tests assert on the result. It replaces `helm unittest` (spec 10.3): no plugin
// to install on an offline runner, and -- the reason that actually matters -- it
// can assert that `helm template` FAILS with a specific message, which is what
// every fail-closed guard in this chart is.
//
// Requires `helm` (>= 3.14) and, for kubeconform tests, `kubeconform` on PATH.
// Build tag `chart` keeps them out of the default `go test ./...` gate, because
// not every machine has helm. The standing gate is:
//
//	go test -tags chart ./internal/charttest/... -count=1
package charttest

import (
	"bytes"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"path/filepath"
	"runtime"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"gopkg.in/yaml.v3"
)

// ChartDir is chart/gonk, resolved from this file's own location so the tests do
// not care what directory `go test` was invoked from.
func ChartDir() string {
	_, self, _, _ := runtime.Caller(0)
	return filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(self), "..", "..", "chart", "gonk")
}

// Minimum is the smallest set of --set flags that satisfies values.schema.json.
// Every required value with no safe default is here; if this list grows, that is
// a NEW required value and it belongs in the chart README too.
func Minimum() []string {
	return []string{
		// Every bundled component's image tag: the DEFAULT render is the WHOLE
		// factory (umbrella model, owner decision 2026-07-13). The controller and
		// Dolt tags are provisional pins; the controller's port/delivery/workload
		// are smoke-gated (Task 0.5).
		"--set", "intake.image.tag=v0.1.0",
		"--set", "meter.image.tag=v0.1.0",
		"--set", "gascity.image.tag=v0.1.0",
		"--set", "dolt.image.tag=v1.43.0",
		"--set", "intake.webhookPublicURL=https://gonk.example.test/hook/gitlab",
		"--set", "litellm.externalURL=http://litellm.litellm.svc:4000",
		// gascity.supervisorURL is DERIVED when the controller is bundled (the
		// default). It is NOT set here, precisely so the default render exercises
		// the bundled path; the BYO path (gascity.enabled=false) supplies it.
		// The chart ships NO rung catalog and NO instance ladder (site-local; a
		// default naming a phantom model 404s at first token -- Plan 04 AD-1). So
		// a MINIMUM render must supply them, exactly as a real operator does via
		// HelmRelease values. `qwen-local` here is a TEST operator's choice, not a
		// chart default.
		"--set", "operatorConfig.instance.ladder={qwen-local}",
		"--set", "operatorConfig.rungs[0].name=qwen-local",
		"--set", "operatorConfig.rungs[0].kind=local",
		"--set", "operatorConfig.rungs[0].model=stub-local",
		"--set", "operatorConfig.rungs[0].est_cost_usd=0",
		"--set", "operatorConfig.rungs[0].est_tokens=200K",
		"--set", "operatorConfig.rungs[0].synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens=0.20",
		"--set", "onboarding.defaultRung=qwen-local",
	}
}

func helm(t *testing.T, args ...string) (string, error) {
	t.Helper()
	if _, err := exec.LookPath("helm"); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("helm is not on PATH; the chart gate needs it: %v", err)
	}
	cmd := exec.Command("helm", args...)
	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
	cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = &stdout, &stderr
	err := cmd.Run()
	if err != nil {
		return stdout.String() + stderr.String(), err
	}
	return stdout.String(), nil
}

// Render runs `helm template` and fails the test if it errors.
func Render(t *testing.T, extra ...string) string {
	t.Helper()
	out, err := RenderErr(t, extra...)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("helm template failed: %v\n%s", err, out)
	}
	return out
}

// RenderErr runs `helm template` and RETURNS the error. This is the guard tests'
// entry point: a guard is proven by a render that fails with the right message.
func RenderErr(t *testing.T, extra ...string) (string, error) {
	t.Helper()
	args := append([]string{"template", "gonk", ChartDir(), "--namespace", "gonk"}, extra...)
	return helm(t, args...)
}

// Lint runs `helm lint --strict`.
func Lint(t *testing.T, extra ...string) {
	t.Helper()
	args := append([]string{"lint", "--strict", ChartDir()}, extra...)
	if out, err := helm(t, args...); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("helm lint failed: %v\n%s", err, out)
	}
}

// Profile returns the --values flag for a ci/ profile.
func Profile(name string) []string {
	return []string{"--values", filepath.Join(ChartDir(), "ci", name+".yaml")}
}

// Object is one rendered Kubernetes object, parsed just enough to assert on.
type Object struct {
	APIVersion string         `yaml:"apiVersion"`
	Kind       string         `yaml:"kind"`
	Metadata   struct {
		Name        string            `yaml:"name"`
		Labels      map[string]string `yaml:"labels"`
		Annotations map[string]string `yaml:"annotations"`
	} `yaml:"metadata"`
	Raw map[string]any `yaml:"-"`
	Doc string         `yaml:"-"` // the original YAML text
}

func (o Object) Data(t *testing.T) map[string]string {
	t.Helper()
	d, _ := o.Raw["data"].(map[string]any)
	out := map[string]string{}
	for k, v := range d {
		s, _ := v.(string)
		out[k] = s
	}
	return out
}

// Objects splits a multi-document render into parsed objects, dropping empties.
func Objects(t *testing.T, out string) []Object {
	t.Helper()
	var objs []Object
	dec := yaml.NewDecoder(strings.NewReader(out))
	for {
		var raw map[string]any
		err := dec.Decode(&raw)
		if err != nil {
			break
		}
		if len(raw) == 0 {
			continue
		}
		b, _ := yaml.Marshal(raw)
		var o Object
		if err := yaml.Unmarshal(b, &o); err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("parse rendered object: %v\n%s", err, string(b))
		}
		o.Raw, o.Doc = raw, string(b)
		objs = append(objs, o)
	}
	return objs
}

// MustObject finds exactly one object by kind+name, or fails with the list of
// what WAS rendered (which is what you actually want when it is missing).
func MustObject(t *testing.T, out, kind, name string) Object {
	t.Helper()
	var have []string
	for _, o := range Objects(t, out) {
		if o.Kind == kind && o.Metadata.Name == name {
			return o
		}
		have = append(have, o.Kind+"/"+o.Metadata.Name)
	}
	t.Fatalf("no %s/%s in the render; got:\n  %s", kind, name, strings.Join(have, "\n  "))
	return Object{}
}

// NoObject asserts a kind+name is absent.
func NoObject(t *testing.T, out, kind, name string) {
	t.Helper()
	for _, o := range Objects(t, out) {
		if o.Kind == kind && o.Metadata.Name == name {
			t.Fatalf("%s/%s was rendered and must not be", kind, name)
		}
	}
}

// Kubeconform validates a render against the Kubernetes API schemas. CRDs
// (ServiceMonitor, PrometheusRule, CNPG Cluster) have no upstream schema, so
// they are validated against vendored schemas in chart/gonk/tests/crd-schemas/
// -- NOT with -ignore-missing-schemas, which would silently skip them.
func Kubeconform(t *testing.T, render string, crdSchemaDir string) {
	t.Helper()
	if _, err := exec.LookPath("kubeconform"); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("kubeconform is not on PATH; the chart gate needs it: %v", err)
	}
	f := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "render.yaml")
	if err := os.WriteFile(f, []byte(render), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	args := []string{"-strict", "-summary"}
	if crdSchemaDir != "" {
		args = append(args, "-schema-location", "default",
			"-schema-location", filepath.Join(crdSchemaDir, "{{.ResourceKind}}_{{.ResourceAPIVersion}}.json"))
	}
	cmd := exec.Command("kubeconform", append(args, f)...)
	var b bytes.Buffer
	cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = &b, &b
	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("kubeconform failed: %v\n%s", err, b.String())
	}
}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Write `chart/gonk/Chart.yaml`**

```yaml
apiVersion: v2
name: gonk
description: A local software factory for self-hosted GitLab — a Gas City pack plus glue.
type: application
# The CHART version. Bump on any chart change. It is not the app version.
version: 0.1.0
# The default app version. Image tags are set per-image and have no default
# (OD-1): a chart that renders and cannot pull is worse than one that refuses.
appVersion: "0.1.0"
kubeVersion: ">=1.28.0-0"
home: https://gitlab.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project
sources:
  - https://gitlab.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project
keywords: [gitlab, gascity, agents, llm, budgets]
maintainers:
  - name: Steve
    email: leftathome@gmail.com
annotations:
  # This chart deploys NO secrets and bundles NO third-party services. LiteLLM
  # and Gas City are BYO (OD-3, OD-4).
  gonk.orac.local/byo: "gitlab,litellm,gascity,postgres,prometheus,grafana"
```

- [x] **Step 5: Write `chart/gonk/values.yaml`** — **the BYO seams, and not one byte of secret material**

```yaml
# =============================================================================
# gonk chart values
#
# *** NO SECRET VALUE EVER APPEARS IN THIS FILE. ***
# Secrets are provisioned OUTSIDE the chart (Vault/1Password -> ExternalSecret ->
# k8s Secret) and referenced BY NAME below. They are mounted as FILES, never as
# environment variables. See chart/gonk/README.md for the exact contract.
#
# The defaults are SAFE, not USEFUL: out of the box this chart cannot spend a
# dollar (instance monthly_cost_usd: 0, and the only rung in the catalog is
# local), exposes nothing (ingress.enabled: false), and will refuse to render
# without the image tags and URLs it cannot guess.
# =============================================================================

nameOverride: ""
fullnameOverride: ""

# Common image settings.
#
# OD-1 [ANSWERED, docs/environment.md]: images live in the IN-CLUSTER GitLab
# container registry at registry.orac.local, under the project's own path. Harbor
# is not deployed; zot is a pull-through cache only. Pulls need the per-namespace
# `gitlab-registry-pull-creds` Secret (itself an ExternalSecret -- the chart does
# not create it, and Task 9's README says how).
#
# TAGS STILL HAVE NO DEFAULT, and `latest` is still rejected by
# values.schema.json (G11). Renovate autodiscovers these and bumps them; a
# floating tag would let it silently roll the BUDGET ENFORCER backwards.
# And note: no GitLab runner has ever executed on this repo, so the FIRST images
# are built and pushed by hand.
image:
  registry: registry.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project
  pullPolicy: IfNotPresent
  pullSecrets:
    - name: gitlab-registry-pull-creds

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# gonk-agent (Plan 04). THE CHART DOES NOT DEPLOY THIS -- the bundled Gas City
# controller's k8s session provider pulls the agent image at runtime. But the pin
# lives here so (a) Renovate autodiscovers and bumps it, and (b) Plan 06's
# values-e2e.yaml (HB-5) can override it per run. Exact tags only; G11 rejects
# `latest` and empty here too. (The controller image, by contrast, IS deployed by
# this chart and lives under `gascity.image` below. The testclock meter image is a
# separate tag -- meter.image.tag + "-testclock" -- selected only by values-e2e.yaml.)
#
# RECONCILED, Task 0: `repository` below is the bare image name, no "gonk/"
# prefix -- images/versions.env + the Makefile (Plan 04, merged) build and tag
# these as `$(REGISTRY)/gonk-agent:$(GONK_TAG)` etc., i.e.
# registry.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project/gonk-agent:<tag>, NOT
# .../gonk-project/gonk/gonk-agent:<tag>. Verified against images/Dockerfile.*
# and the Makefile's four image targets (agent-image, controller-image,
# intake-image, meter-image).
componentImages:
  agent:
    repository: gonk-agent
    tag: ""               # REQUIRED (built by hand first; no runner has ever run)

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# gonk-intake — the GitLab trust boundary (Plan 02)
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
intake:
  enabled: true
  image:
    repository: gonk-intake
    tag: ""               # REQUIRED
  replicaCount: 2         # AD-5: stateless, idempotent, derived cache only
  # TWO listeners. The public one serves POST /hook/gitlab AND NOTHING ELSE.
  # The private one (health, metrics, POST /admin/reconcile) MUST NOT be ingressed.
  ports:
    public: 8080
    private: 9090
  # The URL GitLab will POST to. Intake writes this into every hook it provisions,
  # so it is REQUIRED even when ingress.enabled is false (you may front it with a
  # Gateway or an IngressRoute outside this chart). OD-2.
  webhookPublicURL: ""    # REQUIRED, e.g. https://gonk.orac.local/hook/gitlab
  # Bump this when you rotate the webhook secret: it is the generation marker in
  # the hook URL, and reconcile repairs every hook that does not carry it.
  webhookTokenGen: "1"
  # GitLab verifies the ingress TLS certificate when true. If your ingress uses a
  # private CA that GitLab does not trust, GitLab will refuse to deliver hooks --
  # fix the trust, do not turn this off. (Plan 06 verifies it for real.)
  webhookSSLVerify: true
  reconcileInterval: 10m
  resources:
    requests: {cpu: 50m, memory: 64Mi}
    limits: {memory: 256Mi}
  podAnnotations: {}
  nodeSelector: {}
  tolerations: []
  affinity: {}

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# gonk-meter — the budget brain (Plan 03)
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
meter:
  enabled: true
  image:
    repository: gonk-meter
    tag: ""               # REQUIRED
  # RECONCILED, Task 0 (supersedes the old "DO NOT RAISE THIS" comment below):
  # meter's `ReserveIfFits` atomicity now lives in Postgres (SELECT ... FOR
  # UPDATE + a partial UNIQUE index), proven ACROSS replicas by
  # TestReserveIfFitsRace / TestReserveIsIdempotentAcrossReplicas
  # (internal/meter/store/postgres_race_test.go) -- not in an in-process lock.
  # AD-10 (single-replica) is LIFTED (ADR-004). replicaCount: 1 remains the
  # DEFAULT for the modest footprint of the initial deployment, but it is a
  # resource choice now, not a correctness guard -- the chart does NOT refuse
  # to render a higher value, and there is no unsafeAllowMultipleReplicas key.
  replicaCount: 1
  port: 8080              # one listener; /healthz /readyz /metrics unauthenticated
  resources:
    requests: {cpu: 50m, memory: 64Mi}
    limits: {memory: 256Mi}
  podAnnotations: {}
  nodeSelector: {}
  tolerations: []
  affinity: {}

# =============================================================================
# BYO SEAM: your GitLab
# =============================================================================
gitlab:
  # GitLab 18.10.1 COMMUNITY EDITION, in-cluster, namespace `gitlab`
  # (docs/environment.md). CE matters: multiple MR assignees is an EE feature, so
  # the onboarding MR picks exactly ONE assignee. Nothing here may need EE.
  url: https://gitlab.orac.local
  botUsername: gonk
  # bot-does-everything : one Maintainer bot token (homelab default, spec 5.1)
  # split-credential    : a low-privilege bot token + a separate admin token used
  #                       ONLY for webhook management
  mode: bot-does-everything
  # THE PRIVATE CA. gitlab.orac.local serves a cert from the cluster's own CA.
  #
  # trust-manager publishes the ConfigMap `trust-bundle` (key tls-ca-bundle.pem)
  # into EVERY namespace, so this is a name, not a thing you have to create. The
  # chart mounts it at /etc/ssl/orac/ca.crt and sets SSL_CERT_FILE to that path;
  # Go's crypto/x509 reads SSL_CERT_FILE with no application code at all.
  #
  # *** THERE IS NO VALUE THAT DISABLES TLS VERIFICATION, AND THERE NEVER WILL BE. ***
  # (The gonk git remote uses http.sslVerify=false as a local convenience. A
  # SERVICE must not copy that.)
  caCert:
    existingConfigMap: trust-bundle
    key: tls-ca-bundle.pem
    mountPath: /etc/ssl/orac/ca.crt

# =============================================================================
# BYO SEAM: your LiteLLM  (OD-3: v1 is external-only; the chart does not bundle it)
# =============================================================================
litellm:
  mode: external          # enum: [external]
  # REQUIRED. On the target cluster LiteLLM is ALREADY DEPLOYED (ns `litellm`,
  # Service litellm:4000) -- it is BYO, and this chart does not bundle it.
  externalURL: ""         # e.g. http://litellm.litellm.svc.cluster.local:4000
  # Renders an ADVISORY ConfigMap containing the SAME synthetic prices as the rung
  # catalog below, for you to copy into LiteLLM's config. They MUST agree: a local
  # rung priced at $0 in LiteLLM means the USD virtual-key ceiling never closes on
  # tokens, and monthly_tokens has NO hard enforcement anywhere.
  #
  # WHERE YOU ACTUALLY PASTE THEM -- it is one named file, in the GITOPS repo:
  #   clusters/orac/apps/litellm/litellm.yaml
  #     spec.values.proxy_config.model_list          <- the models
  #     spec.values.litellm_settings.model_cost_map  <- THE PRICES
  # LOCAL MODELS ARE PRICED AT ZERO THERE TODAY. That is the hole this exists to
  # close, and closing it is a gitops MR, not a gonk change.
  #
  # Nothing in this chart can verify the agreement. Plan 06 does (P3-4).
  emitModelPricesConfigMap: true

# =============================================================================
# COMPONENT: the Gas City controller  (OD-4 [ANSWERED]: the chart DEPLOYS it)
#
# There is NO upstream Helm chart; this chart templates the controller as a proper
# workload (Task 6.5). It runs `gc start --foreground /city`, hosts the supervisor
# REST/SSE listener, and spawns agent pods via its k8s session provider.
#
# THREE FIELDS BELOW ARE SMOKE-GATED (Task 0.5) and their defaults are PROVISIONAL:
# supervisorPort, delivery, workload. Do not treat them as settled until the smoke
# deploy has recorded chart/gonk/smoke/gc-controller-smoke.md.
# =============================================================================
gascity:
  enabled: true           # deploy the bundled controller. false => BYO controller,
                          # supervisorURL becomes REQUIRED (G17), no controller rendered.
  image:
    repository: gonk-controller
    tag: ""               # REQUIRED when enabled (G11/G20). Built by Plan 04, by hand first.
  # http : intake POSTs orders to the supervisor
  # log  : intake LOGS orders instead of firing them. A DRY RUN. Nothing happens.
  dispatch: http
  # When gascity.enabled=true the supervisor URL is DERIVED from the in-chart
  # controller Service, so leave this empty. It is REQUIRED only when enabled=false.
  supervisorURL: ""
  # SMOKE-GATED (Task 0.5): config docs say 9443, earlier research said 8372. The
  # Service, probes and NetworkPolicy all read this. Provisional default:
  supervisorPort: 8372
  # SMOKE-GATED (Task 0.5): how the pack + city.toml reach /city, and whether the
  # gc init -> .gc-start sentinel handshake fires.
  #   prebaked : baked into the gonk-controller image (Plan 04 prebaked=true)
  #   configmap: mounted from configmap-gonk-city.yaml at /city
  delivery: prebaked
  # SMOKE-GATED (Task 0.5): whether the single-replica reconcile loop tolerates a
  # Deployment, or needs a bare Pod (restartPolicy: Never) / StatefulSet. NO leader
  # election exists, so EXACTLY ONE controller must ever run.
  workload: deployment    # deployment | pod | statefulset
  supervisor:
    bind: 0.0.0.0         # REQUIRED for in-container use (defaults to 127.0.0.1)
    allowMutations: true  # REQUIRED: the reconcile loop mutates city state
  city:
    # Only consumed when delivery=configmap. When prebaked, the image already
    # carries /city/city.toml and the pack. The [session]/[beads]/[dolt] stanzas
    # are rendered from the component values above so beads points at the Dolt below.
    tomlOverride: ""
  resources:
    requests: {cpu: 100m, memory: 256Mi}
    limits: {memory: 1Gi}
  podAnnotations: {}
  nodeSelector: {}
  tolerations: []
  affinity: {}

# =============================================================================
# COMPONENT: Dolt  (the bundled SQL server -- backs BOTH beads AND, optionally, the ledger)
#
# BEADS IS DOLT-ONLY. There is no "beads on Postgres" option and there never will
# be (`docs/environment.md`: the beads backend enum is dolt/doltlite; sqlite/
# coordstore were removed and hard-error). CNPG can only ever replace the LEDGER.
#
# The shipped Dolt auths as root / empty password / --no-tls, host/port only --
# there is no user/password/DSN to configure, so the chart does not pretend to.
# =============================================================================
dolt:
  enabled: true           # deploy the bundled Dolt StatefulSet. false => external
                          # Dolt (e.g. self-hosted DoltLab); external.host REQUIRED (G16).
  image:
    # RECONCILED, Task 0: registry is set EXPLICITLY here (docker.io), overriding
    # the global image.registry default. Dolt is a third-party upstream image --
    # it is never built into registry.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project, and the
    # "gonk.image" helper (Task 1) now honors this per-image override precisely
    # so this line does not resolve to an unpullable path.
    registry: docker.io
    repository: dolthub/dolt-sql-server
    tag: ""               # REQUIRED when enabled (no floating tag on tier-0 state)
  database: gonk           # beads live as a database on this server. RECONCILED,
                          # Task 0: the LEDGER is never a Dolt database (see
                          # ADR-004 / docs/spikes/dolt-reservation-isolation.md) --
                          # this server backs beads ONLY, full stop.
  port: 3306
  external:
    host: ""              # REQUIRED when enabled=false. Gas City AND the ledger use it.
    port: 3306
  persistence:
    size: 20Gi
    storageClass: ""      # AD-2: "" = the cluster default
    accessMode: ReadWriteOnce
  resources:
    requests: {cpu: 100m, memory: 256Mi}
    limits: {memory: 2Gi}

# =============================================================================
# The METER LEDGER backend  (which durable store meter's reservations live in)
#
# RECONCILED, Task 0: SETTLED, not pending. Task 0b's spike ran
# (docs/spikes/dolt-reservation-isolation.md): 32 concurrent writers raced a
# ceiling with headroom for 2, and EVERY one of 90 iterations overspent 12.8x,
# at every isolation level tried including `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE`. The owner
# moved the ledger to Postgres on the existing CNPG cluster (ADR-004 Decision
# 10). `GONK_METER_STORE_BACKEND` (cmd/gonk-meter/main.go's `openStore`) has
# EXACTLY ONE case: "postgres". There is no `store.OpenDolt`, so `backend: dolt`
# is not a value this chart may offer -- it would render a meter that fails to
# start, not a meter with a different (worse) durability property.
#
#   backend: postgres -> the ONLY backend. The dolt component above still exists
#                        and still runs (bundled or external, per dolt.enabled)
#                        -- it backs Gas City's BEADS STORE ONLY, and has no
#                        relationship to the ledger any more.
# =============================================================================
ledger:
  backend: postgres       # enum: [postgres]. This is a single-value enum, not a
                          # switch -- it exists so a future backend can be added
                          # without a breaking values-key rename, and so the
                          # render-time fail (below) has something to check
                          # against if someone sets it to "dolt" by hand.
                          # `memory` and `dolt` are NOT options: `memory` loses
                          # reservations on restart (fail-open on headroom);
                          # `dolt` does not serialize the reservation race at
                          # all (measured, not assumed -- see the spike above).
  postgres:
    # shared   : TENANCY ON THE CNPG CLUSTER THE OWNER ALREADY RUNS
    #            (databases-app/postgres). Renders NOTHING. This is the DEFAULT and
    #            it is what docs/environment.md says the fallback actually is --
    #            standing up a second Postgres next to it would be a new stateful
    #            dependency nobody asked for.
    #            *** IT NEEDS FOUR OBJECTS IN THE GITOPS REPO, NOT ONE. *** See
    #            Task 6 Step 5b and chart/gonk/README.md. Copy apps/nagus/.
    # cnpg     : emit a NEW postgresql.cnpg.io/v1 Cluster (for a stranger with no
    #            existing CNPG tenancy). Requires the CNPG operator; the chart does
    #            not install it.
    # external : nothing to render; meter just reads the DSN.
    mode: shared          # shared | cnpg | external
    database: gonk_ledger
    shared:
      clusterName: postgres
      clusterNamespace: databases-app
      roleName: gonk
      # meter connects to <clusterName>-rw.<clusterNamespace>.svc:5432.
      # *** -rw, NOT -ro. *** A reservation written to a read-only replica is a
      # reservation that does not exist, and a reservation that does not exist is
      # a budget that gets spent twice.
    cnpg:
      instances: 1
      imageName: ""       # "" -> CNPG's default
      storage:
        size: 20Gi
        storageClass: ""
      # CNPG generates the app-user Secret itself; meter still reads its DSN from
      # secrets.ledger (below), which you point at CNPG's generated app secret or
      # at your own. The chart does NOT copy one Secret into another.

# =============================================================================
# SECRETS: names and key names ONLY. Never values.
# The chart renders NO Secret objects. It mounts what you provisioned.
# =============================================================================
secrets:
  mountRoot: /etc/gonk/secrets
  gitlab:
    existingSecret: gonk-gitlab
    keys:
      token: token
      tokenPrevious: token-previous  # rotation slot 2 (P02 AD-4b); opt-in below
      adminToken: admin-token        # only read when gitlab.mode=split-credential
  webhook:
    existingSecret: gonk-webhook
    keys:
      token: token
      tokenPrevious: token-previous
  meterAPI:
    existingSecret: gonk-meter-api
    keys:
      token: token
      tokenPrevious: token-previous
  litellm:
    existingSecret: gonk-litellm
    keys:
      adminKey: admin-key
      adminKeyPrevious: admin-key-previous
  ledger:
    existingSecret: gonk-ledger
    keys:
      dsn: dsn
  # Rotation slot 2 is OPT-IN per credential, because a projected volume whose key
  # does not exist in the Secret makes the pod fail to start -- and slot 2 usually
  # does not exist. Rotation procedure: write the new value to slot 1 and the old
  # to slot 2, flip these to true, roll, drop slot 2, flip back.
  rotation:
    gitlabPrevious: false
    webhookPrevious: false
    meterAPIPrevious: false
    litellmAdminPrevious: false
  # The Secret gonk-meter WRITES per-project LiteLLM virtual keys into (the
  # KeySink, Task 4). One Secret per project, named gonk-key-<slug>, key
  # LITELLM_API_KEY. Meter's Role is scoped to this prefix.
  keySink:
    namePrefix: gonk-key-

# =============================================================================
# OPERATOR CONFIG  (mounted into gonk-meter; consumed by pkg/opercfg)
#
# THIS IS LOAD-BEARING POLICY, NOT DECORATION.
#
# `instance.ladder` is an ALLOW-LIST, and silence at a layer imposes NO
# constraint (ADR-002). An empty instance ladder therefore means a project may
# name ANY rung it likes, bounded only by budget. The schema requires at least
# one entry, and gonk-meter refuses to start without one.
#
# Budget ceilings only TIGHTEN downward: a project can spend less than the
# instance allows, never more. monthly_cost_usd: 0 below means NO PROJECT CAN
# SPEND A DOLLAR, whatever its .gonk.yml says. To enable cloud rungs you must
# make THREE deliberate edits: add the rung to `rungs`, add it to
# `instance.ladder`, and raise `instance.budget.monthly_cost_usd`.
# =============================================================================
operatorConfig:
  version: 1
  instance:
    enabled: true
    # THE CHART SHIPS NO LADDER DEFAULT. A rung name is site-local (it maps, via
    # `rungs` below, to a real LiteLLM model on YOUR proxy). Shipping a default
    # like `qwen-local` would name a model the operator's LiteLLM has never heard
    # of -- there IS no model called `qwen-local` (Plan 04 AD-1); the real cluster
    # runs `qwen3-14b`/`qwen3-8b-bailey`. A chart that "works out of the box" by
    # pointing at a phantom model 404s at the first token and gonk gets blamed. So
    # this is EMPTY, and the chart FAILS TO RENDER until you supply it (G1). The
    # operator provides the ladder + catalog via HelmRelease values.
    ladder: []                              # REQUIRED from the operator. Empty -> fail (G1).
    budget:
      monthly_cost_usd: 0                   # AD-4: no cloud spend by default
      monthly_tokens: "5G"
      per_task_tokens: "2M"
  groups: {}
    # agentic:
    #   budget: { monthly_cost_usd: 50 }
    # agentic/experiments:
    #   enabled: false
  # THE CHART SHIPS NO RUNG CATALOG. It is site-local operator config; the chart
  # FAILS TO RENDER when `rungs` is empty (a rung named in the ladder that is not
  # in the catalog cannot be priced -- G2). Supply it via HelmRelease values.
  # LOCAL rungs: est_cost_usd MUST be 0 and synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens MUST be
  # > 0 (the synthetic price is what makes LiteLLM's USD virtual-key ceiling a
  # hard door for TOKENS too -- configure the SAME number in LiteLLM's model list).
  # CLOUD rungs: est_cost_usd MUST be > 0 and synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens MUST NOT
  # be set. Example (NOT a default -- on this cluster qwen-local -> qwen3-14b):
  #   - {name: qwen-local, kind: local, model: qwen3-14b, est_cost_usd: 0, est_tokens: "200K", synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens: 0.20}
  #   - {name: glm,        kind: cloud, model: glm-5,     est_cost_usd: 0.40, est_tokens: "200K"}
  rungs: []                                 # REQUIRED from the operator. Empty -> fail (G2).
  meter:
    max_spend_staleness: 5m
    reservation_ttl: 60m
    max_clock_skew: 5m
    key_retry_backoff: 5m
    max_infra_retries: 5
    enforce_ladder_order: true

# The rung the onboarding MR's default .gonk.yml names (Plan 02 renders the
# template's `ladder:` from this). It MUST appear in operatorConfig.instance.ladder,
# or every freshly-onboarded project resolves to `disabled` and nothing ever runs
# (G5). THE CHART SHIPS NO DEFAULT -- a site-local rung name here would be the same
# phantom-model trap as the ladder above. Empty -> fail to render (G5 has nothing
# to satisfy). The operator supplies it, and it must be one of their instance.ladder
# rungs.
onboarding:
  defaultRung: ""                           # REQUIRED from the operator. Empty/out-of-ladder -> fail (G5).

# =============================================================================
# INGRESS -- the webhook, and nothing else (spec 9)
# =============================================================================
ingress:
  enabled: false          # the chart exposes NOTHING until you say so
  # OD-2 [ANSWERED, docs/environment.md]: Traefik is the only IngressClass on the
  # cluster; the host is gonk.orac.local; the issuer is orac-services-ca-issuer.
  className: traefik      # REQUIRED when enabled
  host: gonk.orac.local   # REQUIRED when enabled
  path: /hook/gitlab      # DO NOT WIDEN. The private listener must never be exposed.
  pathType: Exact
  # cert-manager issues the cert FROM THE ANNOTATION.
  #
  # *** DO NOT ALSO SHIP AN EXPLICIT Certificate OBJECT. *** Combining the two
  # once REISSUED 863 CERTS on this cluster. Annotation only, and the chart
  # renders no Certificate anywhere -- a test asserts that.
  #
  # AND: add `gonk.orac.local` to the CoreDNS hosts block
  # (gitops:clusters/orac/foundation/coredns/configmap-coredns.yaml) or in-cluster
  # resolution FAILS SILENTLY. That is a gitops-repo change; it is not optional;
  # and the symptom is a webhook that simply never arrives.
  annotations:
    cert-manager.io/cluster-issuer: orac-services-ca-issuer
    external-dns.alpha.kubernetes.io/hostname: gonk.orac.local
  tls:
    enabled: true
    secretName: ""        # "" -> cert-manager names it from the Ingress

# =============================================================================
# NETWORK POLICY (spec 9)
# =============================================================================
#
# *** READ THIS BEFORE YOU TRUST ANY OF IT. ***
#
# NETWORKPOLICIES ARE NOT ENFORCED ON THE TARGET CLUSTER (2026-07-13). Flannel
# does not implement them, and the Cilium HelmRelease that would is SUSPENDED.
# Everything below RENDERS, VALIDATES, and BLOCKS NOTHING.
#
# The concrete consequence: LiteLLM routes local models to Ollama at
# http://192.168.1.142:11434, WHICH REQUIRES NO CREDENTIAL. An agent pod can call
# it directly, bypass LiteLLM entirely, and burn local GPU with ZERO METERING.
# Spec 9's "budgets cannot be bypassed" is NOT MET at the network layer.
#
# Owner decision 2026-07-13: SHIP IT, DOCUMENT THE GAP, DO NOT GATE ON IT. So the
# policies are here, correct, ready to enforce the day Cilium is unsuspended.
# Until then, say it the honest way:
#     CLOUD-rung budgets are HARD.  LOCAL-model budgets are ADVISORY.
# See docs/environment.md and chart/gonk/README.md.
#
networkPolicy:
  enabled: true
  # The label selector for the AGENT SESSION PODS that Gas City's k8s session
  # provider creates. THE CHART DOES NOT CREATE THOSE PODS. OD-5 is ANSWERED:
  # Gas City hardcodes `app: gc-agent` on every agent pod (internal/runtime/k8s/
  # pod.go; the provider lists by app=gc-agent), so that is the default below.
  # BUT: a RIGHT selector on an UNENFORCED policy still blocks nothing -- this
  # cluster does not enforce NetworkPolicy (Flannel; Cilium suspended). The chart
  # refuses to render an EMPTY selector (G10); it cannot detect a WRONG one.
  # Plan 06's egress-denial test is the only thing that can, and it is SKIPPED
  # until Cilium lands. Un-skipping it is the gate.
  agentPodSelector:
    app: gc-agent
  # OD-6 [ANSWERED]: GitLab runs IN-CLUSTER, namespace `gitlab`. Use the
  # namespaceSelector below. Do NOT use gitlab.cidrs for an in-cluster Service:
  # a CIDR there is a POD IP, and pod IPs move on every reschedule -- the policy
  # would work until the first restart and then silently stop allowing GitLab.
  gitlabInCluster:
    enabled: true
    namespaceSelector: {kubernetes.io/metadata.name: gitlab}
    podSelector: {}
  # The external-GitLab path, kept for operators whose GitLab is genuinely
  # outside the cluster. A NetworkPolicy cannot name a DNS host, so it is CIDRs.
  gitlab:
    cidrs: []                      # e.g. ["10.0.5.7/32"]
    ports: [443]
  litellm:
    namespaceSelector: {kubernetes.io/metadata.name: litellm}
    podSelector: {}
    ports: [4000]
  gascity:
    namespaceSelector: {kubernetes.io/metadata.name: gascity}
    podSelector: {}
    ports: [8080]
  # TRAEFIK is the cluster's only IngressClass (docs/environment.md).
  ingressController:
    namespaceSelector: {kubernetes.io/metadata.name: traefik}
    podSelector: {}
  prometheus:
    namespaceSelector: {kubernetes.io/metadata.name: monitoring}
    podSelector: {}
  dns:
    namespaceSelector: {kubernetes.io/metadata.name: kube-system}
    ports: [53]
  # RECONCILED, Task 0: NEW. The original egress rule for meter's ledger only
  # covered ledger.postgres.mode=cnpg (a chart-managed Cluster, matched by
  # podSelector); mode=shared -- the actual target-cluster DEFAULT
  # (databases-app/postgres) -- and mode=external had NO egress rule at all,
  # so meter's own default profile would have had no NetworkPolicy path to its
  # own ledger. Defaults to `shared`'s real namespace; override for `external`.
  ledgerPostgres:
    namespaceSelector: {kubernetes.io/metadata.name: databases-app}

# =============================================================================
# BYO SEAM: your Prometheus / Grafana. The chart NEVER installs either. (spec 7.4)
# All of this is OFF by default and gated on VALUES, not on .Capabilities --
# capability gating makes `helm template` render differently from `helm install`,
# which would make the golden tests a lie (AD-6).
# =============================================================================
monitoring:
  serviceMonitor:
    enabled: false
    interval: 30s
    scrapeTimeout: 10s
    labels: {}            # must match your Prometheus's serviceMonitorSelector
  prometheusRule:
    enabled: false
    labels: {}
  dashboards:
    enabled: false
    label: grafana_dashboard   # AD-7: kube-prometheus-stack's sidecar default
    labelValue: "1"
  otlpEndpoint: ""        # e.g. http://otel-collector.otel.svc:4317

serviceAccount:
  create: true
  annotations: {}

podSecurityContext:
  runAsNonRoot: true
  runAsUser: 65532
  fsGroup: 65532
  seccompProfile: {type: RuntimeDefault}

containerSecurityContext:
  allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
  readOnlyRootFilesystem: true
  capabilities: {drop: [ALL]}
```

- [x] **Step 6: Write `chart/gonk/values.schema.json`** — **layer A: the impossible states**

Helm validates this itself, before a single template runs. Only the guards that
JSON Schema can express live here; the cross-field ones are `fail`s in Task 2.

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "title": "gonk chart values",
  "type": "object",
  "required": ["image", "intake", "meter", "gitlab", "litellm", "gascity", "dolt", "ledger", "secrets", "operatorConfig", "onboarding"],
  "properties": {
    "image": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "registry": {"type": "string"},
        "pullPolicy": {"enum": ["Always", "IfNotPresent", "Never"]},
        "pullSecrets": {"type": "array"}
      }
    },
    "intake": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["enabled", "image", "replicaCount", "ports", "webhookPublicURL"],
      "properties": {
        "enabled": {"type": "boolean"},
        "image": {"$ref": "#/$defs/image"},
        "replicaCount": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1},
        "ports": {
          "type": "object",
          "required": ["public", "private"],
          "properties": {
            "public": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 65535},
            "private": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 65535}
          }
        },
        "webhookPublicURL": {
          "type": "string",
          "minLength": 1,
          "pattern": "^https?://",
          "description": "REQUIRED. Intake writes this into every GitLab hook it provisions."
        },
        "webhookTokenGen": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
        "webhookSSLVerify": {"type": "boolean"},
        "reconcileInterval": {"type": "string", "pattern": "^[0-9]+(ns|us|ms|s|m|h)$"}
      }
    },
    "meter": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["enabled", "image", "replicaCount", "port"],
      "properties": {
        "enabled": {"type": "boolean"},
        "image": {"$ref": "#/$defs/image"},
        "replicaCount": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1},
        "port": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 65535}
      }
    },
    "gitlab": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["url", "botUsername", "mode"],
      "properties": {
        "url": {"type": "string", "pattern": "^https?://", "minLength": 1},
        "botUsername": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
        "mode": {"enum": ["bot-does-everything", "split-credential"]}
      }
    },
    "litellm": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["mode", "externalURL"],
      "properties": {
        "mode": {"enum": ["external"]},
        "externalURL": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1, "pattern": "^https?://"},
        "emitModelPricesConfigMap": {"type": "boolean"}
      }
    },
    "gascity": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["enabled", "dispatch"],
      "properties": {
        "enabled": {
          "type": "boolean",
          "description": "Deploy the bundled Gas City controller. false => BYO controller; supervisorURL becomes required (G17)."
        },
        "dispatch": {"enum": ["http", "log"]},
        "supervisorURL": {"type": "string"},
        "supervisorPort": {
          "type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 65535,
          "description": "SMOKE-GATED (Task 0.5): config docs say 9443, earlier research 8372. Default is provisional until the smoke deploy settles it."
        },
        "delivery": {
          "enum": ["prebaked", "configmap"],
          "description": "SMOKE-GATED (Task 0.5): pack+city baked into the controller image, or mounted from a ConfigMap at /city. Must fire the gc init->.gc-start sentinel handshake."
        },
        "workload": {
          "enum": ["deployment", "pod", "statefulset"],
          "description": "SMOKE-GATED (Task 0.5): whether the single-replica reconcile loop tolerates a Deployment or needs a bare Pod/StatefulSet."
        },
        "image": {"$ref": "#/$defs/imageOptional"}
      }
    },
    "dolt": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["enabled"],
      "description": "The bundled Dolt SQL server. RECONCILED, Task 0: backs the Gas City beads store ONLY -- the meter ledger is always Postgres/CNPG (ADR-004 Decision 10; there is no ledger.backend=dolt). Beads is Dolt-only; there is no Postgres path for beads.",
      "properties": {
        "enabled": {
          "type": "boolean",
          "description": "Deploy the bundled Dolt StatefulSet. false => external Dolt; dolt.external.host becomes required (G16)."
        },
        "database": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
        "port": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 65535},
        "image": {"$ref": "#/$defs/imageOptional"},
        "external": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "host": {"type": "string"},
            "port": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 65535}
          }
        }
      }
    },
    "ledger": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["backend"],
      "description": "The METER ledger backend. RECONCILED, Task 0: Postgres/CNPG is the ONLY backend (ADR-004 Decision 10; docs/spikes/dolt-reservation-isolation.md -- Dolt measured, and failed, the reservation-race isolation test). It is LEDGER-ONLY -- it never replaces the beads store, which stays on the top-level `dolt` component regardless of this value.",
      "properties": {
        "backend": {
          "enum": ["postgres"],
          "description": "A single-value enum, not a switch: `dolt` is deliberately absent (there is no store.OpenDolt for the ledger -- see the spike). `memory` is also absent: it loses reservations on restart, which is fail-open on budget headroom."
        },
        "postgres": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "mode": {"enum": ["shared", "cnpg", "external"]},
            "database": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1}
          }
        }
      }
    },
    "secrets": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["mountRoot", "gitlab", "webhook", "meterAPI", "litellm", "ledger"],
      "properties": {
        "mountRoot": {"type": "string", "pattern": "^/"},
        "gitlab": {"$ref": "#/$defs/secretRef"},
        "webhook": {"$ref": "#/$defs/secretRef"},
        "meterAPI": {"$ref": "#/$defs/secretRef"},
        "litellm": {"$ref": "#/$defs/secretRef"},
        "ledger": {"$ref": "#/$defs/secretRef"},
        "keySink": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {"namePrefix": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1}}
        },
        "rotation": {"type": "object"}
      }
    },
    "operatorConfig": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["version", "instance", "rungs"],
      "properties": {
        "version": {"const": 1},
        "instance": {
          "type": "object",
          "required": ["ladder"],
          "properties": {
            "enabled": {"type": "boolean"},
            "ladder": {
              "type": "array",
              "minItems": 1,
              "items": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
              "description": "G1. An empty instance ladder imposes NO constraint, so a project could name any rung. gonk-meter also refuses to start without one."
            },
            "budget": {"$ref": "#/$defs/budget"}
          }
        },
        "groups": {"type": "object"},
        "rungs": {
          "type": "array",
          "minItems": 1,
          "items": {"$ref": "#/$defs/rung"}
        },
        "meter": {"type": "object"}
      }
    },
    "onboarding": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["defaultRung"],
      "properties": {"defaultRung": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1}}
    },
    "ingress": {"type": "object"},
    "networkPolicy": {"type": "object"},
    "monitoring": {"type": "object"}
  },
  "$defs": {
    "image": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["repository", "tag"],
      "properties": {
        "repository": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
        "tag": {
          "type": "string",
          "minLength": 1,
          "not": {"const": "latest"},
          "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9_][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,126}$",
          "description": "G11. No default, and `latest` is rejected: a deploy of the budget enforcer must be reproducible."
        }
      }
    },
    "imageOptional": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "repository": {"type": "string"},
        "tag": {"type": "string", "not": {"const": "latest"}}
      }
    },
    "secretRef": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["existingSecret", "keys"],
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "properties": {
        "existingSecret": {
          "type": "string",
          "minLength": 1,
          "description": "The NAME of a Secret provisioned outside this chart. G7/G14: there is no way to inline a value, and no way to disable verification."
        },
        "keys": {"type": "object", "additionalProperties": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1}}
      }
    },
    "budget": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "properties": {
        "monthly_cost_usd": {"type": "number", "minimum": 0},
        "monthly_tokens": {"type": "string", "pattern": "^[0-9]+[KMG]?$"},
        "per_task_tokens": {"type": "string", "pattern": "^[0-9]+[KMG]?$"}
      }
    },
    "rung": {
      "type": "object",
      "required": ["name", "kind", "model", "est_tokens"],
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "properties": {
        "name": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
        "kind": {"enum": ["local", "cloud"]},
        "model": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
        "est_cost_usd": {"type": "number", "minimum": 0},
        "est_tokens": {"type": "string", "pattern": "^[0-9]+[KMG]?$"},
        "synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens": {"type": "number", "exclusiveMinimum": 0}
      },
      "allOf": [
        {
          "if": {"properties": {"kind": {"const": "cloud"}}, "required": ["kind"]},
          "then": {
            "required": ["est_cost_usd"],
            "properties": {
              "est_cost_usd": {"exclusiveMinimum": 0},
              "synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens": false
            },
            "description": "G4. An unpriced cloud rung sails past the cost gate. A synthetic price on a cloud rung is a lie: its price is real and lives in LiteLLM."
          }
        },
        {
          "if": {"properties": {"kind": {"const": "local"}}, "required": ["kind"]},
          "then": {
            "required": ["synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens"],
            "properties": {"est_cost_usd": {"const": 0}},
            "description": "G3. Without a synthetic price, LiteLLM's USD ceiling never closes on tokens."
          }
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

- [x] **Step 7: Write `chart/gonk/templates/_helpers.tpl`**

```
{{/* Standard names. */}}
{{- define "gonk.name" -}}
{{- default .Chart.Name .Values.nameOverride | trunc 63 | trimSuffix "-" -}}
{{- end -}}

{{- define "gonk.fullname" -}}
{{- if .Values.fullnameOverride -}}
{{- .Values.fullnameOverride | trunc 63 | trimSuffix "-" -}}
{{- else -}}
{{- .Chart.Name | trunc 63 | trimSuffix "-" -}}
{{- end -}}
{{- end -}}

{{- define "gonk.labels" -}}
helm.sh/chart: {{ printf "%s-%s" .Chart.Name .Chart.Version | replace "+" "_" | trunc 63 | trimSuffix "-" }}
app.kubernetes.io/name: {{ include "gonk.name" . }}
app.kubernetes.io/instance: {{ .Release.Name }}
app.kubernetes.io/version: {{ .Chart.AppVersion | quote }}
app.kubernetes.io/managed-by: {{ .Release.Service }}
app.kubernetes.io/part-of: gonk
{{- end -}}

{{/* selectorLabels COMPONENT -- must be stable across upgrades. */}}
{{- define "gonk.selectorLabels" -}}
app.kubernetes.io/name: {{ include "gonk.name" .ctx }}
app.kubernetes.io/instance: {{ .ctx.Release.Name }}
app.kubernetes.io/component: {{ .component }}
{{- end -}}

{{/*
  image DICT{ctx,image} -> registry-qualified reference.

  RECONCILED, Task 0: `.image.registry`, if set on the SPECIFIC image dict,
  overrides the global `.Values.image.registry` default. This is required for
  `dolt.image` (docker.io/dolthub/dolt-sql-server) — Dolt is a third-party
  upstream image, never rebuilt into registry.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project,
  so it must NOT inherit the four first-party images' in-cluster registry.
  Without this override the helper previously produced
  `registry.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project/dolthub/dolt-sql-server:<tag>`,
  which the in-cluster registry can never serve. gonk's own four images
  (gonk-agent, gonk-intake, gonk-meter, gonk-controller) leave `.image.registry`
  unset and fall through to the global default, exactly as before.
*/}}
{{- define "gonk.image" -}}
{{- $reg := .ctx.Values.image.registry -}}
{{- if .image.registry -}}{{ $reg = .image.registry }}{{- end -}}
{{- if $reg -}}{{ printf "%s/%s:%s" (trimSuffix "/" $reg) .image.repository .image.tag }}
{{- else -}}{{ printf "%s:%s" .image.repository .image.tag }}
{{- end -}}
{{- end -}}

{{/*
  gonk.secretVolume DICT{name, secret, keys}
  A projected volume, mode 0400. optional is FALSE everywhere on purpose: a
  missing Secret or a missing key must make the pod fail to start LOUDLY, not
  start with an empty credential file.
*/}}
{{- define "gonk.secretVolume" -}}
- name: secret-{{ .name }}
  projected:
    defaultMode: 0400
    sources:
      - secret:
          name: {{ .secret }}
          items:
{{- range .keys }}
            - key: {{ . }}
              path: {{ . }}
{{- end }}
{{- end -}}

{{- define "gonk.secretMount" -}}
- name: secret-{{ .name }}
  mountPath: {{ .root }}/{{ .name }}
  readOnly: true
{{- end -}}

{{/* Where a given secret key lands on disk. NEVER an env VALUE -- only a PATH. */}}
{{- define "gonk.secretPath" -}}
{{ .root }}/{{ .name }}/{{ .key }}
{{- end -}}

{{/* ---- umbrella component wiring helpers (Tasks 5, 6, 6.5) ---- */}}

{{/*
  gonk.supervisorURL -- where intake POSTs orders. DERIVED from the in-chart
  controller Service when the controller is bundled, else the operator-supplied
  external URL. The port is smoke-gated (gascity.supervisorPort, Task 0.5).
*/}}
{{- define "gonk.supervisorURL" -}}
{{- if .Values.gascity.enabled -}}
http://gonk-controller.{{ .Release.Namespace }}.svc:{{ .Values.gascity.supervisorPort }}
{{- else -}}
{{ .Values.gascity.supervisorURL }}
{{- end -}}
{{- end -}}

{{/* gonk.doltHost / gonk.doltPort -- the bundled Dolt Service, or the external one.
     Consumed by the controller (beads) and, via the DSN, by meter (ledger). */}}
{{- define "gonk.doltHost" -}}
{{- if .Values.dolt.enabled -}}
gonk-dolt.{{ .Release.Namespace }}.svc
{{- else -}}
{{ .Values.dolt.external.host }}
{{- end -}}
{{- end -}}

{{- define "gonk.doltPort" -}}
{{- if .Values.dolt.enabled -}}{{ .Values.dolt.port }}{{- else -}}{{ .Values.dolt.external.port }}{{- end -}}
{{- end -}}
```

- [x] **Step 1: Write the failing guard tests** — `internal/charttest/guards_test.go`

```go
//go:build chart

package charttest

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

// mustFail renders and requires the error message to contain `want`. A guard
// whose message does not say what is wrong is a guard nobody can act on.
func mustFail(t *testing.T, want string, extra ...string) {
	t.Helper()
	out, err := RenderErr(t, append(Minimum(), extra...)...)
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatalf("render SUCCEEDED and must not have (expected %q)", want)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(out, want) {
		t.Fatalf("guard fired with the wrong message.\nwant substring: %q\ngot:\n%s", want, out)
	}
}

// G2: a ladder naming a rung the catalog does not price.
func TestGuardLadderRungNotInCatalog(t *testing.T) {
	mustFail(t, "not in the rung catalog",
		"--set", "operatorConfig.instance.ladder={qwen-local,ghost}")
}

// G2 applies to GROUP ladders too -- they are the layer people forget.
func TestGuardGroupLadderRungNotInCatalog(t *testing.T) {
	mustFail(t, "not in the rung catalog",
		"--set", "operatorConfig.groups.agentic.ladder={ghost}")
}

// G5: the onboarding template's rung must be in the instance ladder, or EVERY
// newly-onboarded project resolves to `disabled` and nothing ever runs.
func TestGuardOnboardingRungNotInInstanceLadder(t *testing.T) {
	mustFail(t, "every newly-onboarded project would resolve to `disabled`",
		"--set", "onboarding.defaultRung=glm",
		"--set", "operatorConfig.rungs[1].name=glm",
		"--set", "operatorConfig.rungs[1].kind=cloud",
		"--set", "operatorConfig.rungs[1].model=glm-5",
		"--set", "operatorConfig.rungs[1].est_cost_usd=0.4",
		"--set", "operatorConfig.rungs[1].est_tokens=200K")
}

// RECONCILED, Task 0: the old G6 ("meter past 1 replica quietly doubles a
// budget") and its render-time fail() are DELETED, not implemented. AD-10 is
// lifted (ADR-004) -- meter's reservation atomicity lives in Postgres, across
// replicas, not in an in-process per-project lock. There is no
// meter.unsafeAllowMultipleReplicas value. A plain replicaCount override must
// render cleanly with no guard in the way.
func TestMeterMultipleReplicasRendersWithNoGuard(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, append(Minimum(), "--set", "meter.replicaCount=2")...)
	d := MustObject(t, out, "Deployment", "gonk-meter")
	if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, "replicas: 2") {
		t.Fatalf("meter.replicaCount=2 did not take effect:\n%s", d.Doc)
	}
}

// G8: intake with nowhere to ask about budgets.
func TestGuardIntakeWithoutMeter(t *testing.T) {
	mustFail(t, "dispatch unmetered work", "--set", "meter.enabled=false")
}

// G9/G17: a BYO controller (gascity.enabled=false) with http dispatch and no
// supervisorURL. NOTE: with the controller BUNDLED (the default), the URL is
// DERIVED, so an empty supervisorURL is fine -- see TestBundledGasCityNeedsNoSupervisorURL.
// The guard only fires on the external path.
func TestGuardHTTPDispatchWithoutSupervisorURL(t *testing.T) {
	out, err := RenderErr(t, append(Minimum(),
		"--set", "gascity.enabled=false",
		"--set", "gascity.supervisorURL=")...)
	if err == nil {
		t.Fatal("gascity.enabled=false + dispatch=http with no supervisorURL rendered")
	}
	if !strings.Contains(out, "gascity.supervisorURL") {
		t.Fatalf("wrong message:\n%s", out)
	}
}

func TestLogDispatchIsAllowedWhenDeliberate(t *testing.T) {
	Render(t, append(Minimum(),
		"--set", "gascity.supervisorURL=",
		"--set", "gascity.dispatch=log")...)
}

// G10: a NetworkPolicy with an empty pod selector selects EVERY pod in the
// namespace -- which for an EGRESS policy is a catastrophe, and for the agent
// policy specifically means the spec-9 guarantee silently does not exist.
func TestGuardEmptyAgentPodSelector(t *testing.T) {
	mustFail(t, "agentPodSelector", "--set", "networkPolicy.agentPodSelector=null")
}

// G12
func TestGuardIngressWithoutHost(t *testing.T) {
	mustFail(t, "ingress.host", "--set", "ingress.enabled=true", "--set", "ingress.className=nginx")
}

func TestGuardIngressWithoutClassName(t *testing.T) {
	mustFail(t, "ingress.className", "--set", "ingress.enabled=true", "--set", "ingress.host=gonk.test")
}

// G16: a disabled Dolt with no external server. Beads is Dolt-only, so this is a
// factory that cannot even store its issues.
func TestGuardDoltDisabledWithoutExternalHost(t *testing.T) {
	mustFail(t, "dolt.external.host",
		"--set", "dolt.enabled=false")
}

func TestExternalDoltIsAllowedWithHost(t *testing.T) {
	Render(t, append(Minimum(),
		"--set", "dolt.enabled=false",
		"--set", "dolt.external.host=dolt.databases.svc")...)
}

// G17: a BYO controller with no address. The bundled path (enabled=true) must NOT
// require a supervisorURL -- it is derived.
func TestGuardBYOGasCityWithoutSupervisorURL(t *testing.T) {
	mustFail(t, "gascity.supervisorURL",
		"--set", "gascity.enabled=false")
}

func TestBundledGasCityNeedsNoSupervisorURL(t *testing.T) {
	// Minimum() sets a supervisorURL for the historical default; clearing it must
	// still render when the controller is bundled, because the URL is derived.
	Render(t, append(Minimum(),
		"--set", "gascity.enabled=true",
		"--set", "gascity.supervisorURL=")...)
}

// G20/G11: the bundled controller needs a pinned image.
func TestGuardBundledGasCityNeedsImageTag(t *testing.T) {
	mustFail(t, "gascity.image.tag",
		"--set", "gascity.image.tag=")
}

// G19, RECONCILED (Task 0): the ledger is always Postgres now, and no
// ledger.postgres.mode derives a DSN on its own -- an empty
// secrets.ledger.existingSecret is fatal regardless of dolt.enabled (beads)
// or which postgres mode is selected.
func TestGuardLedgerNeedsDSNSecret(t *testing.T) {
	mustFail(t, "secrets.ledger.existingSecret",
		"--set", "secrets.ledger.existingSecret=")
}

// The whole point of the default values: they render, and they are SAFE.
func TestDefaultsRenderAndCannotSpend(t *testing.T) {
	cm := MustObject(t, Render(t, Minimum()...), "ConfigMap", "gonk-operator-config")
	oc := cm.Data(t)["operator-config.yaml"]
	if !strings.Contains(oc, "monthly_cost_usd: 0") {
		t.Fatalf("the default instance cost ceiling is not $0:\n%s", oc)
	}
	if strings.Contains(oc, "kind: cloud") {
		t.Fatal("a cloud rung is in the DEFAULT catalog; the default install must not be able to spend")
	}
}

// The operator config the chart renders must be exactly what pkg/opercfg eats.
func TestOperatorConfigMapShape(t *testing.T) {
	cm := MustObject(t, Render(t, Minimum()...), "ConfigMap", "gonk-operator-config")
	oc := cm.Data(t)["operator-config.yaml"]
	for _, want := range []string{
		"version: 1", "instance:", "ladder:", "- qwen-local",
		"rungs:", "name: qwen-local", "kind: local",
		"synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens: 0.2", "est_tokens: 200K",
		"enforce_ladder_order: true",
	} {
		if !strings.Contains(oc, want) {
			t.Errorf("operator config is missing %q:\n%s", want, oc)
		}
	}
}

// Decision 9: the synthetic prices the operator must ALSO configure in LiteLLM
// come from the SAME catalog, so they cannot drift within the chart. (They can
// still drift from LiteLLM itself -- that is Plan 06's.)
func TestLiteLLMPriceConfigMapMatchesTheCatalog(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, Minimum()...)
	prices := MustObject(t, out, "ConfigMap", "gonk-litellm-model-prices").Data(t)["model-prices.yaml"]
	if !strings.Contains(prices, "qwen3-coder-30b") {
		t.Fatalf("the local model is not in the advisory price list:\n%s", prices)
	}
	// 0.20 USD / 1M tokens == 2e-07 USD/token, in LiteLLM's per-token fields.
	if !strings.Contains(prices, "input_cost_per_token") {
		t.Fatalf("no per-token price:\n%s", prices)
	}
	// A cloud rung must NOT appear: its price is real and lives in LiteLLM already.
	if strings.Contains(prices, "claude") {
		t.Fatal("a cloud model is in the SYNTHETIC price list; that would be a lie")
	}
}
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement `chart/gonk/templates/_guards.tpl`**

Every layer-B guard in one file, invoked once from the operator ConfigMap (which
always renders). A `fail` anywhere aborts the whole render — which is precisely
the behaviour we want.

```
{{/*
  gonk.guards -- every fail-closed check the JSON Schema cannot express.

  Read the messages carefully before you change one: each says WHAT breaks, not
  just that something is wrong. A guard nobody can act on is a guard that gets
  disabled.
*/}}
{{- define "gonk.guards" -}}

{{- /* Build the set of rung names in the catalog. */ -}}
{{- $catalog := dict -}}
{{- range .Values.operatorConfig.rungs -}}
  {{- $_ := set $catalog .name . -}}
{{- end -}}

{{- /* G2: every rung named in ANY ladder must be priced by the catalog. */ -}}
{{- $ladders := dict "instance" .Values.operatorConfig.instance.ladder -}}
{{- range $g, $p := .Values.operatorConfig.groups -}}
  {{- if $p.ladder -}}{{- $_ := set $ladders (printf "group %s" $g) $p.ladder -}}{{- end -}}
{{- end -}}
{{- range $where, $ladder := $ladders -}}
  {{- range $ladder -}}
    {{- if not (hasKey $catalog .) -}}
      {{- fail (printf "operatorConfig: the %s ladder names rung %q, which is not in the rung catalog (operatorConfig.rungs). gonk-meter cannot price a rung it does not know, and will refuse to start." $where .) -}}
    {{- end -}}
  {{- end -}}
{{- end -}}

{{- /* G3/G4 belt-and-braces: the schema enforces these, but a `--set-json` of a
       whole rung can smuggle a shape past a conditional if the schema is ever
       relaxed. These two invariants are the difference between a budget and a
       suggestion, so check them twice. */ -}}
{{- range .Values.operatorConfig.rungs -}}
  {{- if eq .kind "local" -}}
    {{- if not (gt (float64 (default 0 .synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens)) 0.0) -}}
      {{- fail (printf "operatorConfig: local rung %q has no synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens > 0. Local inference is free, so LiteLLM's USD virtual-key ceiling would never move, and monthly_tokens would have NO hard enforcement anywhere." .name) -}}
    {{- end -}}
    {{- if ne (float64 (default 0 .est_cost_usd)) 0.0 -}}
      {{- fail (printf "operatorConfig: local rung %q has est_cost_usd != 0. Local rungs cost no real money; a non-zero real cost would charge projects for inference they are not billed for." .name) -}}
    {{- end -}}
  {{- else if eq .kind "cloud" -}}
    {{- if not (gt (float64 (default 0 .est_cost_usd)) 0.0) -}}
      {{- fail (printf "operatorConfig: cloud rung %q has est_cost_usd <= 0. gonk-meter applies the cost gate ONLY to priced rungs, so an unpriced cloud rung is free money: it would sail past a $0 budget." .name) -}}
    {{- end -}}
    {{- if .synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens -}}
      {{- fail (printf "operatorConfig: cloud rung %q has a synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens. A cloud rung's price is REAL and lives in LiteLLM's own model config; declaring a synthetic price for it would be a lie." .name) -}}
    {{- end -}}
  {{- end -}}
{{- end -}}

{{- /* G5: the onboarding MR's default .gonk.yml names exactly one rung. */ -}}
{{- if not (has .Values.onboarding.defaultRung .Values.operatorConfig.instance.ladder) -}}
  {{- fail (printf "onboarding.defaultRung is %q but the instance ladder is [%s]. The onboarding MR ships a .gonk.yml naming that rung, and a rung outside the instance allow-list resolves to an empty ladder -- so every newly-onboarded project would resolve to `disabled` and nothing would ever run (ADR-002)." .Values.onboarding.defaultRung (join ", " .Values.operatorConfig.instance.ladder)) -}}
{{- end -}}

{{- /* Former G6 is VOID (Task 0 reconciliation): Task 0b's spike ran, Dolt
       failed, the ledger moved to Postgres, and ADR-004 records that
       ReserveIfFits' atomicity is now enforced in the database (SELECT ...
       FOR UPDATE + a partial UNIQUE index), across replicas -- proven by
       TestReserveIfFitsRace / TestReserveIsIdempotentAcrossReplicas with no
       service-side mutex in the way. AD-10 (single-replica) is lifted. There
       is deliberately no fail() here and no unsafeAllowMultipleReplicas
       value: meter.replicaCount is a plain resource knob now. */ -}}

{{- /* G8: intake must have a meter to ask, or it would dispatch unmetered work. */ -}}
{{- if and .Values.intake.enabled (not .Values.meter.enabled) -}}
  {{- fail "intake.enabled is true but meter.enabled is false. gonk-intake asks gonk-meter whether a project may spend before it fires an order; with no meter it would dispatch unmetered work. Deploy meter, or disable intake." -}}
{{- end -}}

{{- /* G9/G17: orders that go nowhere, and a BYO controller with no address.
       When gascity.enabled=true the supervisor URL is DERIVED from the in-chart
       controller Service, so it need not be set. It is required ONLY when the
       controller is external (enabled=false) and dispatch is http. */ -}}
{{- if and .Values.intake.enabled (eq .Values.gascity.dispatch "http") (not .Values.gascity.enabled) (not .Values.gascity.supervisorURL) -}}
  {{- fail "gascity.dispatch is \"http\" and gascity.enabled is false (BYO controller), but gascity.supervisorURL is empty. Either set gascity.enabled=true to deploy the bundled controller, set gascity.supervisorURL to your external supervisor, or set gascity.dispatch=\"log\" DELIBERATELY (a dry run: orders are logged and never executed)." -}}
{{- end -}}

{{- /* G16: a disabled Dolt with no external server. Beads is Dolt-ONLY -- there is
       no Postgres path for it -- so the only alternative to the bundled server is
       an external Dolt (e.g. a self-hosted DoltLab). */ -}}
{{- if and (not .Values.dolt.enabled) (not .Values.dolt.external.host) -}}
  {{- fail "dolt.enabled is false but dolt.external.host is empty. The Gas City beads store is Dolt-ONLY (there is no beads-on-Postgres option), so a disabled bundled Dolt REQUIRES an external Dolt SQL server (e.g. self-hosted DoltLab). Set dolt.external.host/port, or re-enable dolt.enabled=true." -}}
{{- end -}}

{{- /* G19, RECONCILED (Task 0): the ledger is ALWAYS Postgres now (there is no
       "dolt" position on ledger.backend -- ADR-004 Decision 10), and UNLIKE the
       bundled Dolt (which derives its DSN from the in-chart Service + the
       shipped root/empty/--no-tls auth), NONE of the three Postgres modes
       (shared/cnpg/external) auto-derive a DSN the chart can wire up on its
       own -- `mode: cnpg` renders a Cluster CR, but its generated app Secret
       still has to be pointed at via secrets.ledger.existingSecret by hand
       (Task 6 Step 5's own NOTE), and `shared`/`external` render nothing at
       all. So every mode needs a real DSN Secret, and an empty
       secrets.ledger.existingSecret is fatal in all three, not just one. */ -}}
{{- if not .Values.secrets.ledger.existingSecret -}}
  {{- fail "secrets.ledger.existingSecret is empty. gonk-meter's ledger is Postgres/CNPG (ADR-004; there is no dolt-backed ledger any more), and none of the three ledger.postgres.mode values (shared, cnpg, external) derive a DSN automatically the way the bundled Dolt used to -- even mode=cnpg's generated Cluster Secret must be pointed at explicitly. Provision the ledger DSN Secret (see chart/gonk/README.md and Task 6 Step 5b's four-object gitops recipe for mode=shared)." -}}
{{- end -}}

{{- /* G10: a NetworkPolicy that selects nothing, or everything. */ -}}
{{- if .Values.networkPolicy.enabled -}}
  {{- if not .Values.networkPolicy.agentPodSelector -}}
    {{- fail "networkPolicy.enabled is true but networkPolicy.agentPodSelector is empty. An egress NetworkPolicy with an empty podSelector selects EVERY pod in the namespace. Worse, the agent-session policy is what makes spec 9's \"agent pods reach only GitLab and LiteLLM, so budgets cannot be bypassed\" true -- and gonk does not create those pods (Gas City's session provider does), so the chart cannot infer their labels. Set the selector to match them. NOTE: the chart cannot detect a WRONG selector, only an empty one. AND NOTE: NetworkPolicy is NOT ENFORCED on this cluster today (Flannel does not implement it; the Cilium HelmRelease is suspended), so this policy currently blocks NOTHING and local-model budgets are ADVISORY. Plan 06 carries the egress-denial test, SKIPPED until Cilium lands; un-skipping it is the gate." -}}
  {{- end -}}
  {{- if and (not .Values.networkPolicy.gitlab.cidrs) (not .Values.networkPolicy.gitlabInCluster.enabled) -}}
    {{- fail "networkPolicy.enabled is true but neither networkPolicy.gitlab.cidrs nor networkPolicy.gitlabInCluster is set. A NetworkPolicy egress rule cannot name a DNS host; it needs an ipBlock CIDR (or a selector, if GitLab is in-cluster). Without one, gonk-intake cannot reach GitLab at all." -}}
  {{- end -}}
{{- end -}}

{{- /* G12 */ -}}
{{- if .Values.ingress.enabled -}}
  {{- if not .Values.ingress.host -}}{{- fail "ingress.enabled is true but ingress.host is empty." -}}{{- end -}}
  {{- if not .Values.ingress.className -}}{{- fail "ingress.enabled is true but ingress.className is empty." -}}{{- end -}}
  {{- if ne .Values.ingress.path "/hook/gitlab" -}}
    {{- fail (printf "ingress.path is %q. The public listener serves POST /hook/gitlab and nothing else; widening the ingress path cannot expose anything useful, but it CAN expose the private listener if you also change the backend port. Leave it alone." .Values.ingress.path) -}}
  {{- end -}}
{{- end -}}

{{- /* The bundled Dolt must have a pinned image (it is tier-0 state -- a floating
       tag is not a reproducible deploy). RECONCILED, Task 0: it backs the beads
       store ONLY now (never the ledger -- ADR-004 Decision 10), but that changes
       nothing about why it must be pinned. */ -}}
{{- if and .Values.dolt.enabled (not .Values.dolt.image.tag) -}}
  {{- fail "dolt.enabled is true but dolt.image.tag is empty. The bundled Dolt is tier-0 state (the Gas City beads store); pin an exact image tag (no default, never `latest`)." -}}
{{- end -}}

{{- /* The bundled controller must have a pinned image when it is deployed. */ -}}
{{- if and .Values.gascity.enabled (not .Values.gascity.image.tag) -}}
  {{- fail "gascity.enabled is true but gascity.image.tag is empty. The chart deploys the Gas City controller (Plan 04's gonk-controller image); pin an exact tag (no default, never `latest`)." -}}
{{- end -}}
{{- end -}}
```

- [x] **Step 4: Implement `chart/gonk/templates/configmap-operator-config.yaml`**

```yaml
{{- include "gonk.guards" . -}}
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: gonk-operator-config
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: meter
data:
  # Consumed by gonk-meter via --operator-config. pkg/opercfg validates this and
  # gonk-meter REFUSES TO START if it does not validate -- every budget decision
  # flows from this file. Meter re-reads it from disk on each tick (a ConfigMap
  # update is a file change, not a restart), and keeps the PREVIOUS config if the
  # new one fails to validate. It never falls back to no config at all.
  operator-config.yaml: |
    {{- toYaml (omit .Values.operatorConfig "__nothing__") | nindent 4 }}
```

> **Why `toYaml` of the raw values, and not a hand-written template:** the shape
> in `values.yaml` is *deliberately identical* to `pkg/opercfg`'s on-disk shape.
> One transcription is one place to get it wrong; zero transcriptions is none.
> `TestOperatorConfigMapShape` is what holds that identity down.

- [x] **Step 5: Implement `chart/gonk/templates/configmap-litellm-model-prices.yaml`**

```yaml
{{- if .Values.litellm.emitModelPricesConfigMap }}
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: gonk-litellm-model-prices
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: litellm-advisory
data:
  # ADVISORY. Nothing in this chart applies this to LiteLLM -- LiteLLM is BYO
  # (OD-3). Paste these into LiteLLM's model_list.
  #
  # THIS IS NOT COSMETIC. Local inference is free, so without a SYNTHETIC price
  # configured in LiteLLM, a local rung moves LiteLLM's USD counter by $0 -- and
  # the USD virtual-key budget is the ONLY HARD DOOR gonk has. A project's
  # monthly_tokens ceiling is converted into that USD ceiling using exactly these
  # numbers. If LiteLLM and this file disagree, the hard door is in the wrong
  # place, and nothing will tell you. Plan 06 diffs LiteLLM's /model/info against
  # this. (Cloud models are ABSENT on purpose: their prices are real and already
  # live in LiteLLM.)
  model-prices.yaml: |
    model_list:
    {{- range .Values.operatorConfig.rungs }}
    {{- if eq .kind "local" }}
      - model_name: {{ .model }}
        litellm_params:
          model: {{ .model }}
          input_cost_per_token: {{ divf (float64 .synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens) 1e6 }}
          output_cost_per_token: {{ divf (float64 .synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens) 1e6 }}
    {{- end }}
    {{- end }}
{{- end }}
```

- [x] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `internal/charttest/secrets_test.go`

```go
//go:build chart

package charttest

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

// G14: the chart renders NO Secret, ever. Secrets come from Vault/1Password via
// an ExternalSecret, outside this chart.
func TestChartRendersNoSecretObjects(t *testing.T) {
	for _, o := range Objects(t, Render(t, Minimum()...)) {
		if o.Kind == "Secret" {
			t.Fatalf("the chart rendered a Secret (%s). Secrets are provisioned OUTSIDE the chart.", o.Metadata.Name)
		}
	}
}

// G14: and no credential-shaped string leaks into a manifest.
func TestNoSecretMaterialInRender(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, Minimum()...)
	for _, bad := range []string{"glpat-", "sk-", "-----BEGIN", "PRIVATE KEY"} {
		if strings.Contains(out, bad) {
			t.Fatalf("the render contains %q", bad)
		}
	}
}

// THE RULE: secrets are FILES. Never envFrom, never secretKeyRef.
func TestSecretsAreNeverEnvironmentVariables(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, Minimum()...)
	for _, o := range Objects(t, out) {
		if o.Kind != "Deployment" && o.Kind != "StatefulSet" {
			continue
		}
		for _, bad := range []string{"secretKeyRef", "envFrom"} {
			if strings.Contains(o.Doc, bad) {
				t.Fatalf("%s/%s uses %s. Secrets are FILE MOUNTS: the environment leaks into "+
					"/proc/<pid>/environ, into crash dumps, and into every child process.",
					o.Kind, o.Metadata.Name, bad)
			}
		}
	}
}

// The provisioning contract, asserted: what key, in what Secret, at what path,
// in what env var. If Plans 02/03 rename one of these, THIS is what catches it.
func TestMeterSecretContract(t *testing.T) {
	d := MustObject(t, Render(t, Minimum()...), "Deployment", "gonk-meter")
	for _, want := range []string{
		"LITELLM_ADMIN_KEY_FILE",
		"/etc/gonk/secrets/litellm/admin-key",
		"GONK_METER_TOKEN_FILE",
		"/etc/gonk/secrets/meter-api/token",
		"GONK_METER_STORE_DSN_FILE",
		"/etc/gonk/secrets/ledger/dsn",
		"GONK_METER_STORE_BACKEND",
		"--operator-config",
		"defaultMode: 256", // 0400
	} {
		if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, want) {
			t.Errorf("gonk-meter deployment is missing %q", want)
		}
	}
}

func TestIntakeSecretContract(t *testing.T) {
	d := MustObject(t, Render(t, Minimum()...), "Deployment", "gonk-intake")
	for _, want := range []string{
		"GONK_GITLAB_TOKEN_FILE", "/etc/gonk/secrets/gitlab/token",
		"GONK_WEBHOOK_SECRET_FILE", "/etc/gonk/secrets/webhook/token",
		"GONK_METER_TOKEN_FILE", "/etc/gonk/secrets/meter-api/token",
	} {
		if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, want) {
			t.Errorf("gonk-intake deployment is missing %q", want)
		}
	}
	// The admin token is only mounted in split-credential mode.
	if strings.Contains(d.Doc, "GONK_GITLAB_ADMIN_TOKEN_FILE") {
		t.Error("the admin token is mounted in bot-does-everything mode; that Secret key does not exist there and the pod would not start")
	}
}

func TestSplitCredentialMountsTheAdminToken(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, append(Minimum(), "--set", "gitlab.mode=split-credential")...)
	d := MustObject(t, out, "Deployment", "gonk-intake")
	if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, "GONK_GITLAB_ADMIN_TOKEN_FILE") {
		t.Fatal("split-credential mode did not mount the admin token")
	}
}

// Rotation slot 2 is OPT-IN: a projected volume with a key that does not exist
// in the Secret makes the pod fail to start.
func TestRotationSlotTwoIsOptIn(t *testing.T) {
	d := MustObject(t, Render(t, Minimum()...), "Deployment", "gonk-intake")
	if strings.Contains(d.Doc, "GONK_WEBHOOK_SECRET_PREVIOUS_FILE") {
		t.Fatal("slot 2 is mounted by default; the key usually does not exist and the pod would not start")
	}
	out := Render(t, append(Minimum(), "--set", "secrets.rotation.webhookPrevious=true")...)
	d2 := MustObject(t, out, "Deployment", "gonk-intake")
	if !strings.Contains(d2.Doc, "GONK_WEBHOOK_SECRET_PREVIOUS_FILE") {
		t.Fatal("opting in to slot 2 did not mount it")
	}
}

// AD-5, RECONCILED (Task 0): replicas: 1 + Recreate are the chart's DEFAULT
// for the modest footprint of the initial deployment -- NOT a correctness
// requirement any more. ADR-004 records that meter's ReserveIfFits atomicity
// is enforced in Postgres, across replicas (TestReserveIfFitsRace /
// TestReserveIsIdempotentAcrossReplicas, internal/meter/store), so this test
// asserts the DEFAULT profile's shape, not a guard the chart enforces --
// raising meter.replicaCount renders cleanly (see
// TestMeterMultipleReplicasRendersWithNoGuard, Task 2).
func TestMeterDefaultsToSingleReplicaAndRecreate(t *testing.T) {
	d := MustObject(t, Render(t, Minimum()...), "Deployment", "gonk-meter")
	if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, "replicas: 1") {
		t.Error("gonk-meter's default replicaCount is not 1")
	}
	if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, "type: Recreate") {
		t.Error("gonk-meter's default strategy is not Recreate")
	}
}
```

```yaml
{{- if and .Values.meter.enabled .Values.serviceAccount.create }}
apiVersion: v1
kind: ServiceAccount
metadata:
  name: gonk-meter
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: meter
  {{- with .Values.serviceAccount.annotations }}
  annotations:
    {{- toYaml . | nindent 4 }}
  {{- end }}
# gonk-meter needs an API token: it WRITES per-project LiteLLM virtual keys as
# Secrets (the KeySink, Task 4). This is the one component in the chart that
# talks to the API server.
automountServiceAccountToken: true
{{- end }}
```
