- [x] **Step 4: Implement `chart/gonk/templates/deployment-gonk-meter.yaml`**

```yaml
{{- if .Values.meter.enabled }}
{{- $root := .Values.secrets.mountRoot }}
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: gonk-meter
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: meter
spec:
  replicas: {{ .Values.meter.replicaCount }}
  strategy:
    # Recreate, NOT RollingUpdate. A rolling update runs the old and the new pod
    # at the same time, and gonk-meter's per-project budget lock is IN-PROCESS:
    # two meters, briefly, is the exact state that can grant the last of a budget
    # twice. The cost is a few seconds where /policy/decide is unreachable -- and
    # a decide that fails is a session that does not spawn, which is fail-closed.
    type: Recreate
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      {{- include "gonk.selectorLabels" (dict "ctx" . "component" "meter") | nindent 6 }}
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 8 }}
        app.kubernetes.io/component: meter
      annotations:
        # Roll the pod when the operator config changes. (Meter also re-reads it
        # from disk on each tick, so this is belt-and-braces -- but a config that
        # fails to validate should surface as a failed rollout, not a quiet
        # "kept the previous config" log line.)
        checksum/operator-config: {{ include (print $.Template.BasePath "/configmap-operator-config.yaml") . | sha256sum }}
        {{- with .Values.meter.podAnnotations }}{{- toYaml . | nindent 8 }}{{- end }}
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: gonk-meter
      securityContext:
        {{- toYaml .Values.podSecurityContext | nindent 8 }}
      {{- with .Values.image.pullSecrets }}
      imagePullSecrets:
        {{- toYaml . | nindent 8 }}
      {{- end }}
      containers:
        - name: meter
          image: {{ include "gonk.image" (dict "ctx" . "image" .Values.meter.image) }}
          imagePullPolicy: {{ .Values.image.pullPolicy }}
          args:
            - --operator-config=/etc/gonk/operator/operator-config.yaml
            - --listen=:{{ .Values.meter.port }}
          env:
            # *** NO SECRET VALUE IS AN ENV VAR. These are PATHS. ***
            - name: LITELLM_URL
              value: {{ .Values.litellm.externalURL | quote }}
            - name: LITELLM_ADMIN_KEY_FILE
              value: {{ include "gonk.secretPath" (dict "root" $root "name" "litellm" "key" .Values.secrets.litellm.keys.adminKey) | quote }}
            {{- if .Values.secrets.rotation.litellmAdminPrevious }}
            - name: LITELLM_ADMIN_KEY_PREVIOUS_FILE
              value: {{ include "gonk.secretPath" (dict "root" $root "name" "litellm" "key" .Values.secrets.litellm.keys.adminKeyPrevious) | quote }}
            {{- end }}
            - name: GONK_METER_TOKEN_FILE
              value: {{ include "gonk.secretPath" (dict "root" $root "name" "meter-api" "key" .Values.secrets.meterAPI.keys.token) | quote }}
            {{- if .Values.secrets.rotation.meterAPIPrevious }}
            - name: GONK_METER_TOKEN_PREVIOUS_FILE
              value: {{ include "gonk.secretPath" (dict "root" $root "name" "meter-api" "key" .Values.secrets.meterAPI.keys.tokenPrevious) | quote }}
            {{- end }}
            # THE STORE. [P03 Task 8 Step 5, `openStore`.] Both are REQUIRED and
            # neither has a default: defaulting to the in-memory store would lose
            # reservations on every restart, which is fail-OPEN on budget headroom.
            # `memory` is not selectable at all. An unknown backend, an empty DSN
            # file, or a store that will not connect is a FATAL startup error.
            - name: GONK_METER_STORE_BACKEND
              value: {{ .Values.ledger.backend | quote }}
            - name: GONK_METER_STORE_DSN_FILE
              value: {{ include "gonk.secretPath" (dict "root" $root "name" "ledger" "key" .Values.secrets.ledger.keys.dsn) | quote }}
            # The KeySink (Task 4) writes per-project Secrets into this namespace.
            - name: GONK_KEYSINK_NAMESPACE
              value: {{ .Release.Namespace | quote }}
            - name: GONK_KEYSINK_PREFIX
              value: {{ .Values.secrets.keySink.namePrefix | quote }}
            {{- if .Values.monitoring.otlpEndpoint }}
            - name: OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT
              value: {{ .Values.monitoring.otlpEndpoint | quote }}
            {{- end }}
          ports:
            - name: http-api
              containerPort: {{ .Values.meter.port }}
          # /healthz = the process is alive.
          livenessProbe:
            httpGet: {path: /healthz, port: http-api}
            initialDelaySeconds: 5
            periodSeconds: 20
          # /readyz = the FIRST SPEND SYNC has completed. Until then meter's view
          # of what has been spent is empty, and an empty view is fail-OPEN on
          # headroom -- so it must not serve /policy/decide. A long cold start is
          # correct behaviour; give it room.
          readinessProbe:
            httpGet: {path: /readyz, port: http-api}
            initialDelaySeconds: 5
            periodSeconds: 5
            failureThreshold: 12
          startupProbe:
            httpGet: {path: /healthz, port: http-api}
            periodSeconds: 3
            failureThreshold: 20
          resources:
            {{- toYaml .Values.meter.resources | nindent 12 }}
          securityContext:
            {{- toYaml .Values.containerSecurityContext | nindent 12 }}
          volumeMounts:
            - name: operator-config
              mountPath: /etc/gonk/operator
              readOnly: true
            {{- include "gonk.secretMount" (dict "root" $root "name" "litellm") | nindent 12 }}
            {{- include "gonk.secretMount" (dict "root" $root "name" "meter-api") | nindent 12 }}
            {{- include "gonk.secretMount" (dict "root" $root "name" "ledger") | nindent 12 }}
            - name: tmp
              mountPath: /tmp
      volumes:
        - name: operator-config
          configMap:
            name: gonk-operator-config
        {{- $llKeys := list .Values.secrets.litellm.keys.adminKey }}
        {{- if .Values.secrets.rotation.litellmAdminPrevious }}{{- $llKeys = append $llKeys .Values.secrets.litellm.keys.adminKeyPrevious }}{{- end }}
        {{- include "gonk.secretVolume" (dict "name" "litellm" "secret" .Values.secrets.litellm.existingSecret "keys" $llKeys) | nindent 8 }}
        {{- $mKeys := list .Values.secrets.meterAPI.keys.token }}
        {{- if .Values.secrets.rotation.meterAPIPrevious }}{{- $mKeys = append $mKeys .Values.secrets.meterAPI.keys.tokenPrevious }}{{- end }}
        {{- include "gonk.secretVolume" (dict "name" "meter-api" "secret" .Values.secrets.meterAPI.existingSecret "keys" $mKeys) | nindent 8 }}
        {{- include "gonk.secretVolume" (dict "name" "ledger" "secret" .Values.secrets.ledger.existingSecret "keys" (list .Values.secrets.ledger.keys.dsn)) | nindent 8 }}
        - name: tmp
          emptyDir: {}
      {{- with .Values.meter.nodeSelector }}
      nodeSelector: {{- toYaml . | nindent 8 }}
      {{- end }}
      {{- with .Values.meter.tolerations }}
      tolerations: {{- toYaml . | nindent 8 }}
      {{- end }}
      {{- with .Values.meter.affinity }}
      affinity: {{- toYaml . | nindent 8 }}
      {{- end }}
{{- end }}
```

### Task 4: The RBAC that makes the Kubernetes `KeySink` legal

> ### ⚠ OWNERSHIP CHANGED. READ THIS BEFORE YOU WRITE A LINE OF GO.
>
> An earlier draft of this plan **wrote `internal/meter/keysink/k8s.go` here**,
> because Plan 03 declared a hard dependency on a Kubernetes `KeySink` **that no
> plan specified** (*"gonk-meter cannot deliver virtual keys to agent pods at
> all"*). That left one file with two owners — the exact defect that produces two
> divergent implementations three months later.
>
> **Settled** (P03 §AD-1's ownership table):
>
> | Artifact | Owner |
> |---|---|
> | `internal/meter/keysink/keysink.go` — interface, `Memory`, `Slug` | **Plan 03**, Task 7 Step 4 |
> | **`internal/meter/keysink/k8s.go` + `k8s_test.go`** — the Kubernetes sink | **Plan 03**, Task 7 **Step 4b**. It is Go, it is `internal/meter/`, it needs no cluster. |
> | `GONK_KEYSINK_NAMESPACE` / `GONK_KEYSINK_PREFIX` wiring | **Plan 03**, Task 8 Step 5 |
> | **`serviceaccount-gonk-meter.yaml`, `role-gonk-meter.yaml`, `rolebinding-gonk-meter.yaml`, and the values/env that point the sink at a namespace** | **THIS TASK** |
> | Proving the RBAC actually permits the writes on a real API server | **Plan 06** |
>
> **Do not re-implement `NewK8s` here.** If it has not landed, this task blocks on
> Plan 03 Task 7 Step 4b — go finish that. What follows is the RBAC and the
> wiring, and nothing else.
>
> **Neither half works alone.** Without Plan 03's `k8s.go` meter has no sink;
> without this Role, every `Put` is a `403`, every project sits in `key-missing`,
> and `/decide` defers forever. Fail-closed and loud — and still broken.

**Files:** Create `chart/gonk/templates/serviceaccount-gonk-meter.yaml`,
`chart/gonk/templates/role-gonk-meter.yaml`,
`chart/gonk/templates/rolebinding-gonk-meter.yaml`; extend
`internal/charttest/secrets_test.go`.

**The Secret shape this RBAC has to permit — it is Plan 03's contract, restated
here only so the Role can be read on its own:** one Secret per project, named
`{{ .Values.secrets.keySink.namePrefix }}<slug>` (default `gonk-key-<slug>`),
type `Opaque`, single key **`LITELLM_API_KEY`**. `<slug>` is
`keysink.Slug(project)` — a DNS-1123 fragment with a hash suffix, so `a/b` and
`a-b` cannot collide onto one key and therefore onto one `max_budget`.

<summary><b>REFERENCE ONLY — the sink's tests, which live in Plan 03. Do not copy them into this task.</b></summary>

```go
package keysink

import (
	"context"
	"testing"

	corev1 "k8s.io/api/core/v1"
	metav1 "k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/apis/meta/v1"
	"k8s.io/client-go/kubernetes/fake"
)

func TestK8sPutCreatesASecret(t *testing.T) {
	cs := fake.NewSimpleClientset()
	ks := NewK8s(cs, "gonk", "gonk-key-")

	ref, err := ks.Put(context.Background(), "group/repo", "sk-live-abc")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Put = %v", err)
	}
	if ref.SecretKey != "LITELLM_API_KEY" {
		t.Fatalf("key ref = %+v", ref)
	}
	s, err := cs.CoreV1().Secrets("gonk").Get(context.Background(), ref.SecretName, metav1.GetOptions{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("secret not created: %v", err)
	}
	if got := string(s.Data["LITELLM_API_KEY"]); got != "sk-live-abc" {
		t.Fatalf("stored token = %q", got)
	}
	if s.Type != corev1.SecretTypeOpaque {
		t.Fatalf("secret type = %v", s.Type)
	}
	// The Secret must be findable by project without decoding its name.
	if s.Labels["gonk.orac.local/project"] == "" {
		t.Error("no project label")
	}
}

// Put is called on every reconcile pass. It MUST be idempotent, and it MUST
// update the value on a rotation.
func TestK8sPutIsIdempotentAndUpdates(t *testing.T) {
	cs := fake.NewSimpleClientset()
	ks := NewK8s(cs, "gonk", "gonk-key-")
	ctx := context.Background()

	r1, err := ks.Put(ctx, "group/repo", "sk-one")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	r2, err := ks.Put(ctx, "group/repo", "sk-two")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("second Put = %v", err)
	}
	if r1.SecretName != r2.SecretName {
		t.Fatalf("the secret name is not stable: %q vs %q", r1.SecretName, r2.SecretName)
	}
	s, _ := cs.CoreV1().Secrets("gonk").Get(ctx, r2.SecretName, metav1.GetOptions{})
	if got := string(s.Data["LITELLM_API_KEY"]); got != "sk-two" {
		t.Fatalf("rotation did not take: %q", got)
	}
	list, _ := cs.CoreV1().Secrets("gonk").List(ctx, metav1.ListOptions{})
	if len(list.Items) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("Put created %d secrets; it must be idempotent", len(list.Items))
	}
}

// De-onboarding deletes the key. Deleting a key that is not there is not an error.
func TestK8sDeleteIsIdempotent(t *testing.T) {
	cs := fake.NewSimpleClientset()
	ks := NewK8s(cs, "gonk", "gonk-key-")
	ctx := context.Background()
	if _, err := ks.Put(ctx, "group/repo", "sk-one"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := ks.Delete(ctx, "group/repo"); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Delete = %v", err)
	}
	if err := ks.Delete(ctx, "group/repo"); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("second Delete = %v; deleting an absent key must be a no-op", err)
	}
}

// The Secret NAME is derived from an untrusted GitLab path. It must be a legal
// DNS-1123 subdomain or the API server rejects it -- and a project whose key
// cannot be stored is a project that cannot spend, which is fail-closed but very
// confusing. Slug (Plan 03) is what guarantees this; assert it end to end.
func TestK8sHandlesHostileProjectPaths(t *testing.T) {
	cs := fake.NewSimpleClientset()
	ks := NewK8s(cs, "gonk", "gonk-key-")
	for _, p := range []string{
		"Group/Repo",
		"a/very/deeply/nested/group/path/that/goes/on/and/on/and/on/repo-name",
		"group/repo.with.dots",
		"group/_leading-underscore",
	} {
		ref, err := ks.Put(context.Background(), p, "sk-x")
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("Put(%q) = %v", p, err)
		}
		if len(ref.SecretName) > 63 || ref.SecretName != strings_ToLower(ref.SecretName) {
			t.Fatalf("Put(%q) produced an illegal secret name %q", p, ref.SecretName)
		}
	}
}
```

(`strings_ToLower` is `strings.ToLower` — import `strings`.)

</details>

- [ ] **Step 2: Point the sink at a namespace** — the values and the env, in
      `chart/gonk/templates/deployment-gonk-meter.yaml` (Task 3)

```yaml
            # THE KEY SINK (P03 AD-1). Unset -> meter falls back to the MEMORY sink
            # and logs at ERROR that keys are reaching nobody. A memory sink in
            # production is a factory that provisions credentials no pod can read.
            - name: GONK_KEYSINK_NAMESPACE
              value: {{ .Release.Namespace | quote }}
            - name: GONK_KEYSINK_PREFIX
              value: {{ .Values.secrets.keySink.namePrefix | quote }}
```

Meter builds its client with `rest.InClusterConfig()`, which needs **a
ServiceAccount token projected into the pod** — so `automountServiceAccountToken`
must **not** be false on this Deployment. It is the one component in this chart
that talks to the API server, and it is the only reason meter has a
ServiceAccount at all. (Intake has none: it talks to GitLab, meter, and Gas City,
and nothing else.)

```yaml
metadata:
  name: gonk-meter
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: meter
  {{- with .Values.serviceAccount.annotations }}
  annotations:
    {{- toYaml . | nindent 4 }}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the RBAC** — `chart/gonk/templates/role-gonk-meter.yaml`

```yaml
{{- if .Values.meter.enabled }}
apiVersion: rbac.authorization.k8s.io/v1
kind: Role
metadata:
  name: gonk-meter
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: meter
rules:
  # The KeySink: one Secret per project, holding that project's LiteLLM virtual
  # key. NAMESPACED, and no `get`/`list` on Secrets in general -- meter creates
  # and deletes the keys it provisions, and has no business reading anyone else's
  # (its own credentials arrive as FILE MOUNTS, not as API reads).
  #
  # resourceNames cannot be used here: the names are derived from project paths
  # that do not exist at install time. This is a namespace-scoped write grant on
  # Secrets, and it is the reason gonk-meter lives in its own namespace with
  # nothing else in it.
  - apiGroups: [""]
    resources: [secrets]
    verbs: [create, update, patch, delete]
{{- end }}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `chart/gonk/templates/rolebinding-gonk-meter.yaml`**

```yaml
{{- if .Values.meter.enabled }}
apiVersion: rbac.authorization.k8s.io/v1
kind: RoleBinding
metadata:
  name: gonk-meter
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: meter
roleRef:
  apiGroup: rbac.authorization.k8s.io
  kind: Role
  name: gonk-meter
subjects:
  - kind: ServiceAccount
    name: gonk-meter
    namespace: {{ .Release.Namespace }}
{{- end }}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Add the chart assertions** to `internal/charttest/secrets_test.go`

```go
// The KeySink's RBAC must be a NAMESPACED Role -- never a ClusterRole. A
// cluster-wide write grant on Secrets is a cluster-wide compromise.
func TestKeySinkRBACIsNamespaced(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, Minimum()...)
	MustObject(t, out, "ServiceAccount", "gonk-meter")
	MustObject(t, out, "Role", "gonk-meter")
	MustObject(t, out, "RoleBinding", "gonk-meter")
	for _, o := range Objects(t, out) {
		if o.Kind == "ClusterRole" || o.Kind == "ClusterRoleBinding" {
			t.Fatalf("the chart grants CLUSTER-wide RBAC (%s/%s)", o.Kind, o.Metadata.Name)
		}
	}
}

// The Role is inert if nothing tells meter WHERE to write. A chart that renders
// perfect RBAC and never sets GONK_KEYSINK_NAMESPACE ships a meter that falls
// back to the MEMORY sink -- provisioning virtual keys that no pod can read, and
// leaving every project in `key-missing` (P03 AD-1).
func TestMeterIsPointedAtAKeySinkNamespace(t *testing.T) {
	d := MustObject(t, Render(t, Minimum()...), "Deployment", "gonk-meter")
	if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, "GONK_KEYSINK_NAMESPACE") {
		t.Fatal("meter has RBAC to write key Secrets but no namespace to write them in")
	}
	if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, "gonk-key-") {
		t.Error("GONK_KEYSINK_PREFIX is not set; the Role's Secret names and the sink's would drift")
	}
	if strings.Contains(d.Doc, "automountServiceAccountToken: false") {
		t.Fatal("meter cannot build an in-cluster client without its ServiceAccount token")
	}
}
```

```bash
git commit -m "feat(chart): namespaced RBAC + ServiceAccount for gonk-meter's per-project key sink"
```

### Task 5: `gonk-intake` — Deployment, the public/private Service split, and the Ingress

Spec §9: **the webhook path is the only ingress.** Intake has two listeners for
exactly this reason [P02 §Task 10]: `:8080` serves `POST /hook/gitlab` and returns
404 for everything else; `:9090` serves `/healthz`, `/readyz`, `/metrics` and
**`POST /admin/reconcile`** — which is an unauthenticated trigger, and must never
be reachable from outside the cluster.

**Files:** Create `chart/gonk/templates/deployment-gonk-intake.yaml`,
`chart/gonk/templates/service-gonk-intake-public.yaml`,
`chart/gonk/templates/service-gonk-intake-internal.yaml`,
`chart/gonk/templates/ingress-gonk-intake.yaml`;
create `internal/charttest/listener_test.go`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `internal/charttest/listener_test.go`

```go
//go:build chart

package charttest

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

// The public Service exposes ONE port, and it is the hook listener.
func TestPublicServiceExposesOnlyTheHookPort(t *testing.T) {
	svc := MustObject(t, Render(t, Minimum()...), "Service", "gonk-intake")
	if strings.Contains(svc.Doc, "9090") {
		t.Fatalf("the PUBLIC service exposes the private listener:\n%s", svc.Doc)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(svc.Doc, "8080") {
		t.Fatalf("the public service does not expose the hook port:\n%s", svc.Doc)
	}
}

// G13: the private listener serves /metrics, /healthz and POST /admin/reconcile
// -- an UNAUTHENTICATED reconcile trigger. If the Ingress can reach it, anyone
// who can reach the ingress can drive gonk's reconcile loop.
func TestIngressNeverExposesThePrivateListener(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, append(Minimum(),
		"--set", "ingress.enabled=true",
		"--set", "ingress.className=nginx",
		"--set", "ingress.host=gonk.test")...)
	ing := MustObject(t, out, "Ingress", "gonk-intake")
	if strings.Contains(ing.Doc, "9090") || strings.Contains(ing.Doc, "gonk-intake-internal") {
		t.Fatalf("the Ingress can reach the private listener:\n%s", ing.Doc)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(ing.Doc, "/hook/gitlab") {
		t.Fatalf("the Ingress does not route the webhook:\n%s", ing.Doc)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(ing.Doc, "pathType: Exact") {
		t.Fatalf("the Ingress path is not Exact; a Prefix match on /hook/gitlab is wider than the one endpoint that exists:\n%s", ing.Doc)
	}
}

func TestNoIngressByDefault(t *testing.T) {
	NoObject(t, Render(t, Minimum()...), "Ingress", "gonk-intake")
}

// The internal Service is where Prometheus scrapes and where /readyz is checked.
func TestInternalServiceExposesOnlyThePrivatePort(t *testing.T) {
	svc := MustObject(t, Render(t, Minimum()...), "Service", "gonk-intake-internal")
	if strings.Contains(svc.Doc, "8080") {
		t.Fatalf("the internal service exposes the hook port too:\n%s", svc.Doc)
	}
}

// Intake's probes MUST live on the private listener: the public one 404s
// everything but the hook, so a probe against it would fail forever.
func TestIntakeProbesUseThePrivateListener(t *testing.T) {
	d := MustObject(t, Render(t, Minimum()...), "Deployment", "gonk-intake")
	for _, want := range []string{"path: /readyz", "path: /healthz", "port: http-private"} {
		if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, want) {
			t.Errorf("intake probes are missing %q", want)
		}
	}
}

func TestIntakeCarriesTheDispatchAndMeterWiring(t *testing.T) {
	d := MustObject(t, Render(t, Minimum()...), "Deployment", "gonk-intake")
	for _, want := range []string{
		"GONK_METER_URL", "http://gonk-meter:8080",
		"GONK_SUPERVISOR_URL",
		"GONK_BOT_USERNAME", "GONK_WEBHOOK_PUBLIC_URL", "GONK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN_GEN",
		"GONK_RECONCILE_INTERVAL",
	} {
		if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, want) {
			t.Errorf("intake deployment is missing %q", want)
		}
	}
}

// dispatch=log must leave GONK_SUPERVISOR_URL EMPTY -- that is how intake selects
// the LogDispatcher [P02 Task 10]. Setting it to "" explicitly, rather than
// omitting it, means a stale env from a previous release cannot resurrect the
// HTTP dispatcher.
func TestLogDispatchLeavesSupervisorURLEmpty(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, append(Minimum(), "--set", "gascity.dispatch=log")...)
	d := MustObject(t, out, "Deployment", "gonk-intake")
	if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, `value: ""`) {
		t.Fatalf("dispatch=log did not blank GONK_SUPERVISOR_URL:\n%s", d.Doc)
	}
}

// A private GitLab CA must reach the process, or every GitLab call fails TLS.
func TestGitLabCAIsMountedWhenConfigured(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, append(Minimum(), "--set", "gitlab.caCert.existingConfigMap=gitlab-ca")...)
	d := MustObject(t, out, "Deployment", "gonk-intake")
	for _, want := range []string{"SSL_CERT_FILE", "gitlab-ca"} {
		if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, want) {
			t.Errorf("the GitLab CA is not wired: missing %q", want)
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run and watch fail** — `go test -tags chart ./internal/charttest/ -run 'Listener|Ingress|Service|Intake' -v`

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `chart/gonk/templates/deployment-gonk-intake.yaml`**

```yaml
{{- if .Values.intake.enabled }}
{{- $root := .Values.secrets.mountRoot }}
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: gonk-intake
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: intake
spec:
  replicas: {{ .Values.intake.replicaCount }}
  # Intake IS safe to roll and to scale: it holds no persistent state, its project
  # cache is derived from GitLab on every pass, and its writes (the onboarding MR,
  # the hook) are idempotent. The controller dedupes orders on BeadAnchor.
  strategy:
    type: RollingUpdate
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      {{- include "gonk.selectorLabels" (dict "ctx" . "component" "intake") | nindent 6 }}
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 8 }}
        app.kubernetes.io/component: intake
      {{- with .Values.intake.podAnnotations }}
      annotations:
        {{- toYaml . | nindent 8 }}
      {{- end }}
    spec:
      securityContext:
        {{- toYaml .Values.podSecurityContext | nindent 8 }}
      {{- with .Values.image.pullSecrets }}
      imagePullSecrets:
        {{- toYaml . | nindent 8 }}
      {{- end }}
      containers:
        - name: intake
          image: {{ include "gonk.image" (dict "ctx" . "image" .Values.intake.image) }}
          imagePullPolicy: {{ .Values.image.pullPolicy }}
          env:
            - name: GONK_GITLAB_URL
              value: {{ .Values.gitlab.url | quote }}
            - name: GONK_BOT_USERNAME
              value: {{ .Values.gitlab.botUsername | quote }}
            # *** PATHS, NOT VALUES. ***
            - name: GONK_GITLAB_TOKEN_FILE
              value: {{ include "gonk.secretPath" (dict "root" $root "name" "gitlab" "key" .Values.secrets.gitlab.keys.token) | quote }}
            {{- if .Values.secrets.rotation.gitlabPrevious }}
            # ROTATION SLOT 2 for a credential intake PRESENTS (P02 AD-4b). Not the
            # same mechanism as the webhook token's slot 2: nobody on gonk's side
            # verifies a PAT, so "accept either" is meaningless. Intake presents
            # slot 1 and, on a 401/403 from GitLab, retries ONCE with slot 2 --
            # turning the mid-rotation window from an outage into the counter
            # gonk_intake_gitlab_auth_fallback_total (alert on it: nonzero means a
            # rotation is half-finished).
            - name: GONK_GITLAB_TOKEN_PREVIOUS_FILE
              value: {{ include "gonk.secretPath" (dict "root" $root "name" "gitlab" "key" .Values.secrets.gitlab.keys.tokenPrevious) | quote }}
            {{- end }}
            {{- if eq .Values.gitlab.mode "split-credential" }}
            # Used ONLY for /hooks calls (spec 5.1). Unset -> the bot token does
            # everything, which is the homelab default. NO second slot: a failed
            # hook repair is simply repaired on the next reconcile pass.
            - name: GONK_GITLAB_ADMIN_TOKEN_FILE
              value: {{ include "gonk.secretPath" (dict "root" $root "name" "gitlab" "key" .Values.secrets.gitlab.keys.adminToken) | quote }}
            {{- end }}
            # THE PRIVATE CA. trust-manager publishes `trust-bundle` into every
            # namespace; Go's crypto/x509 reads SSL_CERT_FILE with no code change.
            # *** THIS IS HOW WE TRUST https://gitlab.orac.local. ***
            # There is no value that disables TLS verification, and there never
            # will be: the component on the trust boundary must not be able to talk
            # to anything that answers on 443.
            - name: SSL_CERT_FILE
              value: {{ .Values.gitlab.caCert.mountPath | quote }}
            - name: GONK_WEBHOOK_SECRET_FILE
              value: {{ include "gonk.secretPath" (dict "root" $root "name" "webhook" "key" .Values.secrets.webhook.keys.token) | quote }}
            {{- if .Values.secrets.rotation.webhookPrevious }}
            - name: GONK_WEBHOOK_SECRET_PREVIOUS_FILE
              value: {{ include "gonk.secretPath" (dict "root" $root "name" "webhook" "key" .Values.secrets.webhook.keys.tokenPrevious) | quote }}
            {{- end }}
            - name: GONK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN_GEN
              value: {{ .Values.intake.webhookTokenGen | quote }}
            - name: GONK_WEBHOOK_PUBLIC_URL
              value: {{ .Values.intake.webhookPublicURL | quote }}
            - name: GONK_WEBHOOK_SSL_VERIFY
              value: {{ .Values.intake.webhookSSLVerify | quote }}
            - name: GONK_METER_URL
              value: "http://gonk-meter:{{ .Values.meter.port }}"
            - name: GONK_METER_TOKEN_FILE
              value: {{ include "gonk.secretPath" (dict "root" $root "name" "meter-api" "key" .Values.secrets.meterAPI.keys.token) | quote }}
            # Empty -> the LogDispatcher: orders are LOGGED and never fired. It is
            # set explicitly (not omitted) so a stale env cannot resurrect the HTTP
            # dispatcher on a rolling update. When dispatch=http the URL is DERIVED
            # from the in-chart controller Service (gascity.enabled) or from
            # gascity.supervisorURL (BYO controller) -- see `gonk.supervisorURL`.
            - name: GONK_SUPERVISOR_URL
              value: {{ if eq .Values.gascity.dispatch "log" }}""{{ else }}{{ include "gonk.supervisorURL" . | quote }}{{ end }}
            - name: GONK_RECONCILE_INTERVAL
              value: {{ .Values.intake.reconcileInterval | quote }}
            - name: GONK_LISTEN_ADDR
              value: ":{{ .Values.intake.ports.public }}"
            - name: GONK_PRIVATE_ADDR
              value: ":{{ .Values.intake.ports.private }}"
            - name: GONK_VERSION
              value: {{ .Chart.AppVersion | quote }}
            {{- if .Values.gitlab.caCert.existingConfigMap }}
            - name: SSL_CERT_FILE
              value: /etc/ssl/gonk/{{ .Values.gitlab.caCert.key }}
            {{- end }}
            {{- if .Values.monitoring.otlpEndpoint }}
            - name: OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT
              value: {{ .Values.monitoring.otlpEndpoint | quote }}
            {{- end }}
          ports:
            # The PUBLIC listener: POST /hook/gitlab, and 404 for everything else.
            - name: http-hook
              containerPort: {{ .Values.intake.ports.public }}
            # The PRIVATE listener: /healthz, /readyz, /metrics, POST /admin/reconcile.
            # NEVER INGRESSED. /admin/reconcile is unauthenticated.
            - name: http-private
              containerPort: {{ .Values.intake.ports.private }}
          livenessProbe:
            httpGet: {path: /healthz, port: http-private}
            initialDelaySeconds: 5
            periodSeconds: 20
          # /readyz is true only when the bot identity resolved, the first
          # reconcile completed, AND gonk-meter's /healthz is OK. So a meter
          # outage takes intake OUT of the Service -- the webhook 502s and GitLab
          # retries. That is fail-closed (no unmetered dispatch), but GitLab
          # auto-disables hooks that fail repeatedly: Plan 06 must test the
          # recovery path.
          readinessProbe:
            httpGet: {path: /readyz, port: http-private}
            initialDelaySeconds: 5
            periodSeconds: 10
            failureThreshold: 3
          resources:
            {{- toYaml .Values.intake.resources | nindent 12 }}
          securityContext:
            {{- toYaml .Values.containerSecurityContext | nindent 12 }}
          volumeMounts:
            {{- include "gonk.secretMount" (dict "root" $root "name" "gitlab") | nindent 12 }}
            {{- include "gonk.secretMount" (dict "root" $root "name" "webhook") | nindent 12 }}
            {{- include "gonk.secretMount" (dict "root" $root "name" "meter-api") | nindent 12 }}
            {{- if .Values.gitlab.caCert.existingConfigMap }}
            # The private-CA bundle, mounted at exactly the path SSL_CERT_FILE names.
            # subPath so the file lands at /etc/ssl/orac/ca.crt and not in a dir.
            - name: orac-ca
              mountPath: {{ .Values.gitlab.caCert.mountPath }}
              subPath: {{ .Values.gitlab.caCert.key }}
              readOnly: true
            {{- end }}
            - name: tmp
              mountPath: /tmp
      volumes:
        {{- $glKeys := list .Values.secrets.gitlab.keys.token }}
        {{- if .Values.secrets.rotation.gitlabPrevious }}{{- $glKeys = append $glKeys .Values.secrets.gitlab.keys.tokenPrevious }}{{- end }}
        {{- if eq .Values.gitlab.mode "split-credential" }}{{- $glKeys = append $glKeys .Values.secrets.gitlab.keys.adminToken }}{{- end }}
        {{- include "gonk.secretVolume" (dict "name" "gitlab" "secret" .Values.secrets.gitlab.existingSecret "keys" $glKeys) | nindent 8 }}
        {{- $whKeys := list .Values.secrets.webhook.keys.token }}
        {{- if .Values.secrets.rotation.webhookPrevious }}{{- $whKeys = append $whKeys .Values.secrets.webhook.keys.tokenPrevious }}{{- end }}
        {{- include "gonk.secretVolume" (dict "name" "webhook" "secret" .Values.secrets.webhook.existingSecret "keys" $whKeys) | nindent 8 }}
        {{- include "gonk.secretVolume" (dict "name" "meter-api" "secret" .Values.secrets.meterAPI.existingSecret "keys" (list .Values.secrets.meterAPI.keys.token)) | nindent 8 }}
        {{- if .Values.gitlab.caCert.existingConfigMap }}
        # trust-manager publishes `trust-bundle` (key tls-ca-bundle.pem) into EVERY
        # namespace, so this ConfigMap is a NAME, not something you have to create.
        # It is how a Go service trusts https://gitlab.orac.local's private CA --
        # and it is the reason no code in this repo ever sets InsecureSkipVerify.
        - name: orac-ca
          configMap:
            name: {{ .Values.gitlab.caCert.existingConfigMap }}
            items:
              - key: {{ .Values.gitlab.caCert.key }}
                path: {{ .Values.gitlab.caCert.key }}
        {{- end }}
        - name: tmp
          emptyDir: {}
      {{- with .Values.intake.nodeSelector }}
      nodeSelector: {{- toYaml . | nindent 8 }}
      {{- end }}
      {{- with .Values.intake.tolerations }}
      tolerations: {{- toYaml . | nindent 8 }}
      {{- end }}
      {{- with .Values.intake.affinity }}
      affinity: {{- toYaml . | nindent 8 }}
      {{- end }}
{{- end }}
```

> **Note:** intake presents rotation slot **1** of the meter bearer token; it does
> not need slot 2 (meter is the verifier and accepts either). During a meter-token
> rotation: write the new token to meter's slot 2, roll meter, then move it to
> slot 1 everywhere, roll both.

### Task 6: The Dolt component (beads **only**) and the CNPG ledger (Postgres — settled, not a switch)

> **RECONCILED, Task 0 — this task's title and framing changed.** The original
> title was "The Dolt component (beads **and** ledger) and the ledger backend
> switch," written when Task 0b (Plan 03) had not yet run. **It has run, and
> Dolt failed decisively**: 32 concurrent writers raced a ceiling with headroom
> for 2, at three escalating isolation strategies (plain `BEGIN`,
> `SERIALIZABLE`, and `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` — the exact shape
> `ReserveIfFits` uses), and **every one of 90 independent iterations
> overspent 12.8× with zero errors** — `docs/spikes/dolt-reservation-isolation.md`.
> The owner settled the ledger on **Postgres, on the existing CNPG cluster**
> (ADR-004 Decision 10), and the merged code reflects it:
> `cmd/gonk-meter/main.go`'s `openStore` has exactly one case,
> `"postgres"` — there is no `store.OpenDolt`, and `internal/meter/store/`
> ships only `postgres.go` (plus `memory.go`, test-only). **There is no ledger
> backend "switch" any more, and Task 6 is no longer gated on an unresolved
> spike.**

**Dolt is a single-purpose component now: it backs the Gas City beads store,
and nothing else.** The bundled `dolt sql-server` (`dolt.enabled`, default
`true`) is a StatefulSet for **beads only**. `dolt.enabled: false` points beads
at an external Dolt (`dolt.external.host`, e.g. a self-hosted DoltLab) —
**beads is Dolt-only, there is no Postgres path for it** — and this toggle has
**no effect on the ledger whatsoever.**

**The ledger is Postgres/CNPG, full stop, in one of three modes**
(`ledger.postgres.mode`): `shared` (tenancy on the existing
`databases-app/postgres` CNPG cluster — **the real target-cluster default**,
per `docs/environment.md`), `cnpg` (a chart-managed `Cluster` CR, for a
stranger installing this chart with no existing CNPG tenancy), or `external`
(a BYO DSN, no chart-rendered resource). **None of the three modes derive a
DSN automatically** the way the bundled Dolt used to — even `mode: cnpg`'s
generated app Secret must be pointed at via `secrets.ledger.existingSecret` by
hand (G19, reconciled above).

**Files:** Create `chart/gonk/templates/statefulset-gonk-dolt.yaml`,
`chart/gonk/templates/service-gonk-dolt.yaml`,
`chart/gonk/templates/cluster-gonk-ledger-postgres.yaml`;
create `internal/charttest/ledger_test.go`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `internal/charttest/ledger_test.go`

```go
//go:build chart

package charttest

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

// The DEFAULT: dolt.enabled=true backs BEADS ONLY. ledger.backend=postgres,
// ledger.postgres.mode=shared is the default (RECONCILED, Task 0 -- no
// "ledger.backend=dolt" state exists any more), which renders NO Cluster CR
// (see TestSharedPostgresRendersNoDatabase in Step 5b). Minimum() already sets
// dolt.image.tag.
func TestBundledDoltRendersAStatefulSetForBeadsOnly(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, Minimum()...)
	ss := MustObject(t, out, "StatefulSet", "gonk-dolt")
	if !strings.Contains(ss.Doc, "volumeClaimTemplates") {
		t.Fatal("the bundled Dolt has no persistent volume; a beads store that loses state on restart is fail-open on Gas City state")
	}
	MustObject(t, out, "Service", "gonk-dolt")
	d := MustObject(t, out, "Deployment", "gonk-meter")
	if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, `GONK_METER_STORE_BACKEND`) || !strings.Contains(d.Doc, `value: "postgres"`) {
		t.Fatalf("meter was not told to use postgres (the only valid backend):\n%s", d.Doc)
	}
}

// dolt.enabled=false: NO StatefulSet, and the CONTROLLER (beads) points at the
// external host. Beads has no Postgres path, so external Dolt is the only
// alternative -- and, RECONCILED (Task 0), this has NO EFFECT on the ledger,
// which is Postgres regardless.
func TestExternalDoltRendersNoStatefulSetAndDoesNotTouchLedger(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, append(Minimum(),
		"--set", "dolt.enabled=false",
		"--set", "dolt.external.host=dolt.databases.svc")...)
	NoObject(t, out, "StatefulSet", "gonk-dolt")
	d := MustObject(t, out, "Deployment", "gonk-meter")
	if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, `value: "postgres"`) {
		t.Fatal("disabling the bundled Dolt (a BEADS-only toggle) must not change meter's ledger backend")
	}
}

// The Task 0b fallback is now simply "how it works." mode=cnpg renders a
// chart-managed Cluster CR, and -- RECONCILED, Task 0 -- the bundled Dolt
// StatefulSet still renders alongside it (beads is a completely separate
// component; there is no scenario where enabling the CNPG Cluster mode
// removes it).
func TestCNPGLedgerRendersAClusterAlongsideBeadsDolt(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, append(Minimum(),
		"--set", "ledger.postgres.mode=cnpg")...)
	c := MustObject(t, out, "Cluster", "gonk-ledger-postgres")
	if c.APIVersion != "postgresql.cnpg.io/v1" {
		t.Fatalf("apiVersion = %q", c.APIVersion)
	}
	MustObject(t, out, "StatefulSet", "gonk-dolt")
	d := MustObject(t, out, "Deployment", "gonk-meter")
	if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, `value: "postgres"`) {
		t.Fatalf("meter is not pointed at the postgres backend:\n%s", d.Doc)
	}
}

func TestExternalPostgresRendersNoCluster(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, append(Minimum(),
		"--set", "ledger.postgres.mode=external")...)
	NoObject(t, out, "Cluster", "gonk-ledger-postgres")
	// Beads Dolt still there -- an entirely separate component.
	MustObject(t, out, "StatefulSet", "gonk-dolt")
}
```

**Deleted, not carried forward (Task 0 reconciliation):**
`TestPostgresDoesNotUnlockMeterReplicas` asserted that setting
`ledger.backend=postgres` + `meter.replicaCount=2` must fail render. **This
premise is now false.** ADR-004 records that Postgres enforces both
money-safety properties (`ReserveIfFits`'s overspend guard and `/decide`
idempotency) in the database, across replicas — proven by
`TestReserveIfFitsRace` / `TestReserveIsIdempotentAcrossReplicas`
(`internal/meter/store/postgres_race_test.go`). Multiple meter replicas are
safe with **any** `ledger.postgres.mode`; see
`TestMeterMultipleReplicasRendersWithNoGuard` (Task 2) instead.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run and watch fail.**

- [ ] **Step 3: `chart/gonk/templates/statefulset-gonk-dolt.yaml`**

**One Dolt, one consumer (RECONCILED, Task 0: this used to say "two
consumers" — it is one, now).** This StatefulSet is gated on `dolt.enabled`
alone and backs the Gas City **beads store only**; it has no relationship to
`ledger.backend` (there is only one value, `postgres`) or `ledger.postgres.mode`
at all. `dolt.enabled: false` renders nothing and beads uses
`dolt.external.host` (G16).

```yaml
{{- if .Values.dolt.enabled }}
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: StatefulSet
metadata:
  name: gonk-dolt
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: dolt
spec:
  # ONE replica, ALWAYS. dolt sql-server is not clustered here; the Gas City
  # beads store needs ONE writer with real transactions. RECONCILED, Task 0:
  # this server hosts the beads database ONLY -- gonk-meter's reservation path
  # (the ledger) is Postgres/CNPG, never a database on this server (ADR-004
  # Decision 10; docs/spikes/dolt-reservation-isolation.md).
  replicas: 1
  serviceName: gonk-dolt
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      {{- include "gonk.selectorLabels" (dict "ctx" . "component" "dolt") | nindent 6 }}
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 8 }}
        app.kubernetes.io/component: dolt
    spec:
      securityContext:
        {{- toYaml .Values.podSecurityContext | nindent 8 }}
      {{- with .Values.image.pullSecrets }}
      imagePullSecrets:
        {{- toYaml . | nindent 8 }}
      {{- end }}
      containers:
        - name: dolt
          image: {{ include "gonk.image" (dict "ctx" . "image" .Values.dolt.image) }}
          imagePullPolicy: {{ .Values.image.pullPolicy }}
          # Auth is root / empty password / --no-tls, host+port only. There is no
          # credential to configure, so the chart does not pretend to (env doc).
          args:
            - sql-server
            - --host=0.0.0.0
            - --port={{ .Values.dolt.port }}
            - --no-tls
            - --data-dir=/var/lib/dolt
          ports:
            - name: mysql
              containerPort: {{ .Values.dolt.port }}
          # A TCP probe only: authenticating a real query would need a client, and
          # this server has no credential to authenticate anyway.
          livenessProbe:
            tcpSocket: {port: mysql}
            initialDelaySeconds: 15
            periodSeconds: 20
          readinessProbe:
            tcpSocket: {port: mysql}
            initialDelaySeconds: 5
            periodSeconds: 5
          resources:
            {{- toYaml .Values.dolt.resources | nindent 12 }}
          securityContext:
            {{- toYaml (omit .Values.containerSecurityContext "readOnlyRootFilesystem") | nindent 12 }}
            readOnlyRootFilesystem: false
          volumeMounts:
            - name: data
              mountPath: /var/lib/dolt
  volumeClaimTemplates:
    - metadata:
        name: data
      spec:
        accessModes: [{{ .Values.dolt.persistence.accessMode }}]
        {{- if .Values.dolt.persistence.storageClass }}
        storageClassName: {{ .Values.dolt.persistence.storageClass }}
        {{- end }}
        resources:
          requests:
            storage: {{ .Values.dolt.persistence.size }}
{{- end }}
```

> **Handed to Plan 06 (RECONCILED, Task 0: Dolt is tier-0 state for BEADS
> ONLY now — it was "both beads and the ledger" before Task 0b's spike ran).**
> Spec §4.2 wants a PDB and **scheduled `dolt push` backups to a remote** for
> the beads store — neither is in this chart, and neither can be tested
> without a cluster. The **ledger** (Postgres/CNPG) needs its own tier-0
> treatment instead: `mode: shared`/`cnpg` inherit whatever backup posture the
> CNPG cluster already has; confirm it covers `gonk_ledger`. Also **neither
> schema is ever created by the chart**: meter must migrate the ledger
> (Postgres) on startup and Gas City initialises beads (Dolt) via the sentinel
> handshake, or the first `ReserveIfFits` / first session fails. Confirm both
> in Task 0 / Task 0.5.

```yaml
{{- if .Values.dolt.enabled }}

  name: gonk-dolt
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: dolt
spec:
  # Headless: a StatefulSet's stable network identity (gonk-dolt-0.gonk-dolt).
  clusterIP: None
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `chart/gonk/templates/cluster-gonk-ledger-postgres.yaml`**

```yaml
{{- if and (eq .Values.ledger.backend "postgres") (eq .Values.ledger.postgres.mode "cnpg") }}
{{- /* .Values.ledger.backend is a single-value enum ("postgres" only --
       RECONCILED, Task 0), so the first half of this `and` is always true
       today. It stays in the condition defensively, in case a future backend
       is ever added, and because it costs nothing. */ -}}
# RECONCILED, Task 0: this is no longer "the Task 0b fallback" -- it is THE
# settled ledger backend (ADR-004 Decision 10). Task 0b's spike measured Dolt
# failing the reservation-race isolation test decisively (32/32 concurrent
# writers won a ceiling-of-2 race, 12.8x overspend, at every isolation level
# including SELECT ... FOR UPDATE -- docs/spikes/dolt-reservation-isolation.md).
# Postgres on the CNPG cluster the owner ALREADY RUNS is the answer, not a
# contingency. Not a new stateful dependency -- CNPG's operator is already
# there, and this chart does not install it.
#
# Why Postgres, not Dolt: SERIALIZABLE / SELECT ... FOR UPDATE is well-trodden
# for exactly the concurrent-reservation race on Postgres (verified: it blocks,
# where Dolt's identical-shaped lock was measured NOT to). A budget ceiling
# defended by an unproven isolation guarantee is not defended. The cost is
# Dolt's versioned audit history, which the append-only ledger tables mitigate.
apiVersion: postgresql.cnpg.io/v1
kind: Cluster
metadata:
  name: gonk-ledger-postgres
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: ledger
spec:
  instances: {{ .Values.ledger.postgres.cnpg.instances }}
  {{- if .Values.ledger.postgres.cnpg.imageName }}
  imageName: {{ .Values.ledger.postgres.cnpg.imageName }}
  {{- end }}
  bootstrap:
    initdb:
      database: {{ .Values.ledger.postgres.database }}
      owner: gonk
  storage:
    size: {{ .Values.ledger.postgres.cnpg.storage.size }}
    {{- if .Values.ledger.postgres.cnpg.storage.storageClass }}
    storageClass: {{ .Values.ledger.postgres.cnpg.storage.storageClass }}
    {{- end }}
# NOTE: CNPG generates its own app-user Secret (gonk-ledger-postgres-app). meter
# still reads its DSN from secrets.ledger.existingSecret -- point that at CNPG's
# generated Secret (with a `dsn`-shaped key), or synthesize the DSN into your own
# Secret via ExternalSecret. The chart does NOT copy CNPG's secret, because copying
# a credential from one Secret into another is exactly the kind of thing that ends
# up in a git repo.
{{- end }}
```

- [ ] **Step 5b: `postgres.mode: shared` — tenancy on the CNPG cluster the owner
      ALREADY RUNS. THIS IS THE DEFAULT, AND IT NEEDS FOUR OBJECTS, NOT ONE.**

`docs/environment.md` is explicit: the owner-approved fallback is **Postgres on
the existing CNPG cluster** — `databases-app/postgres`, healthy, 2 instances —
**not a new one**. Standing up a second CNPG `Cluster` (Step 5) is a legitimate
mode for a stranger installing this chart, but it is **not** what happens on the
target cluster, and a plan that only ships Step 5 would have the operator deploy a
redundant database.

Add a third mode:

```yaml
ledger:
  postgres:
    mode: shared          # shared | cnpg | external   (DEFAULT: shared)
    database: gonk_ledger
    shared:
      # The EXISTING CNPG cluster. The chart RENDERS NOTHING for it -- these
      # objects live in the gitops repo, in a DIFFERENT NAMESPACE, and are owned
      # by Flux. All the chart does is consume the DSN. See README.
      clusterName: postgres
      clusterNamespace: databases-app
      roleName: gonk
```

**The four objects, and why it is four.** This is the single most commonly
got-wrong thing in the gitops repo, and getting it wrong fails in a way that looks
like a network problem. `apps/nagus/` is the worked example — **copy it.**

| # | Object | Where it lives | Why it cannot be anywhere else |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | A **managed role** `gonk` on the shared `Cluster` (`spec.managed.roles`) | **gitops**, patching `databases-app/postgres` | CNPG creates DB roles declaratively. A hand-made role drifts away on the next reconcile. |
| 2 | An **ExternalSecret** for that role's password, **in `databases-app`** | **gitops**, `databases-app` | The `Cluster`'s `passwordSecret` must be readable **by CNPG**, which reads it in the cluster's own namespace. |
| 3 | A **`Database` CR** (`postgresql.cnpg.io/v1`) naming `gonk_ledger` and owner `gonk` | **gitops**, `databases-app` | This is how a database gets created on a shared CNPG cluster. `bootstrap.initdb` (Step 5) applies to a **new** cluster only — it will not create a database on an existing one, and reaching for it here is the classic mistake. |
| 4 | A **SECOND ExternalSecret**, **in the `gonk` namespace**, producing the `gonk-ledger` Secret with a `dsn` key | **gitops**, `clusters/orac/apps/gonk/` | **ESO cannot replicate a Secret across namespaces.** The same Vault value must be materialised twice, in two namespaces, by two ExternalSecrets. There is no `copy` — and this is exactly the step people skip, after which meter's pod cannot start and the error looks like DNS. |

**And the gotcha that has already bitten this cluster once: write the Vault value
BEFORE merging the ExternalSecret.** A missing key leaves the ExternalSecret
NotReady and can **wedge the whole Flux reconcile** — not just gonk's.

The DSN (object 4) is what `GONK_METER_STORE_DSN_FILE` points at, and it is
`postgres://gonk:<pw>@postgres-rw.databases-app.svc.cluster.local:5432/gonk_ledger`.
Note `-rw`: CNPG publishes `<cluster>-rw`, `-ro` and `-r` Services, and **the
ledger must be on `-rw`.** A reservation written to a read-only replica is a
reservation that does not exist, and a reservation that does not exist is a budget
that can be spent twice.

Chart-side, `mode: shared` renders **no `Cluster`, no `Database`, no `Secret`** —
it renders the meter env pointing at `secrets.ledger.existingSecret`, exactly like
`external`. Its whole value is that the **schema and the README name the four
objects**, so nobody ships three of them.

```go
// mode: shared must add NOTHING to the cluster. If it renders a Cluster CR, it is
// about to stand up a SECOND Postgres next to the one it was told to share.
func TestSharedPostgresRendersNoDatabase(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, append(Minimum(),
		"--set", "ledger.backend=postgres",
		"--set", "ledger.postgres.mode=shared")...)
	for _, o := range Objects(t, out) {
		if o.Kind == "Cluster" || o.Kind == "Database" {
			t.Fatalf("mode=shared rendered a %s/%s; it must consume the existing cluster, not build one",
				o.Kind, o.Metadata.Name)
		}
	}
	d := MustObject(t, out, "Deployment", "gonk-meter")
	if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, `GONK_METER_STORE_BACKEND`) || !strings.Contains(d.Doc, `value: "postgres"`) {
		t.Fatal("meter is not pointed at the postgres backend")
	}
}
```

> defaults are provisional; the golden manifests for any controller-bearing profile

```go
//go:build chart

package charttest

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

// The bundled controller renders a single-replica workload, a Service on the
// (provisional) supervisor port, and NAMESPACED RBAC. No leader election exists,
// so there must be exactly one.
func TestControllerRendersWorkloadServiceAndRBAC(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, Minimum()...)
	// Default workload is a Deployment (provisional; Task 0.5 may change the default).
	d := MustObject(t, out, "Deployment", "gonk-controller")
	if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, "replicas: 1") {
		t.Error("the controller is not single-replica; with no leader election, two reconcile loops corrupt beads")
	}
	if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, "gc start --foreground /city") && !strings.Contains(d.Doc, "--foreground") {
		t.Errorf("the controller does not run `gc start --foreground /city`:\n%s", d.Doc)
	}
	svc := MustObject(t, out, "Service", "gonk-controller")
	if !strings.Contains(svc.Doc, "8372") { // provisional default; smoke-gated
		t.Errorf("the supervisor Service is not on the (provisional) port:\n%s", svc.Doc)
	}
	MustObject(t, out, "ServiceAccount", "gc-controller")
	MustObject(t, out, "ServiceAccount", "gc-agent")
	role := MustObject(t, out, "Role", "gc-controller")
	for _, want := range []string{"pods/exec", "pods/log", "configmaps"} {
		if !strings.Contains(role.Doc, want) {
			t.Errorf("gc-controller Role is missing %q (from docs/environment.md)", want)
		}
	}
}

// The controller RBAC is NAMESPACED. A ClusterRole would let a compromised
// controller reach every namespace -- exactly what the blast radius must not be.
func TestControllerRBACIsNamespacedNeverCluster(t *testing.T) {
	for _, o := range Objects(t, Render(t, Minimum()...)) {
		if o.Kind == "ClusterRole" || o.Kind == "ClusterRoleBinding" {
			t.Fatalf("the chart rendered a %s/%s; controller RBAC must be namespaced", o.Kind, o.Metadata.Name)
		}
	}
}

// gascity.enabled=false: BYO controller. NOTHING controller-shaped renders, and
// the supervisorURL is required (asserted by the guard test elsewhere).
func TestBYOGasCityRendersNoController(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, append(Minimum(),
		"--set", "gascity.enabled=false",
		"--set", "gascity.supervisorURL=http://gc.external.svc:8372")...)
	for _, kind := range []string{"Deployment", "StatefulSet", "Pod"} {
		NoObject(t, out, kind, "gonk-controller")
	}
	NoObject(t, out, "ServiceAccount", "gc-controller")
	// intake still learns where to dispatch.
	d := MustObject(t, out, "Deployment", "gonk-intake")
	if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, "gc.external.svc") {
		t.Errorf("intake was not pointed at the external supervisor:\n%s", d.Doc)
	}
}

// delivery=configmap mounts the city; delivery=prebaked does not (the image carries
// it). BOTH are smoke-gated (Task 0.5): if the ConfigMap path fails the sentinel
// handshake, prebaked is the only option and this test's configmap case is removed.
func TestPackDeliverySwitch(t *testing.T) {
	prebaked := Render(t, append(Minimum(), "--set", "gascity.delivery=prebaked")...)
	NoObject(t, prebaked, "ConfigMap", "gonk-city")
	cm := Render(t, append(Minimum(), "--set", "gascity.delivery=configmap")...)
	MustObject(t, cm, "ConfigMap", "gonk-city")
	d := MustObject(t, cm, "Deployment", "gonk-controller")
	if !strings.Contains(d.Doc, "/city") {
		t.Errorf("configmap delivery did not mount the city at /city:\n%s", d.Doc)
	}
}

// The workload type is smoke-gated. When the smoke deploy says the controller needs
// a StatefulSet or a bare Pod, the operator sets gascity.workload and the chart
// obeys -- there is exactly one of whatever it is.
func TestWorkloadTypeIsSelectable(t *testing.T) {
	ss := Render(t, append(Minimum(), "--set", "gascity.workload=statefulset")...)
	MustObject(t, ss, "StatefulSet", "gonk-controller")
	NoObject(t, ss, "Deployment", "gonk-controller")
	pod := Render(t, append(Minimum(), "--set", "gascity.workload=pod")...)
	p := MustObject(t, pod, "Pod", "gonk-controller")
	if !strings.Contains(p.Doc, "restartPolicy: Never") {
		t.Errorf("the bare-Pod workload does not match upstream's restartPolicy: Never:\n%s", p.Doc)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run and watch fail.**

- [ ] **Step 3: The RBAC** — `serviceaccount-gc-controller.yaml`, `role-gc-controller.yaml`,
`rolebinding-gc-controller.yaml`, and the three `gc-agent` peers.

Transcribe the Role **exactly** from `docs/environment.md` (it read it off Gas
City's source). All namespaced; the controller's k8s session provider creates/execs/
logs agent pods and reads/writes ConfigMaps.

```yaml
# role-gc-controller.yaml
{{- if .Values.gascity.enabled }}
apiVersion: rbac.authorization.k8s.io/v1
kind: Role
metadata:
  name: gc-controller
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: controller
rules:
  - apiGroups: [""]
    resources: [pods]
    verbs: [get, list, watch, create, update, patch, delete]
  - apiGroups: [""]
    resources: [pods/exec]
    verbs: [create]
  - apiGroups: [""]
    resources: [pods/log]
    verbs: [get]
  - apiGroups: [""]
    resources: [configmaps]
    verbs: [get, list, watch, create, update, patch, delete]
{{- end }}
```

The `gc-agent` Role is `pods: [get]` only. Both `RoleBinding`s bind their SA to
their Role in `.Release.Namespace`. **If either is a `ClusterRole`, stop** — the
test above fails, and a compromised controller must not reach another namespace.

- [ ] **Step 4: The pack/city delivery** — `configmap-gonk-city.yaml`

```yaml
{{- if and .Values.gascity.enabled (eq .Values.gascity.delivery "configmap") }}
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: gonk-city
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: controller
data:
  # SMOKE-GATED (Task 0.5): whether mounting this fires the gc init -> .gc-start
  # sentinel handshake. If it does not, delete this file and bake the pack into the
  # gonk-controller image (delivery=prebaked is then the only option).
  #
  # The [session]/[beads]/[dolt] stanzas are rendered so beads points at the Dolt
  # component and sessions use the k8s provider; auth is root/empty/--no-tls.
  city.toml: |
    [workspace]
    [session]
    provider = "k8s"
    [session.k8s]
    agent_image = {{ include "gonk.image" (dict "ctx" . "image" .Values.componentImages.agent) | quote }}
    agent_service_account = "gc-agent"
    [beads]
    backend = "dolt"
    [dolt]
    host = {{ include "gonk.doltHost" . | quote }}
    port = {{ include "gonk.doltPort" . }}
    [daemon]
    supervisor_bind = "{{ .Values.gascity.supervisor.bind }}:{{ .Values.gascity.supervisorPort }}"
    supervisor_allow_mutations = {{ .Values.gascity.supervisor.allowMutations }}
    {{- with .Values.gascity.city.tomlOverride }}
    {{ . | nindent 4 }}
    {{- end }}
{{- end }}
```

`gonk.doltHost` / `gonk.doltPort` are helpers (add to `_helpers.tpl`): the in-chart
`gonk-dolt` headless Service when `dolt.enabled`, else `dolt.external.host/port`.

- [ ] **Step 5: The workload** — `workload-gonk-controller.yaml`

One file, three shapes, chosen by `gascity.workload` (SMOKE-GATED). All
single-instance. `bind=0.0.0.0` + `allow_mutations=true` via `GC_*` env or the city
toml. Health probes wire the three unauthenticated endpoints
(`/health`, `/v0/readiness`, `/v0/provider-readiness`). When `delivery=prebaked` the
pack is in the image and `/city` is not mounted; when `configmap`, mount `gonk-city`
at `/city`.

```yaml
{{- if .Values.gascity.enabled }}
{{- $kind := "Deployment" }}
{{- if eq .Values.gascity.workload "statefulset" }}{{ $kind = "StatefulSet" }}{{ end }}
{{- if eq .Values.gascity.workload "pod" }}{{ $kind = "Pod" }}{{ end }}
apiVersion: {{ if eq $kind "Pod" }}v1{{ else }}apps/v1{{ end }}
kind: {{ $kind }}
metadata:
  name: gonk-controller
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: controller
spec:
  {{- if ne $kind "Pod" }}
  replicas: 1                     # EXACTLY ONE. No leader election (reconcile loop).
  {{- if eq $kind "StatefulSet" }}
  serviceName: gonk-controller
  {{- end }}
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      {{- include "gonk.selectorLabels" (dict "ctx" . "component" "controller") | nindent 6 }}
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 8 }}
        app.kubernetes.io/component: controller
    spec:
  {{- end }}
  {{- if eq $kind "Pod" }}
      # upstream runs the controller Pod with restartPolicy: Never.
  restartPolicy: Never
  {{- end }}
      serviceAccountName: gc-controller
      {{- with .Values.image.pullSecrets }}
      imagePullSecrets:
        {{- toYaml . | nindent 8 }}
      {{- end }}
      containers:
        - name: controller
          image: {{ include "gonk.image" (dict "ctx" . "image" .Values.gascity.image) }}
          imagePullPolicy: {{ .Values.image.pullPolicy }}
          args: ["gc", "start", "--foreground", "/city"]
          env:
            - name: GC_DAEMON_SUPERVISOR_BIND
              value: "{{ .Values.gascity.supervisor.bind }}:{{ .Values.gascity.supervisorPort }}"
            - name: GC_DAEMON_SUPERVISOR_ALLOW_MUTATIONS
              value: {{ .Values.gascity.supervisor.allowMutations | quote }}
            - name: GC_DOLT_HOST
              value: {{ include "gonk.doltHost" . | quote }}
            - name: GC_DOLT_PORT
              value: {{ include "gonk.doltPort" . | quote }}
            - name: SSL_CERT_FILE
              value: {{ .Values.gitlab.caCert.mountPath | quote }}
          ports:
            - name: supervisor
              containerPort: {{ .Values.gascity.supervisorPort }}
          livenessProbe:
            httpGet: {path: /health, port: supervisor}
            initialDelaySeconds: 10
            periodSeconds: 20
          readinessProbe:
            httpGet: {path: /v0/readiness, port: supervisor}
            initialDelaySeconds: 5
            periodSeconds: 5
          volumeMounts:
            {{- if eq .Values.gascity.delivery "configmap" }}
            - name: city
              mountPath: /city
            {{- end }}
            - name: trust-bundle
              mountPath: {{ .Values.gitlab.caCert.mountPath }}
              subPath: ca.crt
              readOnly: true
          resources:
            {{- toYaml .Values.gascity.resources | nindent 12 }}
      volumes:
        {{- if eq .Values.gascity.delivery "configmap" }}
        - name: city
          configMap:
            name: gonk-city
        {{- end }}
        - name: trust-bundle
          configMap:
            name: {{ .Values.gitlab.caCert.existingConfigMap }}
            items:
              - key: {{ .Values.gitlab.caCert.key }}
                path: ca.crt
{{- end }}
```

> **The `{{ .spec }}` indentation across three workload shapes is fiddly.** The
> golden manifest (Task 9) and `kubeconform` are what keep it honest; do not
> hand-verify the YAML by eye. If Task 0.5 has already run, set the three
> smoke-gated defaults to its findings before generating the golden.

```yaml
spec:
```

### Task 7: NetworkPolicy — the thing spec §9's central claim rests on, and which **is not enforced on this cluster**

Spec §9: *"Agent pods: NetworkPolicy allows egress only to GitLab and LiteLLM. No
direct internet; cloud access exists only through the metered proxy, **so budgets
cannot be bypassed**."*

> ### ⚠ ON THE TARGET CLUSTER, THAT SENTENCE IS FALSE TODAY. SHIP THE POLICY ANYWAY.
>
> **NetworkPolicies are not enforced here.** Flannel does not implement them, and
> the **Cilium HelmRelease that would is suspended**
> (`gitops:clusters/orac/foundation/kustomization.yaml`). There is no cluster-wide
> default-deny and no enforced egress anywhere — **every existing policy in the
> gitops repo carries a comment saying exactly this**, and so must every policy
> below.
>
> **The concrete bypass:** LiteLLM routes local models to **Ollama at
> `http://192.168.1.142:11434`, which needs no credential.** An agent pod can call
> it directly, skip LiteLLM entirely, and burn local GPU with **zero metering and
> no ceiling.**
>
> **Owner decision (2026-07-13): ship it, document the gap, do not gate on it.**
> So this task **writes the correct policies anyway** — *documentation-as-code*:
> they become real the day Cilium is unsuspended, with no further work and no
> second design pass. Guard **G10** still refuses an empty `agentPodSelector`.
> Every template gets the comment. `chart/gonk/README.md` and ADR-006 (Task 9) say
> it to the operator's face.
>
> **What must never happen:** someone reads a rendered `NetworkPolicy`, or a green
> `netpol_test.go`, and concludes that the egress is closed. **These tests prove a
> policy RENDERS. Nothing here proves a packet is DROPPED.** Plan 06 writes the
> egress-denial test and **skips** it with a message naming Cilium; **un-skipping
> that test is the gate**, and because a *wrong* selector (OD-5) renders fine and
> enforces nothing, **the test — not this template — is what will finally prove
> it.**
>
> The honest claim, until then: **cloud-rung budgets are hard** (a cloud call needs
> an API key the pod only ever holds via LiteLLM's virtual key, and LiteLLM refuses
> at the ceiling); **local-model budgets are advisory.**

Write it carefully, and hand the proof to Plan 06.

**Files:** Create `chart/gonk/templates/networkpolicy-default-deny.yaml`,
`networkpolicy-gonk-agent.yaml`, `networkpolicy-gonk-intake.yaml`,
`networkpolicy-gonk-meter.yaml`, `networkpolicy-gonk-dolt.yaml`,
`networkpolicy-gonk-controller.yaml`;
create `internal/charttest/netpol_test.go`.

**Umbrella additions:** the `gonk-dolt` policy (Step 7) admits meter **and** the
bundled controller; the `gonk-controller` policy (Step 7b) is new. Both carry the
NOT-ENFORCED banner, so `TestEveryNetworkPolicyDeclaresItIsNotEnforced` covers them
automatically.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `internal/charttest/netpol_test.go`

```go
//go:build chart

package charttest

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

// GitLab is IN-CLUSTER on the target cluster (namespace `gitlab`, OD-6), so the
// default profile uses a namespaceSelector, NOT an ipBlock: a CIDR for an
// in-cluster Service is a pod IP, and pod IPs move on every reschedule -- the
// policy would work until the first restart and then silently stop allowing
// GitLab. The `gitlab.cidrs` path stays for genuinely-external GitLabs and is
// exercised by withExternalGitLab below.
func withNetpol(extra ...string) []string {
	// Minimum() already sets dolt.image.tag (the bundled factory), so nothing extra
	// is needed for the ledger here.
	return append(append(Minimum(),
		"--set", "networkPolicy.gitlabInCluster.enabled=true",
		"--set", "networkPolicy.gitlabInCluster.namespaceSelector.kubernetes\\.io/metadata\\.name=gitlab"), extra...)
}

func withExternalGitLab(extra ...string) []string {
	return append(append(Minimum(),
		"--set", "networkPolicy.gitlab.cidrs={10.0.5.7/32}"), extra...)
}

// EVERY policy must carry the "not enforced" banner. It is the ONLY thing between
// a future reader and the conclusion that spec 9's egress guarantee is live.
// Deleting the comment is a documentation regression with a security blast radius,
// so it is a TEST, not a convention.
func TestEveryNetworkPolicyDeclaresItIsNotEnforced(t *testing.T) {
	for _, o := range Objects(t, Render(t, withNetpol()...)) {
		if o.Kind != "NetworkPolicy" {
			continue
		}
		if !strings.Contains(o.Doc, "NOT ENFORCED") || !strings.Contains(o.Doc, "Cilium") {
			t.Errorf("NetworkPolicy/%s does not say it is unenforced and name Cilium.\n"+
				"Flannel does not implement NetworkPolicy and the Cilium HelmRelease is suspended: "+
				"this object blocks nothing today, and anyone reading it must be told so.\n%s",
				o.Metadata.Name, o.Doc)
		}
	}
}

// A default-deny policy is the only thing that makes the ALLOW policies mean
// anything. Without it, "allow egress to GitLab" adds a permission to a pod that
// already had every permission.
func TestDefaultDenyExists(t *testing.T) {
	np := MustObject(t, Render(t, withNetpol()...), "NetworkPolicy", "gonk-default-deny")
	for _, want := range []string{"Ingress", "Egress"} {
		if !strings.Contains(np.Doc, want) {
			t.Errorf("default-deny does not cover %s", want)
		}
	}
	if !strings.Contains(np.Doc, "podSelector: {}") {
		t.Error("default-deny does not select every pod in the namespace")
	}
}

// THE spec-9 policy.
//
// This test proves the policy RENDERS CORRECTLY. It does NOT prove a packet is
// dropped -- the CNI does not enforce policy on this cluster at all. That proof
// is Plan 06's egress-denial test, which is WRITTEN AND SKIPPED until Cilium
// lands. Do not mistake this green for that one.
func TestAgentEgressIsLimitedToGitLabLiteLLMAndDNS(t *testing.T) {
	np := MustObject(t, Render(t, withNetpol()...), "NetworkPolicy", "gonk-agent")
	if !strings.Contains(np.Doc, "gitlab") {
		t.Error("agents cannot reach GitLab")
	}
	if !strings.Contains(np.Doc, "litellm") {
		t.Error("agents cannot reach LiteLLM -- which is the ONLY door to a model")
	}
	if !strings.Contains(np.Doc, "port: 53") {
		t.Error("agents cannot resolve DNS, so they can reach nothing at all")
	}
	// The whole point: no blanket egress.
	if strings.Contains(np.Doc, "0.0.0.0/0") {
		t.Fatal("the agent policy allows 0.0.0.0/0. Cloud models would then be reachable OUTSIDE LiteLLM, and budgets could be bypassed -- which is the one thing spec 9 exists to prevent.")
	}
	if !strings.Contains(np.Doc, "agent-session") {
		t.Errorf("the agent policy does not select the agent pods:\n%s", np.Doc)
	}
}

// The public listener may be reached from the ingress controller -- which on this
// cluster is TRAEFIK, the only IngressClass (docs/environment.md). The PRIVATE one
// (metrics, health, and the UNAUTHENTICATED POST /admin/reconcile) may be reached
// only by Prometheus and the e2e harness.
func TestIntakeIngressIsLimited(t *testing.T) {
	np := MustObject(t, Render(t, withNetpol()...), "NetworkPolicy", "gonk-intake")
	if !strings.Contains(np.Doc, "traefik") {
		t.Error("the ingress controller (traefik) cannot reach the webhook listener")
	}
	if !strings.Contains(np.Doc, "8080") || !strings.Contains(np.Doc, "9090") {
		t.Errorf("both listeners must be covered:\n%s", np.Doc)
	}
}

// The external-GitLab path must still render, lint and validate -- it is the only
// thing an operator whose GitLab is NOT in-cluster can use.
func TestExternalGitLabEgressStillRenders(t *testing.T) {
	np := MustObject(t, Render(t, withExternalGitLab()...), "NetworkPolicy", "gonk-agent")
	if !strings.Contains(np.Doc, "10.0.5.7/32") {
		t.Error("the external-GitLab (ipBlock) path does not render")
	}
}

// Meter is reachable from intake and from the pack. With the controller BUNDLED
// (the default) the pack is in THIS namespace (component: controller); with a BYO
// controller it is the gascity namespaceSelector. Nothing else may ask for a
// budget decision.
func TestMeterIngressIsLimited(t *testing.T) {
	np := MustObject(t, Render(t, withNetpol()...), "NetworkPolicy", "gonk-meter")
	if !strings.Contains(np.Doc, "component: controller") {
		t.Error("the bundled controller (the pack) cannot reach /policy/decide, so no session could ever spawn")
	}
	if !strings.Contains(np.Doc, "component: intake") && !strings.Contains(np.Doc, "intake") {
		t.Error("intake cannot reach meter")
	}
	// BYO controller: the gascity namespaceSelector path.
	byo := MustObject(t, Render(t, withNetpol(
		"--set", "gascity.enabled=false",
		"--set", "gascity.supervisorURL=http://gc.external.svc:8372")...),
		"NetworkPolicy", "gonk-meter")
	if !strings.Contains(byo.Doc, "gascity") {
		t.Error("BYO controller: the gascity namespaceSelector path is missing")
	}
}

// RECONCILED, Task 0: NEW. The default ledger.postgres.mode is `shared`
// (the actual target-cluster CNPG tenancy, databases-app/postgres), and the
// original egress rule only covered mode=cnpg -- meter's DEFAULT profile
// would have shipped a NetworkPolicy with no path to its own ledger at all.
// Assert both the default (shared) and the cnpg mode render a real egress
// target, and that they are NOT the same rule.
func TestMeterEgressReachesTheLedgerInEveryPostgresMode(t *testing.T) {
	shared := MustObject(t, Render(t, withNetpol()...), "NetworkPolicy", "gonk-meter")
	if !strings.Contains(shared.Doc, "databases-app") {
		t.Fatalf("default profile (ledger.postgres.mode=shared): meter has no egress path to its own ledger:\n%s", shared.Doc)
	}
	cnpg := MustObject(t, Render(t, withNetpol("--set", "ledger.postgres.mode=cnpg")...), "NetworkPolicy", "gonk-meter")
	if !strings.Contains(cnpg.Doc, "cnpg.io/cluster: gonk-ledger-postgres") {
		t.Fatalf("ledger.postgres.mode=cnpg: meter has no egress path to the chart-managed Cluster:\n%s", cnpg.Doc)
	}
}

// RECONCILED, Task 0: the bundled Dolt accepts connections from the Gas City
// controller (beads) and NOBODY ELSE -- gonk-meter is explicitly NOT admitted
// any more. Its ledger is Postgres/CNPG, unconditionally (ADR-004 Decision
// 10), so it never needs to reach the Dolt Service at all; admitting it would
// be a NetworkPolicy that grants an access path nothing ever uses.
func TestDoltIngressIsControllerOnlyNotMeter(t *testing.T) {
	np := MustObject(t, Render(t, withNetpol()...), "NetworkPolicy", "gonk-dolt")
	if strings.Contains(np.Doc, "component: meter") {
		t.Fatalf("the Dolt policy admits meter, but meter's ledger is Postgres/CNPG -- it has no legitimate reason to reach Dolt:\n%s", np.Doc)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(np.Doc, "component: controller") {
		t.Fatalf("the Dolt policy does not admit the controller (beads):\n%s", np.Doc)
	}
}

func TestNetworkPolicyCanBeDisabledWholesale(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, append(Minimum(),
		"--set", "networkPolicy.enabled=false")...)
	for _, o := range Objects(t, out) {
		if o.Kind == "NetworkPolicy" {
			t.Fatalf("networkPolicy.enabled=false still rendered %s", o.Metadata.Name)
		}
	}
}
```
