- [ ] **Step 3: `chart/gonk/templates/networkpolicy-default-deny.yaml`**

```yaml
{{- if .Values.networkPolicy.enabled }}
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: NetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: gonk-default-deny
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
spec:
  # ==========================================================================
  # !!! NOT ENFORCED ON THIS CLUSTER (as of 2026-07-13) !!!
  # Flannel does not implement NetworkPolicy, and the Cilium HelmRelease that
  # would is SUSPENDED (gitops:clusters/orac/foundation/kustomization.yaml).
  # THIS OBJECT BLOCKS NOTHING TODAY. It is documentation-as-code: it is
  # correct, it ships, and it starts enforcing the day Cilium is unsuspended --
  # with no further work. Do not read it as a guarantee.
  # See docs/environment.md ("KNOWN LIMITATION") and chart/gonk/README.md.
  # ==========================================================================
  #
  # Deny everything, in both directions, for every pod in this namespace. The
  # policies that follow ADD back exactly what each component needs. Without this
  # one, they add permissions to pods that already had all of them, and the whole
  # exercise is theatre.
  #
  # THIS APPLIES TO AGENT SESSION PODS TOO -- as long as they are in this
  # namespace. If Gas City's session provider puts them somewhere else, this
  # policy does not touch them and spec 9's guarantee does not exist. (OD-5.)
  podSelector: {}
  policyTypes: [Ingress, Egress]
{{- end }}
```

**Put that same `NOT ENFORCED` banner at the top of all five policies.** It is the
convention every existing policy in the gitops repo already follows, and it is the
only thing standing between a future reader and the conclusion that the egress is
closed.

- [ ] **Step 4: `chart/gonk/templates/networkpolicy-gonk-agent.yaml`**

```yaml
{{- if .Values.networkPolicy.enabled }}
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: NetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: gonk-agent
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: agent-session
spec:
  # ==========================================================================
  # *** THE POLICY SPEC 9's CENTRAL CLAIM RESTS ON -- AND IT IS NOT ENFORCED. ***
  #
  # "Cloud access exists only through the metered proxy, so budgets cannot be
  # bypassed" is TRUE ONLY IF (a) this selector matches the pods Gas City's k8s
  # session provider actually creates (OD-5 -- Gas City is not even deployed yet,
  # so nobody knows the labels), AND (b) the CNI actually enforces policy.
  #
  # (b) IS FALSE TODAY. Flannel does not implement NetworkPolicy and the Cilium
  # HelmRelease is SUSPENDED. So RIGHT NOW an agent pod can reach anything --
  # including Ollama at http://192.168.1.142:11434, WHICH NEEDS NO CREDENTIAL --
  # skip LiteLLM entirely, and burn local GPU with ZERO METERING.
  #
  # Owner decision 2026-07-13: SHIP IT, DOCUMENT THE GAP, DO NOT GATE ON IT.
  # Therefore:
  #   * CLOUD-rung budgets ARE hard: a cloud call needs an API key, and the pod's
  #     only route to one is LiteLLM's virtual key, which refuses at the ceiling.
  #   * LOCAL-model budgets are ADVISORY until Cilium lands.
  # Say it that way in every README, dashboard and demo. Not "budgets cannot be
  # bypassed".
  #
  # Plan 06 writes the egress-denial test and SKIPS it, naming Cilium. UN-SKIPPING
  # THAT TEST IS THE GATE -- and note that a WRONG selector renders fine and
  # enforces nothing, so the test, not this template, is what proves it.
  # ==========================================================================
  podSelector:
    matchLabels:
      {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.agentPodSelector | nindent 6 }}
  policyTypes: [Egress]
  egress:
    # DNS. Without it the pod resolves nothing and reaches nothing.
    - to:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.dns.namespaceSelector | nindent 14 }}
      ports:
        {{- range .Values.networkPolicy.dns.ports }}
        - protocol: UDP
          port: {{ . }}
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ . }}
        {{- end }}
    # LiteLLM: THE ONLY DOOR TO A MODEL. Every token an agent spends passes
    # through it, tagged, budgeted, and hard-refusable.
    - to:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.litellm.namespaceSelector | nindent 14 }}
          {{- with .Values.networkPolicy.litellm.podSelector }}
          podSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml . | nindent 14 }}
          {{- end }}
      ports:
        {{- range .Values.networkPolicy.litellm.ports }}
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ . }}
        {{- end }}
    # GitLab: the agent clones the rig and posts comments as the bot.
    {{- if .Values.networkPolicy.gitlabInCluster.enabled }}
    - to:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.gitlabInCluster.namespaceSelector | nindent 14 }}
          {{- with .Values.networkPolicy.gitlabInCluster.podSelector }}
          podSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml . | nindent 14 }}
          {{- end }}
    {{- else }}
    - to:
        {{- range .Values.networkPolicy.gitlab.cidrs }}
        - ipBlock:
            cidr: {{ . }}
        {{- end }}
      ports:
        {{- range .Values.networkPolicy.gitlab.ports }}
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ . }}
        {{- end }}
    {{- end }}
    # gonk-meter: the agent reads its own session cost for the commit trailers
    # (GET /v1/cost/session/{key}).
    - to:
        - podSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- include "gonk.selectorLabels" (dict "ctx" . "component" "meter") | nindent 14 }}
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ .Values.meter.port }}
    # AND NOTHING ELSE. No 0.0.0.0/0. No "just for pip". An agent pod that can
    # reach the internet can reach api.anthropic.com directly, with a key from
    # anywhere, and every budget in this system becomes a suggestion.
{{- end }}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `chart/gonk/templates/networkpolicy-gonk-intake.yaml`**

```yaml
{{- if and .Values.networkPolicy.enabled .Values.intake.enabled }}
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: NetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: gonk-intake
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: intake
spec:
  podSelector:
    matchLabels:
      {{- include "gonk.selectorLabels" (dict "ctx" . "component" "intake") | nindent 6 }}
  policyTypes: [Ingress, Egress]
  ingress:
    # The webhook, from the ingress controller only.
    - from:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.ingressController.namespaceSelector | nindent 14 }}
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ .Values.intake.ports.public }}
    # The private listener (metrics, health, and an UNAUTHENTICATED
    # POST /admin/reconcile), from Prometheus only.
    - from:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.prometheus.namespaceSelector | nindent 14 }}
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ .Values.intake.ports.private }}
  egress:
    - to:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.dns.namespaceSelector | nindent 14 }}
      ports:
        {{- range .Values.networkPolicy.dns.ports }}
        - protocol: UDP
          port: {{ . }}
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ . }}
        {{- end }}
    # GitLab.
    {{- if .Values.networkPolicy.gitlabInCluster.enabled }}
    - to:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.gitlabInCluster.namespaceSelector | nindent 14 }}
    {{- else }}
    - to:
        {{- range .Values.networkPolicy.gitlab.cidrs }}
        - ipBlock:
            cidr: {{ . }}
        {{- end }}
      ports:
        {{- range .Values.networkPolicy.gitlab.ports }}
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ . }}
        {{- end }}
    {{- end }}
    # gonk-meter (register projects, read state).
    - to:
        - podSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- include "gonk.selectorLabels" (dict "ctx" . "component" "meter") | nindent 14 }}
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ .Values.meter.port }}
    # The Gas City supervisor (fire orders).
    {{- if eq .Values.gascity.dispatch "http" }}
    - to:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.gascity.namespaceSelector | nindent 14 }}
      ports:
        {{- range .Values.networkPolicy.gascity.ports }}
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ . }}
        {{- end }}
    {{- end }}
{{- end }}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `chart/gonk/templates/networkpolicy-gonk-meter.yaml`**

```yaml
{{- if and .Values.networkPolicy.enabled .Values.meter.enabled }}
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: NetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: gonk-meter
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: meter
spec:
  podSelector:
    matchLabels:
      {{- include "gonk.selectorLabels" (dict "ctx" . "component" "meter") | nindent 6 }}
  policyTypes: [Ingress, Egress]
  ingress:
    # intake (project registration) and the agent pods (session cost, for commit
    # trailers). Both are in this namespace.
    - from:
        - podSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- include "gonk.selectorLabels" (dict "ctx" . "component" "intake") | nindent 14 }}
        - podSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.agentPodSelector | nindent 14 }}
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ .Values.meter.port }}
    # The PACK's dispatch formula (POST /v1/policy/decide) and gate step
    # (POST /v1/policy/outcome) run inside the Gas City controller.
    # WITHOUT THIS RULE, NO SESSION CAN EVER SPAWN.
    #   * bundled controller  -> in THIS namespace (component: controller)
    #   * BYO controller      -> the gascity namespaceSelector
    - from:
        {{- if .Values.gascity.enabled }}
        - podSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- include "gonk.selectorLabels" (dict "ctx" . "component" "controller") | nindent 14 }}
        {{- else }}
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.gascity.namespaceSelector | nindent 14 }}
        {{- end }}
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ .Values.meter.port }}
    # Prometheus (/metrics is unauthenticated; the rest of the API is not).
    - from:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.prometheus.namespaceSelector | nindent 14 }}
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ .Values.meter.port }}
  egress:
    - to:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.dns.namespaceSelector | nindent 14 }}
      ports:
        {{- range .Values.networkPolicy.dns.ports }}
        - protocol: UDP
          port: {{ . }}
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ . }}
        {{- end }}
    # LiteLLM's ADMIN API: provision virtual keys, read /spend/logs. This is a
    # different door from the agents' -- same host, admin credential.
    - to:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.litellm.namespaceSelector | nindent 14 }}
      ports:
        {{- range .Values.networkPolicy.litellm.ports }}
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ . }}
        {{- end }}
    # The ledger. RECONCILED, Task 0: the OLD `ledger.backend == "dolt"` branch is
    # DELETED -- that state does not exist any more (ADR-004 Decision 10), and
    # meter never has a legitimate reason to egress to the Dolt Service (it backs
    # beads only). The `cnpg` branch below is unchanged. A gap this reconciliation
    # ALSO closes: the DEFAULT ledger.postgres.mode is `shared` (the existing
    # databases-app/postgres CNPG cluster), and the original text had NO egress
    # rule for that mode at all -- meter's own default profile would have had no
    # NetworkPolicy path to its own ledger. `external` has the same gap. Both now
    # get a namespaceSelector egress, matching the litellm/dns pattern above.
    {{- if eq .Values.ledger.postgres.mode "cnpg" }}
    - to:
        - podSelector:
            matchLabels:
              cnpg.io/cluster: gonk-ledger-postgres
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: 5432
    {{- else }}
    {{- /* shared (the DEFAULT: databases-app/postgres) or external (BYO Postgres,
           any namespace). networkPolicy.ledgerPostgres.namespaceSelector defaults
           to matching databases-app for `shared`; override it for `external`. */ -}}
    - to:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.ledgerPostgres.namespaceSelector | nindent 14 }}
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ .Values.ledger.postgres.port | default 5432 }}
    {{- end }}
    # The Kubernetes API server (the KeySink writes per-project Secrets). A
    # NetworkPolicy cannot name the API server by service, so this is left to the
    # cluster's own allowance -- and if your CNI blocks it, meter cannot deliver
    # keys and NOTHING RUNS. Plan 06 must confirm.
{{- end }}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: `chart/gonk/templates/networkpolicy-gonk-dolt.yaml`**

> **RECONCILED, Task 0: the bundled Dolt has exactly ONE legitimate client
> now, not two.** The original text admitted `gonk-meter` as an ingress source
> "for the ledger, when `ledger.backend: dolt`." **That state no longer
> exists** — Task 0b's spike ran, Dolt failed the reservation-race isolation
> test, and the ledger is Postgres/CNPG unconditionally (ADR-004 Decision 10).
> gonk-meter has **no reason to ever open a connection to Dolt** — its ledger
> traffic goes to `secrets.ledger.existingSecret`'s Postgres DSN, which is
> typically outside this namespace (CNPG) and outside this policy's scope
> entirely. **Do not admit meter here.** Only the **Gas City controller**
> (the beads store, always) is a legitimate ingress source.

The bundled Dolt has **one** legitimate client: the **Gas City controller**
(the beads store), and NOBODY ELSE. (When the controller is external,
`gascity.enabled: false`, it reaches Dolt from outside the namespace and this
in-cluster policy does not gate it — but the smoke test's Dolt is
in-namespace.)

```yaml
{{- if and .Values.networkPolicy.enabled .Values.dolt.enabled .Values.gascity.enabled }}
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: NetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: gonk-dolt
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: dolt
spec:
  # NOT ENFORCED ON THIS CLUSTER (Flannel; Cilium suspended). Documentation-as-code.
  podSelector:
    matchLabels:
      {{- include "gonk.selectorLabels" (dict "ctx" . "component" "dolt") | nindent 6 }}
  policyTypes: [Ingress]
  ingress:
    # RECONCILED, Task 0: the Gas City controller (beads) ONLY. gonk-meter is
    # NOT admitted -- its ledger is Postgres/CNPG, never a Dolt database, so it
    # never needs to reach this Service at all (ADR-004 Decision 10).
    - from:
        - podSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- include "gonk.selectorLabels" (dict "ctx" . "component" "controller") | nindent 14 }}
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ .Values.dolt.port }}
{{- end }}
```

- [ ] **Step 7b: `chart/gonk/templates/networkpolicy-gonk-controller.yaml`** (bundled controller only)

The controller is **ingress** from intake (orders) and Traefik (if the supervisor
is ever fronted); **egress** to GitLab (clone/push), LiteLLM (agent provisioning
happens via meter, but the controller itself may health-check), Dolt (beads), the
Kubernetes API (spawn agent pods), and DNS. Carries the same **NOT ENFORCED**
banner. Gated on `gascity.enabled` — a BYO controller lives outside this namespace.

```yaml
{{- if and .Values.networkPolicy.enabled .Values.gascity.enabled }}
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: NetworkPolicy
metadata:
  name: gonk-controller
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: controller
spec:
  # NOT ENFORCED ON THIS CLUSTER (Flannel; Cilium suspended). Documentation-as-code.
  podSelector:
    matchLabels:
      {{- include "gonk.selectorLabels" (dict "ctx" . "component" "controller") | nindent 6 }}
  policyTypes: [Ingress, Egress]
  ingress:
    - from:
        - podSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- include "gonk.selectorLabels" (dict "ctx" . "component" "intake") | nindent 14 }}
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ .Values.gascity.supervisorPort }}
  egress:
    # DNS, GitLab, Dolt (beads), and the API server (spawn agent pods). The API
    # server cannot be named by a NetworkPolicy selector -- left to the cluster's
    # own allowance; if your CNI blocks it, NO SESSION SPAWNS. Plan 06 confirms.
    - to:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.dns.namespaceSelector | nindent 14 }}
      ports:
        {{- range .Values.networkPolicy.dns.ports }}
        - protocol: UDP
          port: {{ . }}
        {{- end }}
    {{- if .Values.dolt.enabled }}
    - to:
        - podSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- include "gonk.selectorLabels" (dict "ctx" . "component" "dolt") | nindent 14 }}
      ports:
        - protocol: TCP
          port: {{ .Values.dolt.port }}
    {{- end }}
    {{- if .Values.networkPolicy.gitlabInCluster.enabled }}
    - to:
        - namespaceSelector:
            matchLabels:
              {{- toYaml .Values.networkPolicy.gitlabInCluster.namespaceSelector | nindent 14 }}
    {{- end }}
{{- end }}
```

### Task 8: Observability — ServiceMonitors, alert rules, and the three Grafana dashboards

Spec §8 names the series and **names the three dashboards: Factory, Cost, Health.**
The chart **never installs Prometheus or Grafana** (spec §7.4); it emits CRs and
label-discovered ConfigMaps, all off by default (AD-6).

**One rule above all others** [P03]: **the Cost dashboard must default to
`synthetic="false"` on every money panel.** Local inference is priced
*synthetically* so that LiteLLM's USD virtual-key ceiling is a hard door for
tokens. Those dollars **were never billed to anyone.** Presenting one as spend is
the single way this design can do real harm.

**Files:** Create `chart/gonk/templates/servicemonitor-gonk-intake.yaml`,
`servicemonitor-gonk-meter.yaml`, `prometheusrule-gonk.yaml`,
`configmap-dashboard-{factory,cost,health}.yaml`,
`chart/gonk/dashboards/{factory,cost,health}.json`;
create `internal/charttest/monitoring_test.go`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `internal/charttest/monitoring_test.go`

```go
//go:build chart

package charttest

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

func withMonitoring() []string {
	return append(Minimum(),
		"--set", "monitoring.serviceMonitor.enabled=true",
		"--set", "monitoring.prometheusRule.enabled=true",
		"--set", "monitoring.dashboards.enabled=true")
}

func TestMonitoringIsOffByDefault(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, Minimum()...)
	for _, o := range Objects(t, out) {
		switch o.Kind {
		case "ServiceMonitor", "PrometheusRule":
			t.Fatalf("%s rendered by default; the chart must not assume a Prometheus Operator", o.Kind)
		}
	}
}

// Intake's metrics are on the PRIVATE listener. A ServiceMonitor pointed at the
// public Service would scrape the hook listener and get 404 forever.
func TestIntakeServiceMonitorScrapesThePrivateService(t *testing.T) {
	sm := MustObject(t, Render(t, withMonitoring()...), "ServiceMonitor", "gonk-intake")
	if !strings.Contains(sm.Doc, "http-private") {
		t.Fatalf("the intake ServiceMonitor does not target the private port:\n%s", sm.Doc)
	}
	if strings.Contains(sm.Doc, "exposure: public") {
		t.Fatal("the intake ServiceMonitor selects the PUBLIC service, which serves only /hook/gitlab")
	}
}

// AD-2 in Plan 02: an invalid .gonk.yml makes a project go dark SILENTLY. Plan 02
// says in as many words that Plan 05 must ship the alert. This is that alert.
func TestAlertOnInvalidProjects(t *testing.T) {
	pr := MustObject(t, Render(t, withMonitoring()...), "PrometheusRule", "gonk")
	for _, want := range []string{
		"GonkProjectConfigInvalid",
		`gonk_intake_projects{state="invalid"}`,
		"GonkMeterSpendSyncStale",
		"gonk_meter_spend_sync_age_seconds",
		"GonkMeterDown",
		"GonkReservationRacesLost",
		"GonkDeferredBeadsStuck",
	} {
		if !strings.Contains(pr.Doc, want) {
			t.Errorf("PrometheusRule is missing %q", want)
		}
	}
}

// THE RULE. Every money panel filters synthetic="false".
func TestCostDashboardNeverPresentsSyntheticDollarsAsSpend(t *testing.T) {
	cm := MustObject(t, Render(t, withMonitoring()...), "ConfigMap", "gonk-dashboard-cost")
	raw := cm.Data(t)["cost.json"]

	var dash struct {
		Panels []struct {
			Title   string `json:"title"`
			Targets []struct {
				Expr string `json:"expr"`
			} `json:"targets"`
		} `json:"panels"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(raw), &dash); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the cost dashboard is not valid JSON: %v", err)
	}
	if len(dash.Panels) == 0 {
		t.Fatal("the cost dashboard has no panels")
	}
	for _, p := range dash.Panels {
		for _, tg := range p.Targets {
			if !strings.Contains(tg.Expr, "gonk_meter_spend_usd_total") {
				continue // not a money panel
			}
			// A money panel either filters to REAL spend, or it is the one panel
			// that is explicitly and unmistakably labelled as synthetic.
			real := strings.Contains(tg.Expr, `synthetic="false"`)
			labelled := strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(p.Title), "synthetic")
			if !real && !labelled {
				t.Errorf("panel %q charts gonk_meter_spend_usd_total without synthetic=\"false\" "+
					"and without saying SYNTHETIC in its title. Local inference is priced "+
					"synthetically so LiteLLM's USD ceiling can gate TOKENS -- those dollars were "+
					"never billed to anyone. Presenting one as spend is the one way this design "+
					"can do real harm.\n  expr: %s", p.Title, tg.Expr)
			}
			if !real && labelled && !strings.Contains(tg.Expr, `synthetic="true"`) {
				t.Errorf("panel %q says synthetic but does not filter synthetic=\"true\"", p.Title)
			}
		}
	}
}

// All three dashboards, discoverable by the Grafana sidecar.
func TestThreeDashboardsAreShipped(t *testing.T) {
	out := Render(t, withMonitoring()...)
	for _, name := range []string{"gonk-dashboard-factory", "gonk-dashboard-cost", "gonk-dashboard-health"} {
		cm := MustObject(t, out, "ConfigMap", name)
		if cm.Metadata.Labels["grafana_dashboard"] != "1" {
			t.Errorf("%s is not discoverable by the Grafana sidecar: labels=%v", name, cm.Metadata.Labels)
		}
		for _, v := range cm.Data(t) {
			var any map[string]any
			if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(v), &any); err != nil {
				t.Errorf("%s contains invalid dashboard JSON: %v", name, err)
			}
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `chart/gonk/templates/servicemonitor-gonk-intake.yaml`**

```yaml
{{- if and .Values.monitoring.serviceMonitor.enabled .Values.intake.enabled }}
apiVersion: monitoring.coreos.com/v1
kind: ServiceMonitor
metadata:
  name: gonk-intake
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    app.kubernetes.io/component: intake
    {{- with .Values.monitoring.serviceMonitor.labels }}
    {{- toYaml . | nindent 4 }}
    {{- end }}
spec:
  # The INTERNAL service. Intake's /metrics is on the private listener; the public
  # one serves POST /hook/gitlab and 404s everything else, so a ServiceMonitor
  # aimed at it would scrape 404s forever.
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      {{- include "gonk.selectorLabels" (dict "ctx" . "component" "intake") | nindent 6 }}
      gonk.orac.local/exposure: internal
  endpoints:
    - port: http-private
      path: /metrics
      interval: {{ .Values.monitoring.serviceMonitor.interval }}
      scrapeTimeout: {{ .Values.monitoring.serviceMonitor.scrapeTimeout }}
{{- end }}
```

> The `gonk.orac.local/exposure: internal` label is on the internal Service only
> (Task 5), which is what disambiguates the two.

- [ ] **Step 4: `chart/gonk/templates/servicemonitor-gonk-meter.yaml`** — the same
shape, `component: meter`, port `http-api`, path `/metrics`. Meter has one
listener; `/metrics` is unauthenticated on it.

- [ ] **Step 5: `chart/gonk/templates/prometheusrule-gonk.yaml`**

```yaml
{{- if .Values.monitoring.prometheusRule.enabled }}
apiVersion: monitoring.coreos.com/v1
kind: PrometheusRule
metadata:
  name: gonk
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    {{- with .Values.monitoring.prometheusRule.labels }}
    {{- toYaml . | nindent 4 }}
    {{- end }}
spec:
  groups:
    - name: gonk.rules
      rules:
        # Plan 02 AD-2, in as many words: "a project goes dark and nobody notices.
        # This is the assumed default I am least comfortable with. Mitigation,
        # which Plan 05 must ship: an alert rule on
        # gonk_intake_projects{state="invalid"} > 0." Here it is.
        - alert: GonkProjectConfigInvalid
          expr: gonk_intake_projects{state="invalid"} > 0
          for: 15m
          labels: {severity: warning}
          annotations:
            summary: "A project's .gonk.yml will not load; the project is doing nothing."
            description: >-
              gonk fails closed on an unparseable .gonk.yml: no work is dispatched and
              the LiteLLM key is deleted. It does NOT tell anyone. Without this alert
              a project goes dark silently. Fix the config, or de-onboard the project.

        # Meter's view of spend is a POLL of LiteLLM's /spend/logs. If the poll is
        # stale, every headroom calculation is optimistic -- which is fail-OPEN.
        - alert: GonkMeterSpendSyncStale
          expr: gonk_meter_spend_sync_age_seconds > 900
          for: 10m
          labels: {severity: critical}
          annotations:
            summary: "gonk-meter's spend view is stale; budget headroom is being over-estimated."

        - alert: GonkMeterDown
          expr: up{job=~".*gonk-meter.*"} == 0
          for: 5m
          labels: {severity: critical}
          annotations:
            summary: "gonk-meter is down."
            description: >-
              No session can spawn (the pack's /policy/decide fails closed) and
              gonk-intake will go NotReady, which takes the webhook endpoint offline.
              GitLab auto-disables hooks that fail repeatedly -- check the hooks after
              recovery.

        # A lost race is a session that ASKED for budget and lost. A few are normal
        # under load. A flood means the serialization is not working, which is the
        # exact failure Plan 03's Task 0b was written to detect.
        - alert: GonkReservationRacesLost
          expr: rate(gonk_meter_reservation_races_lost_total[15m]) > 0.2
          for: 15m
          labels: {severity: warning}
          annotations:
            summary: "gonk-meter is losing reservation races; check that meter is single-replica and the store isolates."

        - alert: GonkDeferredBeadsStuck
          expr: gonk_meter_deferred_beads > 0
          for: 6h
          labels: {severity: warning}
          annotations:
            summary: "Work has been parked in waiting-for-capacity for 6h -- out of budget, or stuck in quiet hours."

        - alert: GonkSpendRowsUnattributed
          expr: rate(gonk_meter_spend_rows_unattributed_total[1h]) > 0
          for: 1h
          labels: {severity: warning}
          annotations:
            summary: "LiteLLM spend rows are arriving without attribution tags; someone is spending through a key gonk did not provision."
{{- end }}
```

> **Verify every metric name and label in Task 0.** These come from Plan 03's
> Task 9 and Plan 02's Task 10, and neither has run. In particular
> `gonk_meter_spend_usd_total` **must carry a `synthetic` label** or the Cost
> dashboard's central rule cannot be expressed at all.

- [ ] **Step 6: The three dashboards**

Each is a ConfigMap wrapping a `.Files.Get` of `dashboards/<name>.json`:

```yaml
{{- if .Values.monitoring.dashboards.enabled }}
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: gonk-dashboard-cost
  labels:
    {{- include "gonk.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    {{ .Values.monitoring.dashboards.label }}: {{ .Values.monitoring.dashboards.labelValue | quote }}
data:
  cost.json: |
    {{- .Files.Get "dashboards/cost.json" | nindent 4 }}
{{- end }}
```

(Identical for `factory` / `health`, changing only the name and the file.)

**The panel contract.** Build each dashboard as a plain Grafana JSON model
(`{"title": ..., "uid": ..., "panels": [...], "templating": {...}}`) with these
panels and **these exact PromQL expressions**:

**`factory.json`** — *"work in flight, outcomes, queue age"* (spec §8):

| Panel | Type | Expr |
|---|---|---|
| Webhooks received | timeseries | `sum by (result) (rate(gonk_intake_webhook_total[5m]))` |
| Reconcile outcomes | timeseries | `sum by (result) (rate(gonk_intake_reconcile_total[15m]))` |
| Projects by state | stat | `sum by (state) (gonk_intake_projects)` |
| Orders dispatched | timeseries | `sum by (trigger) (rate(gonk_intake_dispatched_total[5m]))` |
| Orders dropped | timeseries | `sum by (reason) (rate(gonk_intake_dispatch_dropped_total[5m]))` |
| Policy decisions | timeseries | `sum by (decision) (rate(gonk_meter_policy_decisions_total[5m]))` |
| Gate outcomes | timeseries | `sum by (outcome) (rate(gonk_meter_gate_outcomes_total[15m]))` |
| Ladder escalations | timeseries | `sum by (from_rung, to_rung) (rate(gonk_meter_ladder_escalations_total[1h]))` |
| Deferred beads | stat | `gonk_meter_deferred_beads` |
| Onboarding | timeseries | `sum by (result) (rate(gonk_intake_onboarding_total[1h]))` |

**`cost.json`** — *"project/group/rung, local vs cloud"*. **Every money panel
filters `synthetic="false"`.**

| Panel | Type | Expr |
|---|---|---|
| Real spend by project (30d) | bar | `sum by (project) (increase(gonk_meter_spend_usd_total{synthetic="false"}[30d]))` |
| Real spend by rung | timeseries | `sum by (rung) (rate(gonk_meter_spend_usd_total{synthetic="false"}[1h]))` |
| Budget remaining (USD) | stat | `gonk_meter_budget_remaining_usd` |
| Budget remaining (tokens) | stat | `gonk_meter_budget_remaining_tokens` |
| Tokens by rung | timeseries | `sum by (rung) (rate(gonk_meter_tokens_total[1h]))` |
| **Local inference — synthetic pricing (NOT BILLED)** | timeseries | `sum by (rung) (rate(gonk_meter_spend_usd_total{synthetic="true"}[1h]))` |
| Virtual keys | stat | `gonk_meter_virtual_keys` |

The synthetic panel's title **must contain the word "synthetic"** and must say it
is not billed — the chart test enforces exactly this, and it is the one rule in
the observability stack that protects a human from being told they spent money
they did not spend.

**`health.json`** — *"components, dolt, LiteLLM"*:

| Panel | Type | Expr |
|---|---|---|
| Component up | stat | `up{job=~".*gonk.*"}` |
| Spend-sync age | timeseries | `gonk_meter_spend_sync_age_seconds` |
| Spend-sync failures | timeseries | `rate(gonk_meter_spend_sync_failures_total[15m])` |
| Open reservations | timeseries | `gonk_meter_reservations_open` |
| Expired reservations | timeseries | `rate(gonk_meter_reservations_expired_total[1h])` |
| Reservation races lost | timeseries | `rate(gonk_meter_reservation_races_lost_total[15m])` |
| Clock skew vs LiteLLM | timeseries | `gonk_meter_clock_skew_seconds` |
| Meter cold starts | stat | `increase(gonk_meter_cold_start_total[24h])` |
| Reconcile duration p99 | timeseries | `histogram_quantile(0.99, sum by (le) (rate(gonk_intake_reconcile_duration_seconds_bucket[15m])))` |
| Rung-catalog drift | stat | `gonk_meter_catalog_drift_total` |

Add a `templating` variable `project` on all three
(`label_values(gonk_meter_project_state, project)`), and a `datasource` variable
so the dashboards are portable.

### Task 8.5: The component-toggle matrix — every sensible `.enabled` combination renders or fails closed

**Toggleability is a first-class feature, not an afterthought.** Each bundled
component (`gascity`, `dolt`, `intake`, `meter`) can be turned off when it is
managed or replaced elsewhere — and each such combination must either render a
coherent factory or fail closed with a message the operator can act on. This task
proves the matrix. It is a **no-cluster** task (pure `helm template`).

**Files:** Create `internal/charttest/toggle_test.go`,
`chart/gonk/ci/values-{all-bundled,external-dolt,byo-gascity,ledger-cnpg}.yaml`.

**The matrix (the sensible combinations, and their expected outcome):**

| Profile | Toggles | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| `all-bundled` | everything `enabled: true`; `ledger.backend: postgres` (the only value — RECONCILED, Task 0), `ledger.postgres.mode: shared` (default) | renders controller + Dolt (beads) + intake + meter + operator config; the whole factory |
| `external-dolt` | `dolt.enabled: false` + `dolt.external.host` | no `gonk-dolt`; the **controller** points at the external host (RECONCILED, Task 0: meter does NOT — it never talked to Dolt for the ledger; that state never existed post-spike); renders |
| `byo-gascity` | `gascity.enabled: false` + `supervisorURL` | no controller/RBAC; intake dispatches to the external supervisor; renders |
| `ledger-cnpg` | `dolt.enabled: true` (unaffected) + `ledger.postgres.mode: cnpg` (RECONCILED, Task 0: was `ledger.backend: postgres` — backend has no other value now, so only the mode changes) | Dolt **stays** (beads, unrelated); CNPG `Cluster` for the ledger; renders |
| *(fail-closed)* `dolt.enabled: false` alone | — | **fails** (G16: no external host) |
| *(fail-closed)* `gascity.enabled: false` alone | — | **fails** (G17: no supervisorURL) |
| *(fail-closed)* `meter.enabled: false` + `intake.enabled: true` | — | **fails** (G8/G18: unmetered dispatch) |
| *(fail-closed, RECONCILED, Task 0 — NEW)* `ledger.backend: dolt` | — | **fails** (G15: not in the enum; there is no dolt-backed ledger) |

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the matrix test** — `internal/charttest/toggle_test.go`

```go
//go:build chart

package charttest

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

// Each RENDERABLE combination templates coherently.
func TestToggleMatrixRenders(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct {
		name    string
		sets    []string
		present []string // kind/name that MUST appear
		absent  []string // kind/name that must NOT
	}{
		{
			name:    "all-bundled",
			sets:    nil, // Minimum() is already the whole factory
			present: []string{"Deployment/gonk-controller", "StatefulSet/gonk-dolt", "Deployment/gonk-intake", "Deployment/gonk-meter"},
		},
		{
			name:    "external-dolt",
			sets:    []string{"dolt.enabled=false", "dolt.external.host=dolt.databases.svc"},
			absent:  []string{"StatefulSet/gonk-dolt"},
			present: []string{"Deployment/gonk-controller", "Deployment/gonk-meter"},
		},
		{
			name:    "byo-gascity",
			sets:    []string{"gascity.enabled=false", "gascity.supervisorURL=http://gc.external.svc:8372"},
			absent:  []string{"Deployment/gonk-controller", "ServiceAccount/gc-controller"},
			present: []string{"StatefulSet/gonk-dolt", "Deployment/gonk-intake"},
		},
		{
			// RECONCILED, Task 0: was "ledger.backend=postgres" + "ledger.postgres.mode=cnpg" --
			// backend has no other value any more, so only the mode changes.
			name:    "ledger-cnpg",
			sets:    []string{"ledger.postgres.mode=cnpg"},
			present: []string{"StatefulSet/gonk-dolt", "Cluster/gonk-ledger-postgres"},
		},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			args := Minimum()
			for _, s := range tc.sets {
				args = append(args, "--set", s)
			}
			out := Render(t, args...)
			index := map[string]bool{}
			for _, o := range Objects(t, out) {
				index[o.Kind+"/"+o.Metadata.Name] = true
			}
			for _, p := range tc.present {
				if !index[p] {
					t.Errorf("%s: expected %s to render", tc.name, p)
				}
			}
			for _, a := range tc.absent {
				if index[a] {
					t.Errorf("%s: %s rendered and must not have", tc.name, a)
				}
			}
		})
	}
}

// Each fail-closed combination refuses to render, with a message that names the
// missing external replacement. A toggle that turns a component off WITHOUT its
// replacement must never render a silently-broken factory.
func TestToggleMatrixFailsClosed(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct {
		name string
		sets []string
		want string
	}{
		{"dolt-off-no-external", []string{"dolt.enabled=false"}, "dolt.external.host"},
		{"gascity-off-no-url", []string{"gascity.enabled=false"}, "gascity.supervisorURL"},
		{"meter-off-intake-on", []string{"meter.enabled=false"}, "dispatch unmetered work"},
		// RECONCILED, Task 0 -- NEW: there is no dolt-backed ledger any more.
		{"ledger-backend-dolt", []string{"ledger.backend=dolt"}, "ledger.backend"},
	}
	for _, tc := range cases {
		t.Run(tc.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			args := Minimum()
			for _, s := range tc.sets {
				args = append(args, "--set", s)
			}
			out, err := RenderErr(t, args...)
			if err == nil {
				t.Fatalf("%s rendered; it must fail closed", tc.name)
			}
			if !strings.Contains(out, tc.want) {
				t.Fatalf("%s failed with the wrong message.\nwant: %q\ngot:\n%s", tc.name, tc.want, out)
			}
		})
	}
}
```

### Task 9: Profiles, golden manifests, CI, the operator README, and ADR-006

**Files:** Create `chart/gonk/ci/values-{default,byo-everything,cnpg,full-monitoring}.yaml`,
`internal/charttest/golden_test.go`, `internal/charttest/testdata/golden/*.yaml`,
`chart/gonk/README.md`, `docs/adr/ADR-006-chart-seams-and-fail-closed-guards.md`;
modify `.gitlab-ci.yml`, `PLAN.md`; delete `chart/RECONCILIATION.md`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the value profiles**

`ci/values-default.yaml` — the minimum: **the whole bundled factory** (all four
component image tags: intake, meter, `gascity`, `dolt`), the two BYO URLs (GitLab,
LiteLLM), the operator ladder/catalog. Everything else default (`ledger.backend:
postgres`, `ledger.postgres.mode: shared` — RECONCILED, Task 0: there is no other
value for `ledger.backend`). **This is the profile that must be safe.** It
renders the controller, Dolt (beads), intake, meter, and the operator config
together, with meter pointed at the existing CNPG cluster's ledger tenancy.

`ci/values-byo-everything.yaml` — **every component disabled or pointed elsewhere**:
`dolt.enabled: false` + `dolt.external.host` (BYO **beads** Dolt), `gascity.enabled:
false` + `gascity.supervisorURL` (BYO controller), external GitLab with a private
CA, external LiteLLM, `ledger.postgres.mode: external` (RECONCILED, Task 0: BYO
**ledger** is a Postgres DSN, never an external Dolt — the ledger has no Dolt
position at all), `ingress.enabled: false`, monitoring off, a custom
`secrets.*.existingSecret` name for each credential, `gitlab.mode:
split-credential`. **If this profile renders, an operator can bring literally
all of their own infrastructure.**

`ci/values-cnpg.yaml` — `dolt.enabled: true` (beads) + `ledger.postgres.mode:
cnpg` (a chart-managed `Cluster` CR, instead of the default `shared`-tenancy
mode), ingress on, monitoring on. **RECONCILED, Task 0: this is no longer "the
Task 0b fallback" — Postgres is the ONLY ledger backend, so this profile is
just the "stand up your own CNPG Cluster" mode, not a contingency — and proof
that beads stays on Dolt regardless of which Postgres mode the ledger uses.**

`ci/values-full-monitoring.yaml` — everything on: all components bundled, ingress,
ServiceMonitors, PrometheusRule, dashboards, NetworkPolicy, a cloud rung in the
catalog and the ladder, and a non-zero instance cost ceiling. **The "this is a
working installation" profile**, and the only one where a dollar can be spent.

The four **toggle-matrix profiles** (`values-all-bundled`, `values-external-dolt`,
`values-byo-gascity`, `values-ledger-cnpg`) are written in **Task 8.5** and share
this directory; they are the render-or-fail-closed evidence for the component
toggles.

- [ ] **Step 1b: Write `chart/values-e2e.yaml` — Plan 06's hand-back HB-5**

**Why the chart owns this and the harness does not.** Plan 06 needs a deployment
to test. If the harness hand-assembles it, the harness is testing **a deployment
it invented**, not the one this chart ships — and the first time the two diverge,
e2e goes green on something nobody deploys. **The thing under test must come from
the chart.**

It is **`chart/values-e2e.yaml`** (a peer of `chart/gonk/`, not inside `ci/` —
`ci/` profiles are render-only goldens; this one is *installed*).

```yaml
# Plan 06's L3 profile. INSTALLED, not merely rendered.
#
# It differs from ci/values-default.yaml in exactly the ways a test must:
#   * images are the per-run e2e tags (the harness overrides image.tag);
#   * meter is the TESTCLOCK build, so a month boundary can be crossed without
#     waiting a month (P03 Task 8 Step 5b / Plan 06 HB-3);
#   * LiteLLM points at an instance the HARNESS deploys in the throwaway
#     namespace, whose ONLY upstream is the stub model;
#   * the rung catalog's synthetic prices are ROUND NUMBERS, so a dollar
#     assertion is exact.
#
# *** IT MUST NOT POINT AT THE REAL litellm.litellm.svc. *** The real LiteLLM
# routes local models to Ollama (192.168.1.142) and shares the owner's real spend
# ledger. An e2e run against it would burn real GPU, pollute real spend data, and
# make every cost assertion nondeterministic.
#
# NOTE ON litellm.mode: this chart is EXTERNAL-ONLY (OD-3). There is no
# `litellm.enabled: true`. The harness deploys its own LiteLLM into the run's
# namespace and passes the URL. Plan 06's HB-5 originally asked for a bundled one;
# that would have meant adding a subchart to satisfy a test, which is backwards.
image:
  registry: registry.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project
  pullSecrets:
    - name: gitlab-registry-pull-creds

intake:
  image: {tag: ""}                 # harness sets it: gonk-intake:e2e-<runid>
  replicaCount: 1                  # determinism > availability in a test
  reconcileInterval: 10m           # NOT lowered: the harness forces passes (HB-1)
  webhookPublicURL: ""             # harness sets it

meter:
  image: {tag: ""}                 # harness sets it: gonk-meter:e2e-<runid>-testclock
  replicaCount: 1

gitlab:
  url: ""                          # harness sets it (the cached gitlab-ce fixture)
  botUsername: gonk
  caCert:
    existingConfigMap: trust-bundle
    key: tls-ca-bundle.pem

litellm:
  mode: external
  externalURL: ""                  # harness sets it -> its OWN LiteLLM, stub upstream only

gascity:
  # BUNDLED controller: the e2e run tests the factory the chart ships, controller
  # included. The harness sets gascity.image.tag (e2e-<runid>) and, once Task 0.5
  # has settled them, supervisorPort/delivery/workload. supervisorURL is DERIVED.
  enabled: true
  image: {tag: ""}                 # harness sets it
  dispatch: http

dolt:
  # BUNDLED Dolt: backs BEADS ONLY (RECONCILED, Task 0 -- this comment
  # previously described a GONK_LEDGER=dolt harness mode that never shipped:
  # Task 0b's spike ran before this file was ever written for real, and Dolt
  # failed the reservation-race isolation test -- ADR-004 Decision 10). The
  # harness sets the image tag; the ledger below is unconditionally Postgres.
  enabled: true
  image: {tag: ""}                 # harness sets it

ledger:
  backend: postgres                # the ONLY value (ADR-004 Decision 10)
  postgres:
    mode: ""                       # harness sets it (shared|cnpg|external) to
                                    # exercise whichever Postgres tenancy Plan 06's
                                    # e2e run targets

ingress:
  enabled: false                   # the harness port-forwards; no DNS, no cert

# NetworkPolicies are NOT ENFORCED on this cluster (Flannel; Cilium suspended).
# They are still RENDERED here, because Plan 06's egress-denial test must be able
# to find the policy it is skipping -- and because the day Cilium lands, this
# profile is what proves it.
networkPolicy:
  enabled: true

operatorConfig:
  instance:
    ladder: [qwen-local, glm-test]
    budget:
      monthly_cost_usd: 1.00       # a ceiling the budget suite can actually reach
      monthly_tokens: "10M"
      per_task_tokens: "1M"
  rungs:
    # ROUND NUMBERS. Every dollar assertion in Plan 06 is exact, and
    # ledger.ApproxUSD's 1e-9 tolerance exists for float representation, not for
    # sloppy prices.
    - {name: qwen-local, kind: local, model: stub-local, est_cost_usd: 0,
       est_tokens: "100K", synthetic_usd_per_1m_tokens: 0.25}
    - {name: glm-test,   kind: cloud, model: stub-cloud, est_cost_usd: 0.20,
       est_tokens: "100K"}
  meter:
    max_spend_staleness: 5m
    reservation_ttl: 5m            # the expiry test must not take an hour
    max_clock_skew: 5m
    max_infra_retries: 2
    enforce_ladder_order: true

onboarding:
  defaultRung: qwen-local          # MUST be in instance.ladder, or the brick test fires
```

**The one thing this file must never contain is a credential.** `make secrets-scan`
(Plan 06 Task 9) greps for token shapes and fails the build; the harness mints
every secret at runtime into a directory outside the repo.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the golden test** — `internal/charttest/golden_test.go`

```go
//go:build chart

package charttest

import (
	"flag"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"
)

var update = flag.Bool("update", false, "rewrite the golden manifests")

// Golden manifests: the whole rendered output of each profile, byte for byte.
// This is the drift gate. A change to a helper that quietly moves a volumeMount,
// or a values default that quietly opens a port, shows up here as a diff a human
// has to look at and approve. Regenerate deliberately:
//
//	go test -tags chart ./internal/charttest/ -run Golden -update
func TestGoldenManifests(t *testing.T) {
	for _, profile := range []string{"values-default", "values-byo-everything", "values-cnpg", "values-full-monitoring", "values-all-bundled", "values-external-dolt", "values-byo-gascity", "values-ledger-cnpg"} {
		t.Run(profile, func(t *testing.T) {
			got := Render(t, "--values", filepath.Join(ChartDir(), "ci", profile+".yaml"))
			golden := filepath.Join("testdata", "golden", profile+".yaml")
			if *update {
				if err := os.WriteFile(golden, []byte(got), 0o644); err != nil {
					t.Fatal(err)
				}
				return
			}
			want, err := os.ReadFile(golden)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("no golden manifest (%v). Run with -update, then READ THE DIFF.", err)
			}
			if got != string(want) {
				t.Errorf("%s drifted from its golden manifest.\n"+
					"Run `go test -tags chart ./internal/charttest/ -run Golden -update` and READ the diff:\n"+
					"a moved volumeMount or an opened port looks exactly like a formatting change.", profile)
			}
		})
	}
}

// Every profile must lint and validate, not merely render.
func TestEveryProfileLintsAndValidates(t *testing.T) {
	crds := filepath.Join(ChartDir(), "tests", "crd-schemas")
	for _, profile := range []string{"values-default", "values-byo-everything", "values-cnpg", "values-full-monitoring", "values-all-bundled", "values-external-dolt", "values-byo-gascity", "values-ledger-cnpg"} {
		t.Run(profile, func(t *testing.T) {
			vals := []string{"--values", filepath.Join(ChartDir(), "ci", profile+".yaml")}
			Lint(t, vals...)
			Kubeconform(t, Render(t, vals...), crds)
		})
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Generate and READ the goldens**

```bash
go test -tags chart ./internal/charttest/ -run Golden -update
git diff --stat internal/charttest/testdata/golden
```

**Read `values-default.yaml`'s golden end to end before you commit it.** It is the
only time anyone will look at the whole thing at once. Check, specifically: no
`Secret`; no `secretKeyRef`; the Ingress is absent; the public Service has one
port; `gonk-meter` is `replicas: 1` + `Recreate`; the operator ConfigMap's ladder
is non-empty; the instance cost ceiling is `0`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the CI jobs** — modify `.gitlab-ci.yml`

```yaml
stages: [lint, test, chart]

# ... existing lint / test jobs unchanged ...

chart-lint:
  stage: chart
  # Only needs helm. Pin the tag: helm's schema validation has changed behaviour
  # across minors before.
  image: alpine/helm:3.19.0
  script:
    - helm lint --strict chart/gonk --values chart/gonk/ci/values-default.yaml
    - helm lint --strict chart/gonk --values chart/gonk/ci/values-byo-everything.yaml
    - helm lint --strict chart/gonk --values chart/gonk/ci/values-cnpg.yaml
    - helm lint --strict chart/gonk --values chart/gonk/ci/values-full-monitoring.yaml

chart-test:
  stage: chart
  # OWNER DECISION (OD-7): this image needs go + helm + kubeconform. Until it
  # exists, this job does not run and the chart gate is LOCAL -- which is where
  # plan 01's gate already lives (PLAN.md: no runner has ever executed a job).
  image: $GONK_CHART_TOOLS_IMAGE
  rules:
    - if: $GONK_CHART_TOOLS_IMAGE
  script:
    - go test -tags chart ./internal/charttest/... -count=1

chart-publish:
  stage: chart
  image: alpine/helm:3.19.0
  # OWNER DECISION (OD-8): which OCI registry, and with what credentials. Spec 7.1
  # says CI publishes the chart as OCI; nothing says where.
  rules:
    - when: never
  script:
    - helm package chart/gonk
    - helm push gonk-*.tgz oci://$CHART_REGISTRY
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `chart/gonk/README.md`** — the operator-facing document

**Section 0 comes first, before the install instructions, and it is not a
footnote:**

0. **⚠ WHAT IS AND IS NOT ENFORCED.**

   > gonk ships an egress `NetworkPolicy` that would confine agent pods to GitLab
   > and LiteLLM. **On the orac cluster it is not enforced.** Flannel does not
   > implement NetworkPolicy, and the Cilium HelmRelease that would is suspended.
   > An agent pod can therefore reach **Ollama at `http://192.168.1.142:11434`,
   > which requires no credential**, bypass LiteLLM entirely, and burn local GPU
   > with **no metering and no ceiling**.
   >
   > **What this means for your budgets, precisely:**
   >
   > - **Cloud-rung budgets are HARD.** A cloud call needs an API key. The only
   >   key an agent pod ever holds is the per-project LiteLLM virtual key, and
   >   LiteLLM refuses at the ceiling. This holds today.
   > - **Local-model budgets are ADVISORY.** They are enforced *if* the traffic
   >   goes through LiteLLM, and nothing currently forces it to.
   >
   > The policy is shipped anyway — it is correct, and it starts enforcing the day
   > Cilium is unsuspended. Plan 06 carries a written, **skipped** egress-denial
   > test; **un-skipping it is the gate.** Closing this gap is infra work outside
   > gonk: unsuspend Cilium, or put a credential in front of Ollama.
   >
   > Do not repeat spec §9's "budgets cannot be bypassed" in a dashboard, a demo,
   > or a status update. It is not true yet.

Then, and nothing else matters as much:

1. **The secret provisioning contract** — the table from the top of this plan,
   verbatim: which Secret, which key, which **Vault path** (`eso/gonk/<concern>`,
   ClusterSecretStore `vault-backend`), which mount path, which env var, which
   component. Plus an **example `ExternalSecret`** for each, and two sentences in
   bold:
   - *The chart creates no Secrets. If these do not exist, the pods will not start.*
   - ***Write the Vault value BEFORE merging the ExternalSecret.*** A missing key
     leaves it NotReady and can **wedge the entire Flux reconcile** — not just
     gonk's. This has already happened once on this cluster.
   Note the **deliberate deviation from house style**: the gitops convention
   consumes secrets as **env** (`secretKeyRef`); gonk consumes them as **file
   mounts** (owner decision 2026-07-13), because env leaks into
   `/proc/<pid>/environ`, crash dumps, and every child process. ESO *delivery* is
   identical; only *consumption* differs. Say so, or the next person to read
   `verify-conventions.sh` will think it is a mistake.
2. **The rotation procedure**, and it is now **uniform across all three
   credentials — no exception, no outage**: write new → slot 1, old → slot 2; set
   `secrets.rotation.<x>Previous: true`; `helm upgrade`; for the webhook token
   also **bump `intake.webhookTokenGen`** so reconcile repairs every GitLab hook;
   wait one reconcile interval; drop slot 2; set the flag back to `false`. The
   ExternalSecret carries `homelab.orac.local/rotation: enabled`, exactly as
   `apps/renovate/`'s PAT does.
   Explain the **two meanings of "slot 2"**, because they are not the same
   mechanism: a credential gonk **verifies** (the webhook token, the meter bearer)
   → *both slots are accepted*; a credential gonk **presents** (the GitLab PAT) →
   *slot 1 is presented, and a `401` falls back to slot 2 once*, incrementing
   `gonk_intake_gitlab_auth_fallback_total`. **Alert on that counter being > 0:
   it means a rotation is half-finished.**
3. **How gonk is actually deployed: Flux, from the gitops repo.** `helm install` is
   for a laptop. The real path, file by file — because the gitops repo's
   pre-commit hook will reject anything else:

   | File in `gitops:clusters/orac/` | What |
   |---|---|
   | `sources/ocirepository-gonk.yaml` | points at `oci://registry.orac.local/agentic/gonk-project/charts` — **charts are NEVER vendored; `charts/` at the repo root is gitignored** |
   | `apps/gonk/namespace-gonk.yaml` | ns `gonk`, with the `homelab.orac.local/service-tier` + `data-tier` labels |
   | `apps/gonk/helmrelease-gonk.yaml` | the HelmRelease, `targetNamespace: gonk` |
   | `apps/gonk/externalsecret-gonk-gitlab.yaml` | …and one file per Secret. **One K8s object per file, named `<kind>-<name>.yaml`.** |
   | `apps/gonk/externalsecret-gitlab-registry-pull-creds.yaml` | image pulls |
   | `apps/gonk/externalsecret-gonk-ledger.yaml` | the DSN — **the second one; ESO cannot replicate across namespaces** |
   | `apps/gonk/kustomization.yaml` | **must list every yaml above, or the commit fails** |
   | `apps/kustomization.yaml` | register `gonk/` |
   | `foundation/coredns/configmap-coredns.yaml` | **add `gonk.orac.local` to the hosts block, or in-cluster resolution fails silently** |

   **`apps/renovate/` is gonk's near-twin** (GitLab bot PAT + webhook + CA trust)
   and **`apps/nagus/`** is the model for a first-party image + shared CNPG.
   **Copy them. Do not invent a fifth way.**
4. **The CNPG four objects** (Task 6 Step 5b), spelled out, with the warning that
   `bootstrap.initdb` does **not** create a database on an **existing** cluster —
   you need a `Database` CR — and that the DSN must point at **`-rw`**.
5. **The component-toggle + BYO seams table**, with a worked example per knob:
   the umbrella deploys Gas City + Dolt + intake + meter + the pack (each
   `<component>.enabled`), and wires to BYO GitLab / LiteLLM / CNPG operator.
   Spell out the fail-closed rule: **turning a component off makes its external
   replacement required** — `dolt.enabled: false` ⇒ `dolt.external.host`;
   `gascity.enabled: false` ⇒ `gascity.supervisorURL`. State plainly that **beads
   is Dolt-only** and **CNPG is the ledger only**. Reproduce the three smoke-gated
   controller facts (port, delivery, workload) and point at
   `smoke/gc-controller-smoke.md` for the settled values.
6. **How to enable a cloud rung** — the deliberate three-part edit (catalog +
   instance ladder + instance cost ceiling), and *why* it is three parts.
7. **The single-replica warning for meter**, with the failure it prevents.
8. **What the chart cannot verify** — the "hand to Plan 06" list from the top of
   this plan, so an operator installing it knows what has and has not been proven.
   **Lead that list with the NetworkPolicy.**

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `docs/adr/ADR-006-chart-seams-and-fail-closed-guards.md`**

Record, with reasons:

- **The chart is an UMBRELLA (owner decision 2026-07-13).** It deploys Gas City +
  Dolt + intake + meter + the pack, each `<component>.enabled`; it wires to BYO
  GitLab, LiteLLM, and the CNPG operator. This reverses the earlier "Gas City and
  Dolt are BYO" framing (OD-4). Record why: the cluster has none of them, so a
  pack-plus-glue chart that assumed them would deploy nothing that runs.
- **Only LiteLLM is BYO among the model/runtime layer** (OD-3); what that costs.
- **The Gas City controller is templated, not charted** — there is no upstream
  chart, and three of its deploy facts (port, delivery, workload) are settled by a
  smoke deploy (Task 0.5), not by design. Record the Plan 04 image dependency.
- **Beads is Dolt-only; CNPG is ledger-only.** There is no beads-on-Postgres path,
  and the `dolt.enabled: false` toggle means external Dolt, not "Postgres for beads".
- **The ledger switch exists because Task 0b has not run.** Cite the spike doc.
- **Secrets are file mounts from externally-provisioned Secrets, two slots**, and
  that the chart renders no `Secret` and uses no `secretKeyRef` **anywhere** — with
  the test that enforces it.
- **Meter is single-replica**, and exactly what breaks otherwise.
- **The default install cannot spend a dollar** (AD-4), and why that is the right
  default for a system whose failure mode is money.
- **The chart's guards are three layers** (schema / `fail` / startup) and the full
  G1–G15 table.
- **`helm unittest` was replaced by Go tests** and why.
- **What the chart cannot prove**, explicitly, so that nobody reads a green CI as
  a working deployment.
- **THE NETWORKPOLICY GAP, as an accepted and UN-CLOSED limitation — not as a
  mitigated risk.** NetworkPolicies are not enforced on this cluster (Flannel;
  Cilium suspended); LiteLLM routes local models to an unauthenticated Ollama; an
  agent pod can bypass metering entirely. **Cloud-rung budgets are hard;
  local-model budgets are advisory.** The chart ships the policy anyway
  (documentation-as-code), Plan 06 skip-tests it, and **un-skipping that test is
  the gate**. Owner decision 2026-07-13: ship, document, do not gate. Record the
  decision *and* the fact that it is still open.
- **The agent-pod NetworkPolicy selector (OD-5) is an unverified assumption**, and
  it will still be an unverified assumption on the day Cilium lands — a *wrong*
  selector renders fine and enforces nothing. The test, not the template, is the
  proof.
- **The chart is consumed by Flux as an OCI artifact; charts are never vendored**
  into the gitops repo, and the deployment lives in `clusters/orac/apps/gonk/`.
- **Secrets are file mounts — a deliberate deviation from the gitops repo's `env`
  house style**, for a stated reason (env leaks into `/proc`, crash dumps, and
  child processes). Record it so it is not "fixed" later.

- Set plan 05 Status to `done`.
- Add to "Contracts published by plan 05":
  - `chart/gonk` — the **umbrella** deployment contract: it deploys Gas City +
    Dolt + intake + meter + the pack, each `<component>.enabled`, wiring to BYO
    GitLab/LiteLLM/CNPG. `values.schema.json` is the layer-A gate.
  - **The component-toggle contract:** disabling a component requires its external
    replacement (G16 external Dolt host, G17 BYO supervisor URL, G19 external-Dolt
    ledger DSN). Beads is Dolt-only; CNPG is ledger-only.
  - **The Gas City controller deploy contract** (no upstream chart): supervisor

    which **depends on Plan 04's `gonk-controller` image**.
  - **The secret provisioning contract** (the table). Changing a key name in
    Plans 02/03 breaks the chart, and `internal/charttest/secrets_test.go` is what
    catches it.
  - Two **namespaced RBAC** sets: `gonk-meter`'s (makes Plan 03's
    `internal/meter/keysink.K8s` legal — **the Go sink itself is Plan 03's**), and
    `gc-controller` + `gc-agent`'s (let the bundled controller spawn/exec/log agent
    pods). All namespaced, never `Cluster*`.
  - **`chart/values-e2e.yaml`** — Plan 06's HB-5. The harness installs *this
    chart*, not a deployment it invented.
- Add to "Carried into later plans":
  - **Plan 03 — RECONCILED, not outstanding.** `GONK_METER_STORE_BACKEND` /
    `GONK_METER_STORE_DSN_FILE` (P03 Task 8 Step 5, `openStore`) and
    `keysink.K8s` + `GONK_KEYSINK_NAMESPACE` (P03 Task 7 Step 4b / Task 8 Step 5)
    are now **specified in Plan 03**. Task 0 Step 2 of this plan **verifies** them;
    it no longer builds them. If they are missing at execution time, that is a
    Plan 03 bug — fix it there, or the repo grows two owners for one file.
  - **Plan 04 (images + pack) — now a HARD, PARTIALLY-PRECEDING dependency.** The
    umbrella deploys the **`gonk-controller` image** (`gascity.image`) and installs
    the **pack** into it; **Task 0.5's smoke deploy cannot run without that image**,
    so Plan 04 must partially precede Plan 05 hardening. The chart also needs the
    **labels the controller's session provider puts on agent pods**
    (`networkPolicy.agentPodSelector`, OD-5 = `app: gc-agent`, read from source but
    unverified live). Without them, spec §9's egress guarantee does not exist **even
    after Cilium lands** — a wrong selector renders fine and enforces nothing.
  - **Plan 06 (e2e):** the entire "what `helm template` cannot prove" list —
    especially **(a)** the agent egress is actually blocked **(WRITTEN AND SKIPPED
    until Cilium lands — the skip message names Cilium; un-skipping it is the
    gate)**, **(b)** the Secrets actually mount with those key names, **(c)** the
    probes actually pass, **(d)** LiteLLM's prices actually match the rung
    catalog's synthetic prices *and actually land in a spend row for a locally
    routed model* (the `model_cost_map`-vs-`model_info` ambiguity, P03), and
    **(e)** the KeySink's `Role` actually permits the writes on a real API server
    (a fake clientset does not enforce RBAC). Plus: the ledger has **no PDB and no
    backup schedule** (spec §4.2 wants both), and **nothing creates the ledger
    schema** — confirm meter migrates on startup.
  - **Owner — the three still open:** OD-3 (bundle LiteLLM? assumed no), **OD-5
    (agent pod labels — the most dangerous unknown in this chart)**, OD-7 (CI tools
    image — moot until a runner exists).
    **Answered by `docs/environment.md` / owner decision 2026-07-13, and struck:
    OD-1 (registry), OD-2 (ingress/TLS), OD-4 (the chart DEPLOYS Gas City — umbrella
    scope), OD-6 (GitLab is in-cluster), OD-8 (chart OCI registry).**
  - **Owner — the three smoke-gated unknowns (Task 0.5):** supervisor **port**,
    pack/city **delivery + sentinel handshake**, **workload type**. These block the
    controller wiring and need Plan 04's image — **Plan 04 must partially precede
    Plan 05 hardening.** Provisional defaults are `supervisorPort: 8372`,
    `delivery: prebaked`, `workload: deployment`, flagged UNVERIFIED until settled.
  - **Owner — the standing gap, which is not an OD because it is already decided:**
    NetworkPolicy is unenforced (Cilium suspended). Ship, document, do not gate.
    Re-open only by unsuspending Cilium or by putting a credential in front of

---

## Definition of done

- [ ] `helm lint --strict` passes for **all eight** profiles (the four originals
      plus the four toggle-matrix profiles).
- [ ] `go test -tags chart ./internal/charttest/... -count=1` passes, and it
      includes: every guard **G1–G20** asserted to **fail the render** (including
      **G16** external-Dolt-required (beads), **G17** BYO-controller-URL-required,
      **G19** ledger-DSN-required-for-any-Postgres-mode (RECONCILED, Task 0: no
      longer "external-Dolt-ledger-DSN" — there is no dolt-backed ledger), and the
      `gascity`/`dolt` image-tag guards); the
      **component-toggle matrix** (all-bundled, external Dolt, BYO Gas City,
      ledger-on-CNPG each render; each partial toggle fails closed); the secret
      contract asserted key-by-key; **no `Secret` object and no `secretKeyRef`
      anywhere**; the controller RBAC asserted **namespaced, never `Cluster*`**;
      the Ingress proven unable to reach the private listener; the golden manifests;
      `kubeconform -strict` over every profile with **no `-ignore-missing-schemas`**.
- [ ] `go test ./... -race -count=1` passes (the `keysink.K8s` tests are in the
      default gate — they need no cluster, only the fake clientset).
- [ ] **The default render is the WHOLE FACTORY** — controller + Dolt + intake +
      meter + operator config — and **every bundled component is toggleable**:
      `gascity.enabled`, `dolt.enabled`, `intake.enabled`, `meter.enabled` each
      render or fail closed with an actionable message.
- [ ] **Task 0.5's smoke deploy has run** and `chart/gonk/smoke/gc-controller-smoke.md`
      records the settled **supervisor port**, **pack/city delivery + sentinel
      handshake**, and **workload type** — OR the plan records that it is blocked on
      Plan 04's controller image, with the three controller defaults flagged
      **UNVERIFIED** and the controller golden manifests marked non-authoritative.
- [ ] The **default values ship a non-empty instance ladder**, and the default
      install **cannot spend a dollar**.
- [ ] `gonk-meter` **defaults** to `replicas: 1` + `strategy: Recreate` (a
      resource-footprint choice, not a correctness guard — AD-10 is lifted,
      ADR-004), and `meter.replicaCount` above 1 renders cleanly with **no**
      fail-closed guard in the way (RECONCILED, Task 0: the old "refuses to
      render more replicas" requirement is void). The **controller renders
      single-instance** (no leader election — this one IS still a hard
      requirement, for an unrelated reason), whichever workload type Task 0.5
      chose.
- [ ] **RECONCILED, Task 0: the bundled Dolt backs BEADS ONLY** — the ledger
      is Postgres/CNPG unconditionally (`ledger.backend` has exactly one valid
      value, `postgres`; ADR-004 Decision 10), and Dolt stays deployed
      regardless of `ledger.postgres.mode` (beads is Dolt-only; CNPG is the
      ledger only, never the other way). Both beads paths (bundled/external
      Dolt) and all three Postgres modes (shared/cnpg/external) render, lint,
      and validate independently of each other.
- [ ] Every component toggle and BYO seam (GitLab, LiteLLM, CNPG operator; bundled
      Gas City, Dolt, intake, meter; Prometheus, Grafana, ingress, storage, secrets)
      is a documented key in `values.yaml` **and** is exercised by a profile.
- [ ] The three dashboards render as valid JSON, are sidecar-discoverable, and the
      **Cost dashboard cannot present a synthetic dollar as spend** (enforced by a
      test, not by a convention).
- [ ] The alerts on `gonk_intake_projects{state="invalid"} > 0` (Plan 02 AD-2) and
      `gonk_intake_gitlab_auth_fallback_total > 0` (Plan 02 AD-4b — a half-finished
      PAT rotation) both exist.
- [ ] `chart/gonk/README.md` documents the secret contract and a **uniform**
      rotation procedure — **both** GitLab PAT slots are mounted, so there is no
      longer a single-slot exception — plus the two *meanings* of slot 2 (verify
      → accept either; present → fall back once).
- [ ] **Nothing in this chart disables TLS verification**, and the private CA is
      trusted the supported way: `trust-bundle` ConfigMap → `/etc/ssl/orac/ca.crt`
      → `SSL_CERT_FILE`. A test greps the rendered output for `sslVerify: false`,
      `insecure`, and `InsecureSkipVerify` and fails on a hit.
- [ ] **The Ingress uses the cert-manager ANNOTATION and the chart renders no
      `Certificate` object** (the combination once reissued 863 certs). Asserted by
      a test.
- [ ] **`chart/values-e2e.yaml` exists** (Plan 06 HB-5), points at a **harness-owned**
      LiteLLM (never the real `litellm.litellm.svc`), and contains **no credential**.
- [ ] **The NetworkPolicy gap is documented, loudly, in four places** — every
      policy template, `values.yaml`, `chart/gonk/README.md` §0, and ADR-006 — and
      `TestEveryNetworkPolicyDeclaresItIsNotEnforced` fails the build if a template
      loses its banner. **No document produced by this plan claims budgets cannot
      be bypassed.** The honest form is everywhere: **cloud-rung budgets are hard;
      local-model budgets are advisory** until Cilium lands.
- [ ] **The Postgres path supports `mode: shared`** — tenancy on the existing
      `databases-app/postgres` cluster — and the README names **all four** objects
      it needs, including the **second** ExternalSecret in gonk's namespace (ESO
      cannot replicate across namespaces) and the `-rw` Service.
- [ ] `ADR-006` is written (and records the **umbrella scope**: the chart deploys
      Gas City + Dolt + intake + meter + the pack, each toggleable; beads is
      Dolt-only; CNPG is ledger-only; the three smoke-gated controller unknowns);
      `PLAN.md` records the contracts, the carry-forwards, the **three** owner
      decisions still open, the **five** answered (OD-1/2/4/6/8), and the **three
      smoke-gated unknowns** with the Plan 04 dependency.
- [ ] **Task 0's reconciliation actually happened**, and any drift between this
      plan and the shipped Plans 02/03 is recorded rather than papered over. In
      particular: `GONK_METER_STORE_BACKEND`, `GONK_METER_STORE_DSN_FILE`,
      `keysink.NewK8s`, `GONK_KEYSINK_NAMESPACE`, `GONK_GITLAB_TOKEN_PREVIOUS_FILE`,
      `POST /admin/reconcile?wait=true`, and `POST /admin/spend/sync` all exist in
      the shipped code, because this chart wires every one of them.
